*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
Function Display_Saft_ERR
	*
	Select Erori_Saft
	*
	lcMsgErrorOrWarning = ''
	*
*!*		Scan
*!*			lcMsgErrorOrWarning = lcMsgErrorOrWarning + AllTrim(Sectiune)+' - '+AllTrim(Erori_User)+Chr(13)
*!*		EndScan
	*
	*Select DISTINCT Sectiune From Erori_Saft Into Cursor cSectiuni_Erori_Saft
	Select;
			a.NrCrt;
		,	a.Sectiune;
	From;
		(;
		Select;
				RecNo() As NrCrt;
			,	Sectiune;
		From Erori_Saft;
		) a;
	Group By a.Sectiune;
	Order By a.NrCrt;
	Into Cursor cSectiuni_Erori_Saft	
	*
	lcMsgErrorOrWarning = ''
	Scan
		lcMsgErrorOrWarning = lcMsgErrorOrWarning + AllTrim(AllTrim(Sectiune))+Chr(13)
		Select Erori_Saft
		*
		Scan For Sectiune = cSectiuni_Erori_Saft.Sectiune
			lcMsgErrorOrWarning = lcMsgErrorOrWarning + ' - '+AllTrim(Erori_User)+Chr(13)
		EndScan
		*
		lcMsgErrorOrWarning = lcMsgErrorOrWarning + Chr(13)
	EndScan
	*	
	If .Not. Empty(lcMsgErrorOrWarning)
		*
		lcMsgErrorOrWarning = "Erori SAF-T: "+Chr(13)+lcMsgErrorOrWarning
		*
		If Not Directory(ExpDir + 'TMP')
			Md ExpDir + 'TMP'
		EndIf	
		*
		lcCaleTemp = ExpDir + 'TMP\'
		lcNumeFisier = lcCaleTemp+"ERORI_D406_"+AllTrim(ICas.oSoc.CodFiscal)+"_"+StrTran(DToC(Data1), ".", "-")+"_"+DToC(Data2)+".txt"
		StrToFile(lcMsgErrorOrWarning, lcNumeFisier)
		*
		Declare Integer ShellExecute In Shell32.Dll Integer, String, String, String, String, Integer
		*ShellExecute(0, "open", "wordpad", lcNumeFisier, "", 1)
		ShellExecute(0, "open", "notepad", lcNumeFisier, "", 1)
		*
	EndIf
	*
EndFunc

Function _01Audit
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	01. Audit
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*\\<?xml version="1.0"?>
Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'01.AuditFile.xml' NoShow
\\<?xml version="1.0"?>
\<AuditFile xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance" xmlns="mfp:anaf:dgti:d406:declaratie:v1" xsi:schemaLocation="urn:StandardAuditFile-Taxation-Financial:RO Romanian_SAF-T_Financial_Schema_v_2.1.xsd">
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'01.AuditFile.xml'...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'01.AuditFile.xml'+[...])
EndFunc
*
Function _02Header()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	02. Header
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
Select Header_Structure
AuditFileVersion		=	'2.0'
AuditFileCountry		=	'RO'
AuditFileRegion			=	'RO-'+GetCodJudet(3, ICAS.oSoc.Judet)
AuditFileDateCreated	=	Date()			&& Transform(DToS(Date()), '@R ####-##-##')
SoftwareCompanyName		=	'EXPERT SOFTWARE COMPANY SRL'
SoftwareID				=	'ICAS'
SoftwareVersion			=	ICAS.cVERSIUNE
RegistrationNumber		=	CUI_Raportor
Name					=	ICAS.oSoc.Denumire
StreetName				=	GetOptionalXMLValue(ICAS.oSoc.Strada)
Number					=	GetOptionalXMLValue(ICAS.oSoc.NumarStrada)
City					=	ICAS.oSoc.Localitate
PostalCode				=	GetOptionalXMLValue(ICAS.oSoc.CodPostal)
Region					=	GetOptionalXMLValue('RO-'+GetCodJudet(3, ICAS.oSoc.Judet))
Country					=	'RO'
AddressType				=	GetOptionalXMLValue('StreetAddress')
Title					=	''

*
Function _04GeneralLedgerAccounts()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	04. GeneralLedgerAccounts
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
Select;
	c.Cont As AccountID;
	, c.Cont As StandardAccountID;
	, c.Denumire As AccountDescription;
	, Space(12) As AccountType;
	, Space(1) As GroupingCategory;
	, c.Sold_In_D As OpeningDebitBalance;
	, c.Sold_In_C As OpeningCreditBalance;
	, c.Fin_D As ClosingDebitBalance;
	, c.Fin_C As ClosingCreditBalance;
	, c.Sold_In_D As SiD;
	, c.Sold_In_C As SiC;
	, c.Rulaj_D As Rulaj_D;
	, c.Rulaj_C As Rulaj_C;
	, c.Fin_D As SD;
	, c.Fin_C As SC;
	, c.Tip;
	, c.Cont;
From Con_Temp c;
Where;
	Between(Val(Left(c.Cont, 1)), 1, 7);
Into Cursor crsGeneralLedgerAccounts ReadWrite
***
***Inner Join cRegistruJurnal r On c.Cont=r.Cont;
***
*
Update c Set c.GroupingCategory=t.TipImpozit From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts c Inner Join Terti t on t.Cont=c.Cont Where Nvl(t.TipImpozit, Space(1))#Space(1)

Delete From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts;
Where;
	Nvl(SiD, 0)=0;
	And Nvl(SiC, 0)=0;
	And Nvl(SD, 0)=0;
	And Nvl(SC, 0)=0;
	And Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
	And Cont Not In(Select Distinct Cont From cRegistruJurnal)
*
*		And Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
*
*
Update crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Set AccountDescription=Elimin_Dia(AccountDescription)
Update crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Set AccountID=Cont_SAFT(AccountID) Where Not AllTrim(Cont)=='411'
Update crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Set AccountType='Activ' Where Tip='A'
Update crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Set AccountType='Pasiv' Where Tip='P'
Update crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Set AccountType='Bifunctional' Where Tip='B'
*
Select * from crsGeneralLedgerAccounts into CURSOR ramase readwrite
*
Delete From ramase ;
Where;
	AreAnalitic(Cont, .F., 'ramase')=.F.;
	And Nvl(SiD, 0)=0;
	And Nvl(SiC, 0)=0;
	And Nvl(SD, 0)=0;
	And Nvl(SC, 0)=0;
	And Abs(Rulaj_D)+Abs(Rulaj_C)=0;
	And Cont Not In(Select Distinct Cont From cRegistruJurnal);
	And not Deleted()
******	AreAnalitic(Cont, .F., 'Con_Temp')=.F.;
*
Select crsGeneralLedgerAccounts 
Zap
Append From Dbf('ramase')
*
*!*	Delete From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Where	Not areRulaj(Cont)
*!*	Delete From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Where AreAnalitic(Cont, .F., 'Con_Temp')=.T.
******
Select a.AccountId, a.StandardAccountID, a.AccountDescription;
From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts a;
Inner Join (;
			Select AccountId, StandardAccountID, Count(AccountId) As Dubluri;
			From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts;
			Having Dubluri>1;
			Group By AccountID;
			) b  On b.AccountID = a.AccountID;
Order By a.AccountID ;
Into Cursor AcelasiCont
*
***
Tabela_Erori_Saft('DubluriConturiDupaMapareSaft')
*

Select MasterFiles_GeneralLedgerAccounts
Append From Dbf('crsGeneralLedgerAccounts')
*
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'GLA_din_facturare'
DO Saft_Ecosal_Preluare_Dbf     WITH 'GLA_din_facturare'  &&&

*** la ecosal o fac mai sus in  ----DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'GLA_din_facturare'
If INLIST( m.NumeDB, "ecuaqua", "SCECOSALSERVICIIOLTENITASRL") 
	Verific_Balanta_ICAS_Cu_GLA()
	Tabela_Erori_Saft('Balanta_Versus_GLA')
EndIf
*** la ecoaqua o fac mai sus in  ----DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'GLA_din_facturare'
If m.NumeDB <> "ecuaqua"  
	Verific_Balanta_ICAS_Cu_GLA()
	Tabela_Erori_Saft('Balanta_Versus_GLA')
EndIf	
*
Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'04.GeneralLedgerAccounts.xml' Additive NoShow
\		<GeneralLedgerAccounts>
	Select MasterFiles_GeneralLedgerAccounts
	Scan
\			<Account>
\				<AccountID><<AllTrim(AccountId)>></AccountID>
\				<AccountDescription><<AllTrim(AccountDescription)>></AccountDescription>
\				<StandardAccountID><<AllTrim(StandardAccountID)>></StandardAccountID>
\				<AccountType><<AllTrim(AccountType)>></AccountType>
		*IF OpeningDebitBalance<>0 Or (OpeningDebitBalance=0 And OpeningCreditBalance=0 And InList(AllTrim(AccountType), 'Activ', 'Bifunctional'))
		If;
			OpeningDebitBalance<>0;
			Or (OpeningCreditBalance=0 And OpeningDebitBalance=0 And InList(AllTrim(AccountType), 'Activ', 'Bifunctional'))
\				<OpeningDebitBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(OpeningDebitBalance))>></OpeningDebitBalance>
		Else
\				<OpeningCreditBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(OpeningCreditBalance))>></OpeningCreditBalance>
		EndIf
		If;
			ClosingDebitBalance<>0;
			Or (ClosingDebitBalance=0 And ClosingCreditBalance=0 And InList(AllTrim(AccountType), 'Activ', 'Bifunctional'))
\				<ClosingDebitBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(ClosingDebitBalance))>></ClosingDebitBalance>
		Else	
\				<ClosingCreditBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(ClosingCreditBalance))>></ClosingCreditBalance>
		EndIf
\			</Account>
EndScan
\		</GeneralLedgerAccounts>
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'04.GeneralLedgerAccounts.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'04.GeneralLedgerAccounts.xml'+[...])
If FORCE_REFRESH
	DoEvents FORCE
EndIf	
EndFunc
*
Function _05Customers()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	05. Customers
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
****	Inner Join cRegistruJurnal
Select;
	c.RegistrationNumber;
	, c.Denumire As Name;
	, c.Localitate As City;
	, c.Tara As Country;
	, c.RegistrationNumber As CustomerID;
	, c.Cont As AccountID;
	, c.Cont As StandardAccountID;
	, c.Cont As StandardID;
	, Sold_In_D As OpeningDebitBalance;
	, Sold_In_C As OpeningCreditBalance;
	, Fin_D As ClosingDebitBalance;
	, Fin_C As ClosingCreditBalance;
From Con_Temp b;
Inner Join crsClienti c On c.Cont=b.Cont;
Where;
	InList(Left(b.Cont, 2),'41','46');
	And;
	(;
		ABS(Evl(Nvl(Sold_In_D, 0), Nvl(Sold_In_C, 0)))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0))+ABS(Evl(Nvl(Fin_D, 0), Nvl(Fin_C, 0)))<>0;
		Or;
		c.Cont In(Select Distinct Cont From cRegistruJurnal);
	);
Order By c.Cont;
Into Cursor crsBalCustomers ReadWrite
*
Update crsBalCustomers Set Name=Elimin_Dia(Name), City=Elimin_Dia(City), AccountID=Cont_SAFT(AccountID)
*
*Update crsBalCustomers Set Country='RO' Where IsNull(Country)
* CRIDA:
*	18557714
*	411.1280		AQUA CONSTRUCT SRL
*
Select *;
From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts;
Where;
	Cont Not In ( Select StandardID From crsBalCustomers);
	And Left(Cont, 2)='41';
	And Left(Cont, 5)<>'4118.';
	And ABS(Evl(Nvl(SID, 0), Nvl(SIC, 0)))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0))+ABS(Evl(Nvl(SD, 0), Nvl(SC, 0)))<>0;
	And Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
	And AreAnalitic( Cont, .F., 'Con_Temp')=.F.;  &&& nic adaugat pe 02-04-2024
Into Cursor Lipsa_CIF_Customers 
*
Tabela_Erori_Saft('Customers')
*
Select * From crsBalCustomers Where IsNullOrEmpty(Country) Into Cursor Customers_Country_Null
If _Tally>0
	*
	Scan
	    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
	    Values ('Customers', 'Lipsa Tara: ' + AllTrim(Customers_Country_Null.StandardAccountID) +' '+ALLTRIM(Customers_Country_Null.Name) )
	EndScan
	*
	*Display_Saft_ERR()
	*Return .F.
	Update crsBalCustomers Set Country='' Where IsNull(Country)			&& ca sa pot trece mai departe
EndIf
*
Select MasterFiles_Customers
Append From Dbf('crsBalCustomers')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Customers'
DO Saft_Ecosal_Preluare_Dbf     WITH 'Customers'

Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'05.Customers.xml' Additive NoShow
\		<Customers>
	Select MasterFiles_Customers
	Scan
\			<Customer>
\				<CompanyStructure>
\					<RegistrationNumber><<AllTrim(RegistrationNumber)>></RegistrationNumber>
\					<Name><<AllTrim(Name)>></Name>
\					<Address>
\						<City><<AllTrim(City)>></City>
\						<Country><<AllTrim(Country)>></Country>
\					</Address>
*		lcIBAN = ICase( .Not. IsNullOrEmpty(ContB1) .And. VerifIBAN(AllTrim(ContB1)), AllTrim(ALLTRIM(ContB1)), 'RO62RNCB0099007787650033')	&& penru testare
*\					<BankAccount>
*\						<IBANNumber><<lcIBAN>></IBANNumber>
*\					</BankAccount>
\				</CompanyStructure>
\				<CustomerID><<AllTrim(CustomerID)>></CustomerID>
\				<AccountID><<AllTrim(AccountID)>></AccountID>
		If ! Empty(OpeningCreditBalance)
\				<OpeningCreditBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(OpeningCreditBalance))>></OpeningCreditBalance>
		Else
\				<OpeningDebitBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(OpeningDebitBalance))>></OpeningDebitBalance>
		EndIf
		If ! Empty(ClosingCreditBalance)
\				<ClosingCreditBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(ClosingCreditBalance))>></ClosingCreditBalance>
		Else
\				<ClosingDebitBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(ClosingDebitBalance))>></ClosingDebitBalance>
		EndIf
\			</Customer>
	EndScan
\		</Customers>
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'05.Customers.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'05.Customers.xml'+[...])
EndFunc
*
Function _06Suppliers()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	06. Suppliers
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
Select;
	c.RegistrationNumber;
	, c.Denumire As Name;
	, c.Localitate As City;
	, c.Tara As Country;
	, c.RegistrationNumber As SupplierID;
	, b.Cont As AccountID;
	, c.Cont As StandardAccountID;
	, c.Cont As StandardID;
	, Sold_In_D As OpeningDebitBalance;
	, Sold_In_C As OpeningCreditBalance;
	, Fin_D As ClosingDebitBalance;
	, Fin_C As ClosingCreditBalance;
From Con_Temp b;
Inner Join crsFurnizori c On c.Cont=b.Cont;
Where;
	InList(Left(b.Cont, 2), '40', '46');
	And;
	(;
		ABS(Evl(Nvl(Sold_In_D, 0), Nvl(Sold_In_C, 0)))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0))+ABS(Evl(Nvl(Fin_D, 0), Nvl(Fin_C, 0)))<>0;
		Or c.Cont In(Select Distinct Cont From cRegistruJurnal);
	);
Order By c.Cont;
Into Cursor crsBalSuppliers ReadWrite
*
Update crsBalSuppliers Set Name=Elimin_Dia(Name), City=Elimin_Dia(City), AccountID=Cont_SAFT(AccountID)
*
Select *;
From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts;
Where;
	Cont Not In ( Select StandardID From crsBalSuppliers);
	And Left(Cont, 2)='40';
	And ABS(Evl(Nvl(SID, 0), Nvl(SIC, 0)))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0))+ABS(Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0))+ABS(Evl(Nvl(SD, 0), Nvl(SC, 0)))<>0;
	And Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
	And AreAnalitic( Cont, .F., 'Con_Temp')=.F.;  &&& nic adaugat pe 02-04-2024
Into Cursor Lipsa_CIF_Suppliers 
*
Tabela_Erori_Saft('Suppliers')
*
Select * From crsBalSuppliers Where IsNullOrEmpty(Country) Into Cursor Suppliers_Country_Null
*
If _Tally>0
	*
	Scan
	    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
	    Values ('Suppliers', 'Lipsa Tara: ' + AllTrim(Suppliers_Country_Null.StandardAccountID) +' '+ALLTRIM(Suppliers_Country_Null.Name) )
	EndScan
	*
	*Display_Saft_ERR()
	*Return .F.
	Update crsBalSuppliers Set Country='' Where IsNull(Country)			&& ca sa pot trece mai departe
EndIf
*

Select MasterFiles_Suppliers
Append From DBF('crsBalSuppliers')
Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'06.Suppliers.xml' Additive NoShow
\		<Suppliers>
	Select MasterFiles_Suppliers
	Scan
\			<Supplier>
\				<CompanyStructure>
\					<RegistrationNumber><<AllTrim(RegistrationNumber)>></RegistrationNumber>
\					<Name><<AllTrim(Name)>></Name>
\					<Address>
\						<City><<AllTrim(City)>></City>
\						<Country><<AllTrim(Country)>></Country>
\					</Address>
*!*			lcIBAN = ICase( .Not. IsNullOrEmpty(ContB1) .And. VerifIBAN(AllTrim(ContB1)), AllTrim(ALLTRIM(ContB1)), 'RO62RNCB0099007787650033')	&& penru testare
*!*	\					<BankAccount>
*!*	\						<IBANNumber><<lcIBAN>></IBANNumber>
*!*	\					</BankAccount>
\				</CompanyStructure>
\				<SupplierID><<AllTrim(SupplierID)>></SupplierID>
\				<AccountID><<AllTrim(AccountID)>></AccountID>
		If ! Empty(OpeningDebitBalance)
\				<OpeningDebitBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(OpeningDebitBalance))>></OpeningDebitBalance>
		Else
\				<OpeningCreditBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(OpeningCreditBalance))>></OpeningCreditBalance>
		EndIf
		If ! Empty(ClosingDebitBalance)
\				<ClosingDebitBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(ClosingDebitBalance))>></ClosingDebitBalance>
		Else		
\				<ClosingCreditBalance><<AllTrim(Transform(ClosingCreditBalance))>></ClosingCreditBalance>
		EndIf
\			</Supplier>
	EndScan
\		</Suppliers>
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'06.Suppliers.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'06.Suppliers.xml'+[...])
EndFunc

*
Function _10MovementTypeTable()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	10. MovementTypeTable						Mandatory - by request
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'10.MovementTypeTable.xml' Additive NoShow
*
If lcTipDeclaratie = 'C'  And glPreluareDinEstoc = .T.
	*
	Select;
		MovementTy As MovementType,;
		Descriptio As Description  ;
	From Movement_Estoc;
	Into Cursor Movement_Estoc_Temp
	*
	Select MovementTypeTable
	Zap
	Append From Dbf('Movement_Estoc_Temp')
EndIf
*
\		<MovementTypeTable>	
*
Select MovementTypeTable
Scan
\			<MovementTypeTableEntry>	
\				<MovementType><<AllTrim(MovementType)>></MovementType>
\				<Description><<AllTrim(Description)>></Description>
\			</MovementTypeTableEntry>	
EndScan
*
\		</MovementTypeTable>
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
*
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'10.MovementTypeTable.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'10.MovementTypeTable.xml'+[...])
*
EndFunc
*
Function _11Products()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	11. Products
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
If lcTipDeclaratie = 'C'  And glPreluareDinEstoc = .T.
	*
	Select;
		ProductCod 	As ProductCode,;
		Descriptio	As Description,;
		ProductCom	As ProductCommodityCode,;
		UomBase,;
		UomStandar	As UomStandard,;
		UOMToUOMBa 	As UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor;
	From Products_Estoc;
	Into Cursor crsProducts
	*
Else
	*
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select
				[ProductCode]					= RTrim(LTrim(Str(a.Id)))
			,	[Description]					= a.Denumire
			,	[ProductCommodityCode]			= Case When a.Nc8 IS NOT NULL  And Len(a.Nc8)>0 Then a.Nc8 Else '0' End
			,	[UomBase]						= a.Um
			,	[UomStandard]					= a.Um
			,	[UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor]	= 1.00
		From Articole a
		Inner Join TipuriArticole t On a.IdTipArt=t.Id
		Inner Join ItemsTransactions i On i.IdArticol=a.Id		-- 28Februarie2025
		Where
			IsNull(t.Sters, 0)=0
			And t.Stocabil=1
			And t.Cont Not Like '303%'
			And t.Cont Not Like '32%'
			And t.Cont Not Like '33%'
			And t.Cont Not Like '8035%'
			And i.Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2				-- 28Februarie2025
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [crsProducts])
	*
	If RecCount([crsProducts])=0
		Insert Into [crsProducts] (ProductCode, Description, UomBase) VALUES ('COD_GENERIC', 'VANZARE', 'BUC')
	EndIf
	*
EndIf
*
Select MasterFiles_Products
Zap
Append From Dbf([crsProducts])
*
Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'11.Products.xml' Additive NoShow
\		<Products>
Select MasterFiles_Products
Scan
\			<Product>
\				<ProductCode><<AllTrim(ProductCode)>></ProductCode>
\				<Description><<AllTrim(Description)>></Description>
\				<ProductCommodityCode><<AllTrim(ProductCommodityCode)>></ProductCommodityCode>
*!*		If !IsNullOrEmpty(CodBare)
*!*	\				<ProductNumberCode><<AllTrim(CodBare)>></ProductNumberCode>
*!*		EndIf
\				<UOMBase><<Evl(GetUnitOfMeasure(UomBase), 'H87')>></UOMBase>
\				<UOMStandard><<Evl(GetUnitOfMeasure(UOMStandard), 'H87')>></UOMStandard>
\				<UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor><<AllTrim(Transform(UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor))>></UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor>
\			</Product>
EndScan
\		</Products>
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'11.Products.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'11.Products.xml'+[...])
EndFunc
*
Function _12PhysicalStock()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	12. Products
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
If lcTipDeclaratie = 'C'  And glPreluareDinEstoc = .T.
	Select 	0			As NId							,;
			WareHouseI 	As WareHouseId					,;
			ProductCod	As ProductCode					,;
			Cont		As ProductType					,;	&&& Pentru a determina dac? produs/contul de stocuri este materie prim?, produc?ie în curs de execu?ie, produs finit, marf? etc.
			OwnerId										,;	&&& 00+codfiscal
			GetUnitOfMeasure(UOMPhysica) As UOMPhysicalStock ,;	&&& Nu este necesar atunci când UOMPhysicalStock = UOMBase
			1.00		As UOMtoUOMBaseConversionFactor	,;	&&& Factorul de conversie a UOM la UOMBase
			''			As StockAccountCommodityCode	,;	&&& Clasificarea pentru import / export
			UnitPrice									,;
			OpeningSto	As OpeningStockQuantity			,;
			OpeningSt2	As OpeningStockValue			,;
			ClosingSto	As ClosingStockQuantity			,;
			ClosingSt2	As ClosingStockValue			,;
			'0'			As StockCharacteristic			,;	&&& Caracteristicile bunurilor de definit de c?tre utilizator. Tabel predefinit este posibil.
			'0'			As StockCharacteristicValue		,;	&&& SAFT_Nomenclator_StockChar - Pt. produsele accizabile (motorina) pentru care intervine obliga?ia de marcare ?i colorare conform Codului Fiscal ( blue_35/yellow_124/0 )
			RecNo() 	As NrCrt						 ;
		From PhysicalStock_estoc;							&&& Pentru orice alte cazuri nedefinite în lista de mai sus ( 0 - zero )
		Into Cursor PhysicalStock_Temp readwrite
	*
	*   Pentru a determina dac? produs/contul de stocuri este materie prim?, produc?ie în curs de execu?ie, produs finit, marf? etc.
	*   Icase( left(Cont,2)='30', "Materii prime"		,;
			   left(Cont,2)='32', "Stocuri"				,;
			   left(Cont,2)='33', "Productie in curs"	,;
			   left(Cont,2)='34', "Produse"				,;
			   left(Cont,2)='35', "Stocuri terti"		,;
			   left(Cont,2)='36', "Active biologice"	,;
			   left(Cont,2)='37', "Marfuri"				,;
			   left(Cont,2)='38', "Ambalaje"			,;
			   left(Cont,2)='39', "Ajustari depreciere"	,;
			   "xx"										 ;
			   ) As ProductType,					
	
	Select PhysicalStock
	Zap
	Append From Dbf('PhysicalStock_Temp')	
	*
	*** pot lasa direct contul 301,3021,3028 sau sa trec denumirea lui ProductType (C18)
	***Replace All ProductType With 'marfa' &&& Pentru a determina dac? produs/contul de stocuri este materie prim?, produc?ie în curs de execu?ie, produs finit, marf? etc.										   		
EndIf
*
Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'12.PhysicalStock.xml' Additive NoShow
\		<PhysicalStock>
Select PhysicalStock
Scan
\			<PhysicalStockEntry>
\				<WarehouseID><<AllTrim(WareHouseId)>></WarehouseID>
\				<ProductCode><<AllTrim(ProductCode)>></ProductCode>
\				<ProductType><<AllTrim(ProductType)>></ProductType>
\				<StockAccountCommodityCode>0</StockAccountCommodityCode>
\				<OwnerID><<AllTrim(OwnerID)>></OwnerID>
\				<UOMPhysicalStock><<AllTrim(UOMPhysicalStock)>></UOMPhysicalStock>
\				<UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor><<AllTrim(Str(UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor))>></UOMToUOMBaseConversionFactor>
\				<UnitPrice><<AllTrim(Str(UnitPrice,15,2))>></UnitPrice>
\				<OpeningStockQuantity><<AllTrim(Str(OpeningStockQuantity,15,2))>></OpeningStockQuantity>
\				<OpeningStockValue><<AllTrim(Str(OpeningStockValue,15,2))>></OpeningStockValue>
\				<ClosingStockQuantity><<AllTrim(Str(ClosingStockQuantity,15,2))>></ClosingStockQuantity>
\				<ClosingStockValue><<AllTrim(Str(ClosingStockValue,15,2))>></ClosingStockValue>
\				<StockCharacteristics>
\					<StockCharacteristic><<AllTrim(StockCharacteristic)>></StockCharacteristic>
\					<StockCharacteristicValue><<AllTrim(StockCharacteristicValue)>></StockCharacteristicValue>
\				</StockCharacteristics>
\			</PhysicalStockEntry>
EndScan
\		</PhysicalStock>
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'12.PhysicalStock.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'12.PhysicalStock.xml'+[...])
*
EndFunc

Function _14Assets()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	14.Assets				Mandatory - once per year
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
If lcTipDeclaratie#'A'	
	Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'14.Assets.xml' Additive NoShow
\		<Assets/>
	Set TextMerge To
	Set TextMerge Off
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'14.Assets.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'14.Assets.xml'+[...])
	Return
EndIf
*
mySQLExec('Select Id, Cont, Cont_AM, ValActual, ValRam, AmortPl From MijloaceFixe', 'MijloaceFixe')
*
=MijloaceFixe_ADV('MijloaceFixe', '')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets', 'MijloaceFixe'
*
*** aflu amortizaridetaliat ( trebuie sa stiu iesiri, deprecieri, reevaluari, etc...)
***   'Casare','Conservare','Modernizare','Reevaluare','Reevaluare Net','Vanzare', ....		
*Data1={01/01/2023}
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	 Select	ad.*
	 		, m.NrInv
	 		, m.CodCls
	 		, Cast ( 0 As Numeric(18,2))   As reevaluare_dif
	 		, Cast ( 0 As Numeric(18,2))   As Amort_reev_negativa
	 		, Cast ( la.Id As Numeric(18)) As IdLA 
	 		, Cast ( '' As Character(35))  As TransactionId
	 		, idet.Total as vanzare
	 		, m.ValActual As Val_MF
	From AmortizariDetaliat ad
		Inner Join	MijloaceFixe	m	On ad.Id_MijlocFix = m.Id 
		Left Join	LunaAnul   		la On la.nLuna = Month(ad.data) And la.nAnul= Year(ad.data) And la.TipDoc='A'
		Left Join	Iesiri			i  	On i.DataDoc = ad.Data And i.NumarDoc = ad.Nr
		Left Join	IesiriDetaliat idet	On idet.IdArticol = ad.Id_mijlocFix And i.IdDoc=idet.IdDoc
	Where  
			IsNull(m.Sters, 0)=0 
		And	IsNull(i.Anulat,0)=0
		And IsNull(ad.Sters,0)=0 
		And	Year(ad.Data)=Year(?Data1)
		And IsNull(la.Sters,0)=0
	Order By ad.Id_Mijlocfix, Data
EndText
*mySQLExec(lcSQL, [AmortizariDetaliatAn1])
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [AmortizariDetaliatAn])
Replace All Valoare With Vanzare For InList( Tip, 'Vanzare')
Replace All Id_Luna_An With IdLA

*return
*mySQLExec("select * from LunaAnul","hhh")
*mySQLExec("select * from amortizaridetaliat","hhh")


=MijloaceFixe_ADV('AmortizariDetaliatAn', '')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets', 'AmortizariDetaliatAn'


**********************************************************************************
*** mijloacele care exista doar in anii anteriori (sint amortizate complet si nu apar in amortizari in anul curent) 
*** o trec cu ultima pozitie care apare in Amortizari in server; nu 12 pozitii ca cele pt. anul curent  
*aa= Seconds()
*Data1={01/01/2023}
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Declare @Data1 Date = ?Data1;

	WITH AmortizariAnterioare  (ID, MaxLuna)
	AS
	(
		Select
			m.Id,
			MAX( dbo.MyFirstDay( l.nLuna, l.nAnul)) As MaxLuna
		From MijloaceFixe m
			Inner Join Amortizari a   On a.IdMF = m.Id
			Inner Join LunaAnul   l   On l.Id   = a.Id_Luna_An
		Where
				IsNull(m.Sters, 0)	= 0
			And IsNull(a.Sters, 0)	= 0
			And IsNull(l.Sters, 0)	= 0
			And l.nAnul				< Year(@Data1)
		Group By m.Id
	),
	LuniAmortizareAnterioare  (ID, nLuna, nAnul, MaxLuna)
	As
	(
		Select
			l.Id, 
			nLuna, 
			nAnul, 
			dbo.MyFirstDay( nLuna, nAnul) As MaxLuna    
		From LunaAnul				l
			Inner Join ( Select Id_Luna_An From Amortizari Where Sters=0  Group By Id_Luna_An) a On a.Id_Luna_An=l.Id
		Where
				IsNull(l.Sters, 0)	= 0
			And nAnul				< Year(@Data1)
	),
	IesiriAnterioare (Id_MF)
	As
	(
		Select
			[Id_MF] = Id_MijlocFix
		From AmortizariDetaliat
		Where
				Sters						= 0
			And Tip							In ('Casare','Vanzare')
			And IsNull(Data, '99990101')	< @Data1
	) 
	Select 
			[MaxLuna]										= t.MaxLuna
		,	a.*
		,	[Id_Din_MF]										= m.Id
		,	[An_Ant]										= Cast( 'DA' As Char(2))
		,	[IsPublic]										= m.IsPublic
		,	[nLuna]											= l.nLuna
	--	,	[nAnul]											= l.nAnul
		,	[Data_Ies]										= m.Data_Ies
		,	[Data_V]										= m.Data_V
		,	[Durata_In_An]									= Cast( 0 				As Numeric(15))
		,	[Data_punere_functiune]							= Cast( a.Data			As Date)
		,	[TipAmortDet]									= Cast( '' 				As Char(30))
		,	[NrTipAmortDet]									= Cast( '' 				As Char(3))
		,	[AssetTransactionID]							= CAST( '' 				As Char(35))
		,	[AssetID]										= Cast( a.NrInv			As Char(35))
		,	[AssetTransactionType]							= Cast( '' 				As Char(9))
		,	[Description]									= Cast( '' 				As Char(35))
		,	[AssetTransactionDate]							= Cast( m.Data 			As Date)
		,	[TransactionID]									= Cast( 0 				As Numeric(15,2))
		,	[AssetValuationType]							= Cast( 'Contabil' 		As Char(35))
		,	[AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction]	= Cast( 0	 			As Numeric(15,2))
		,	[BookValueOnTransaction]						= Cast( 0 				As Numeric(15,2))
		,	[AssetTransactionAmount]						= Cast( 0 				As Numeric(15,2))
	From			MijloaceFixe				m
		Inner Join	AmortizariAnterioare		t	On	m.Id		= t.Id
		Inner Join	LuniAmortizareAnterioare	l	On	l.MaxLuna	= t.MaxLuna
		Left Join	Amortizari					a	On	a.IdMF		= t.Id		And	a.Id_Luna_An= l.Id
		Left Join	IesiriAnterioare			i	On	i.Id_MF		= t.Id
	Where  
			IsNull(m.Sters, 0)	= 0
		And IsNull(a.Sters, 0)	= 0
		And	m.Data				< @Data1
		And i.ID_MF Is Null
		And IsNull(m.Data_V, '99990101')>=@Data1
	Order By m.NrInv
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Mf_Detalii_Ani_Anteriori])

*Wait Window Str(Seconds()-aa,10,4) noclear
*
=MijloaceFixe_ADV('Mf_Detalii_Ani_Anteriori','')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets','Mf_Detalii_Ani_Anteriori'

*** aflu ultima luna din Amortizari in anii anteriori, pt. mf care nu se amortizeaza deloc ( nu exista in Amortizari)
*** trebuie sa le iau in select separat !!! 
*data1={01/01/2023}
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select 
		dbo.MyFirstDay(Month(m.Data), Year(m.Data)) As MaxLuna
		, m.*
		, m.Id		As Id_Din_MF
		, m.Id 		As IdMF
		, 'DA' 		As An_Ant
		, Month(m.Data) As nLuna
		, Year(m.Data)  As nAnul	
		, 0			As Durata_In_An
		, m.Data	As Data_punere_functiune
		, ''		As TipAmortDet
		, ''		As NrTipAmortDet
		, ''		As AssetTransactionID		
		, Cast( m.NrInv	As Char(35)) As AssetID
		, ''		As AssetTransactionType
		, ''		As Description
		, m.Data	As AssetTransactionDate
		, 0 		As TransactionID	
		,'Contabil' As AssetValuationType
		, 0 		As AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction
		, 0 		As BookValueOnTransaction
		, 0  		As AssetTransactionAmount
		From MijloaceFixe m  
		Where  
				IsNull(m.Sters, 0)=0
			And	m.Data<=?Data2
			And m.Id Not In 
				(
				Select a.IdMF	
				From Amortizari	a
				Inner Join LunaAnul l On a.Id_Luna_An = l.Id
				Where 	IsNull(a.Sters, 0)=0
					And IsNull(l.Sters, 0)=0
					And dbo.MyFirstDay(Month(l.nAnul), Year(l.nLuna))<=?Data2
				Group By a.IdMF	
				)  	
			And IsNull(m.Data_V, '99990101')>=?Data1
		Order By m.NrInv	
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari])
***			And	m.Data<=?Data2 
***
***					And DATEFROMPARTS( Year(l.nAnul), Month(l.nLuna), 01)<?Data1
*
=MijloaceFixe_ADV('MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari', '')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets', 'MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari'

*Browse Last


******  doar mijloacele care exista in anul curent **********************
*data1={01/01/2023}
*data2={31/12/2023}
*aa=Seconds()

*-------------------------------------------------------------------
*&& VICOS.28.05.2025  CodCls ar trebui sa fie luat din MijloaceFixe
*-------------------------------------------------------------------
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select 
		a.*	
		, Cast( 'nu' As Char(2)) As An_Ant
		, m.IsPublic
		, m.Data_Ies
		, m.Data_V	
		, l.nLuna
		, l.nAnul
		, Cast( '' 			As Date)      		As DataOper
		, Cast( 0 			As Numeric(15,2))	As ValActualAnterior				
		, Cast( 0 			As Numeric(15,2)) 	As ValRamAnterior
		, Cast( 0 			As Numeric(15,2))	As AmortPlAnterior
		, Cast( 0 			As Numeric(15))		As Durata_in_an
		, Cast( a.Data		As Date)			As Data_punere_functiune
		, Cast( '' 			As Char(30))		As TipAmortDet
		, Cast( '' 			As Char(3))  		As NrTipAmortDet
		, CAST( '' 			As Char(35))		As AssetTransactionID		
--		, Cast( a.IdMF 		As Char(35))	 	As AssetID
		, Cast( a.NrInv		As Char(35))	 	As AssetID
		, Cast( '' 			As Char(9))  		As AssetTransactionType
		, Cast( '' 			As Char(35))		As Description
		, Cast( m.Data 		As Date)	 		As AssetTransactionDate
--		, Cast( r.Id_nota	As Char(70)) 		As TransactionID	
--		, Cast( r.Ndp		As Char(70)) 		As TransactionID	
		, Cast( 'MF - '+ LTrim(Str(L.nLuna)) As Char(70)) 	As TransactionID	
		, Cast( 'Contabil' 	As Char(35)) 		As AssetValuationType
		, Cast( 0 			As Numeric(15,2)) 	As AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction
		, Cast( 0 			As Numeric(15,2)) 	As BookValueOnTransaction
		, Cast( 0	 		As Numeric(15,2)) 	As AssetTransactionAmount
		From MijloaceFixe m
			Inner Join Amortizari	a On a.IdMF	= m.ID
			Inner Join LunaAnul		l On l.Id	= a.Id_Luna_An
		Where  	
				IsNull(m.Sters, 0)=0 
			And	IsNull(a.Sters, 0)=0
			And	IsNull(l.Sters, 0)=0 
			And	m.Data<=?Data2
			And	l.nAnul=Year(?Data1)
		Order By NrInv	
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Mf_Detalii_An])
*Wait Window Str(Seconds()-aa,10,4)	  &&&& 47 secunde la ecf pe 2023 mullllt de tot !!!
*** schimba cele 2 comenturi din idmf in nrinv si din id_nota in Ndp daca trebuie.
*
** ecf Daf  dereglat - este vindut in 2022 deci nu ma influeanteaza in saft, in balanta valactual  +33915.70 ?????
*mySQLExec("Update amortizari set amortpl  =31243.10 where nrinv=68 and id_luna_an=276")
*mySQLExec("Update amortizari set valactual=31243.10 where valactual=-33915.70 and nrinv=68")
*Browse Last for nrinv=121 title '1  este 121 ? '


*Browse Last  fields  title 'Mf_Detalii_An '   nrinv, idmf, assetId  
=MijloaceFixe_ADV('Mf_Detalii_An', '')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets', 'Mf_Detalii_An'


Select Mf_Detalii_An
Replace All Data_punere_functiune With MyFirstDay( Month(GoMonth(Data_punere_functiune,1)), Year(GoMonth(Data_punere_functiune,1))) In Mf_Detalii_An
Replace All DataOper With MyLastDay( nLuna, nAnul)

Delete From Mf_Detalii_Ani_Anteriori Where Id_Din_MF In (Select IdMF From Mf_Detalii_An)

Select Mf_Detalii_An
Append From Dbf('Mf_Detalii_Ani_Anteriori') 
Append From Dbf('MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari') 
*Browse Last for nrinv=121 title '2  este 121 ? '

*** am caz la crida: nrinv (3,4,27,37,46,49,95, .......) la ceilati nu am vazut asa ( ecf, esc, eco)
*** MijloaceFixe cont:2132 cont_am:2813  Nota contabila o face cu cont din MijloaceFixe
*
Update Mf_Detalii_An;
Set;
	Cont 	= MijloaceFixe.Cont,;
	Cont_AM = MijloaceFixe.Cont_AM;
From MijloaceFixe;
Where;
	MijloaceFixe.Id = Mf_Detalii_An.IdMF
*

**** necesar pt. data punere in functiune cind au fost in conservare
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	 Select 
	 		ad.Data As DataIntrareConservare
	 		, ad.DataIesireConservare
	 		, ad.Id_mijlocfix as IdMF
	 		, m.NrInv
	 		, m.Denumire
		From ( Select Id_mijlocfix, Min(Data) as Data, DataIesireConservare 
					From AmortizariDetaliat 
					Where Denumire='Conservare' And Sters=0 And Data<=?Data2
					Group By Id_mijlocfix, DataIesireConservare 
			 ) ad
		Inner Join MijloaceFixe m On ad.Id_MijlocFix = m.Id 
		Left Join Amortizari    a On ad.Id_mijlocfix = a.IdMF 
		Where  
			m.Sters=0 
			And a.Sters=0
		Group By ad.Id_mijlocfix, ad.Data, ad.DataIesireConservare, m.NrInv, m.Denumire
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [AmortDet_Conservari])
***
=MijloaceFixe_ADV('AmortDet_Conservari','')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets','AmortDet_Conservari'


Update Mf_Detalii_An;
Set;
	Data_punere_functiune = AmortDet_Conservari.DataIesireConservare;
From AmortDet_Conservari;
WHERE AmortDet_Conservari.IdMF= Mf_Detalii_An.IdMF;
	And Data_punere_functiune = AmortDet_Conservari.DataIntrareConservare


*** pun durata in luni (la inceput an) pt. mijl.fixe care exista in ultima luna an anterior (prima luna din an!!!) ************************
***(LUNI RAMASE LA INCEPUT AN (mf vechi) SI DURATA TOTALA (mf noi in an) )	
* Data1={01/01/2023}
*Wait Window data1
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	 Select a.IdMF, 
	        a.Dur_Ram
		From MijloaceFixe m
		Left Join Amortizari a On a.IdMF=m.Id
		Inner Join LunaAnul  l On l.Id=a.Id_luna_an  
		Where  
			IsNull(m.Sters,0)=0 
			And IsNull(a.sters,0)=0
			And IsNull(l.Sters,0)=0
			And l.nAnul=Year(?Data1)-1 And l.nLuna=12 
		Order By a.IdMF
EndText
*!*		Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
*!*			 Select ..... 
*!*			        Case When a.Dur_ram+1 <= a.Durata_n Then a.Dur_ram+1 Else a.Durata_n End As Dur_Ram
*!*			Where	.... And l.nAnul=Year(?Data1) And l.nLuna=1 
*!*		EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Dur_ram_MF])
******
=MijloaceFixe_ADV('Dur_ram_MF','')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets','Dur_ram_MF'

*
Update Mf_Detalii_An;
	Set;
		Durata_in_an= Dur_ram_MF.Dur_ram;
	From Dur_ram_MF;
	WHERE Dur_ram_MF.IdMF= Mf_Detalii_An.IdMF
*****************************************************	  
***  pun durata in luni pt. mijl.fixe care Nu exista in prima luna din an ( intra in cursul anului ) *******************
Select ;
 		m.IdMF;
 		, m.Durata_n As Durata_n;
 		, m.nLuna;
	From Mf_Detalii_An m;
	Inner Join ( select IdMF, Min(nluna) as nLuna  from Mf_Detalii_An Where data>=myfirstday( 1, YEAR(data1)) Group By IdMF) a;
		 On a.IdMF=m.IdMF ;
		 	And m.nLuna=a.nLuna;
	Order By a.IdMF;
	Into Cursor Durata_n_MF
* 		Inner Join ( select IdMF, Min(nluna) as nLuna  from Mf_Detalii_An Where nLuna>1 Group By IdMF) a
Update Mf_Detalii_An;
	Set;
		Durata_in_an= Durata_n_MF.Durata_n;
	From Durata_n_MF;
	WHERE Durata_n_MF.IdMF= Mf_Detalii_An.IdMF;
		  And Durata_in_an=0
***	  

*Verificare dublare inregistrari( caz real ecoaqua pe 2023 fiecare luna dublata in id_luna_an ... toate sters=0)
	Select nLuna, idMF,  Count(nluna) As Dubluri ;
		From Mf_Detalii_An;
		Having dubluri >1;
		Where Nvl(an_ant,'nu')='nu';
		Group By nLuna, IdMF;
		Into Cursor dubluri_Id_luna_an
	If RecCount('dubluri_Id_luna_an')>0
		MessageBox("Aveti luni dublate la amortizari.",64, "Info...")
		Browse Last title 'Aveti luni dublate la amortizari.'
	EndIf 
	*
	Select durata_in_an, nrinv, Denumire ;
		From Mf_Detalii_An;
		Where durata_in_an=0;
		Order By nrinv;
		Into Cursor Lipsa_Durata_In_An
	If RecCount('Lipsa_Durata_In_An')>0
		*MessageBox("Lipsa durata inceput an.",64, "Info...")
		***Browse Last title "Lipsa durata inceput an (corect daca sint terenuri/ mijl. fixe care nu se amortizeaza)"
	EndIf 


***** versiunea 1  (CostBeginEnd tine cont de reevaluare neta) ************************************
**** necesar pt. AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin = Cost achizitie + modernizari (fara reevaluari)
***   'Casare','Conservare','Modernizare','Reevaluare','Reevaluare Net','Vanzare', ....		
*** tot pina la Reev. net se ignora (sterge).
*** caz 2005	 95.000  achizitie
***		2011	 17.265  modernizare
***		2011     15.508	 reevaluare
***     2014    128.880  revaluare net
***     2020	      0	 conservare
***
*Data1={01/01/2023}
*Data2={31/12/2023}
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select ad.* 
			From AmortizariDetaliat ad
			Inner Join MijloaceFixe m On m.Id= ad.Id_MijlocFix
			Where m.data <= ?Data2 And IsNull(ad.Sters,0)=0 And IsNull(m.Sters,0)=0
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [amort_det_la_zi])
	
	DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets','amort_det_la_zi'
	

	Select Max(Data) As Data, Id_MijlocFix, valoare  From amort_det_la_zi Where Denumire='Reevaluare Net' Group By Id_MijlocFix, valoare Into Cursor amortDet_reev_net
	Scan
		Delete From amort_det_la_zi ;
			Where amort_det_la_zi.Id_MijlocFix = amortDet_reev_net.Id_MijlocFix ;
				  And amort_det_la_zi.Data <= amortDet_reev_net.Data
	EndScan
	*
	Select 	m.IdMF,;
	 		Cast( Iif( m.Data<Data1,  m.Val, 0.00) + Nvl(ad.M_Begin,0) As N(15,2)) As Acq_Begin,;
	 		Cast( Iif( m.Data<=Data2, m.Val, 0.00) + Nvl(ad.M_End,0)   As N(15,2)) As Acq_End,;
			m.Val,;
	 		m.NrInv,;
	 		m.Denumire;
		  From ( Select m.IdMF, m.Data,;
		  				Iif( Nvl(rn.Id_MijlocFix,0)=0, m.Val, rn.Valoare ) as Val,;
		  				m.NrInv, m.Denumire ;
		  			From Mf_Detalii_An m;
		  			Left Join amortDet_reev_net rn On rn.Id_MijlocFix = m.IdMF; 
		  			Group By m.IdMF; 
			   ) m;
		  Left Join ( Select Id_mijlocfix,;
							 Cast( Sum( Iif( Data< Data1, Valoare, 000000000000000.00)) As N(15,2))  As M_Begin,;
							 Cast( Sum( Iif( Data<=Data2, Valoare, 000000000000000.00)) As N(15,2))  As M_End;
						From amort_det_la_zi;
						Where Denumire='Modernizare';
						Group By Id_mijlocfix;
				    ) ad On ad.Id_MijlocFix = m.IdMF; 
		  Into Cursor CostBeginEnd ReadWrite

*!*	***** versiunea 2  (CostBeginEnd NU tine cont de reevaluare neta) ************************************
*!*	*** old bunisor ( nu tine cont de reevaluari net >>> se incepe cu valoare noua desi corect ar fi fara reev. neta pt. ca este 
*!*	***               AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin = val achizitie+modernizare ( daca las asa nu pot verifica begin/end la balanta vs. saft )
*!*	***				  - o sa fac si varianta cu reev. net sa am 2 xls de verificare.
*!*	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
*!*		 Select 
*!*				m.ID As IdMF
*!*		 		, Case When  m.Data<?Data1   Then m.Val   Else 0 End    + IsNull(ad.M_Begin,0) As Acq_Begin 
*!*		 		, Case When  m.Data<=?Data2  Then m.Val   Else 0 End    + IsNull(ad.M_End,0)   As Acq_End
*!*				, m.Val
*!*		 		, m.NrInv
*!*		 		, m.Denumire
*!*			From MijloaceFixe m
*!*			Left Join ( Select Id_mijlocfix
*!*							, Sum( Case When Data<?Data1 	Then Valoare Else 0 End ) As M_Begin
*!*							, Sum( Case When Data<=?Data2 	Then Valoare Else 0 End ) As M_End
*!*						From AmortizariDetaliat 
*!*						Where Denumire='Modernizare' And Sters=0
*!*						Group By Id_mijlocfix
*!*				 ) ad On ad.Id_MijlocFix=m.Id 
*!*			Where IsNull(m.Sters,0)=0 
*!*				And m.Data<=?Data2
*!*	EndText
*!*	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [CostBeginEnd_fara_reev_net])
*!*	*Browse Last for AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin <> AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd
*!*	** pt. Mfixe intrate in cursul anului AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin = 0
*!*	** pt. Mfixe iesite  in cursul anului AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd   = 0
*
*!*	Update CostBeginEnd_fara_reev_net;
*!*		Set Acq_End = 0;
*!*		From AmortizariDetaliatAn;
*!*		Where AmortizariDetaliatAn.Id_mijlocfix= CostBeginEnd_fara_reev_net.IdMF And ;
*!*			  InList( AmortizariDetaliatAn.Tip, 'Casare', 'Vanzare')	

=MijloaceFixe_ADV('CostBeginEnd','')
	
*
Update CostBeginEnd;
Set;
	Acq_End = 0;
From AmortizariDetaliatAn;
Where;
		AmortizariDetaliatAn.Id_mijlocfix = CostBeginEnd.IdMF;
	And	InList( AmortizariDetaliatAn.Tip, 'Casare', 'Vanzare')
		  
*
*!*	Select	  m.*;
*!*			, Cast(m.Cont  			As Char(18))		As Cont_MF;
*!*			, Cast(Cont_SAFT(m.Cont) As Char(18))  		As AccountId;
*!*			, Cast(c.Acq_Begin  	As Numeric(15,2))	As AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin;
*!*			, Cast(c.Acq_End		As Numeric(15,2))	As AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd;	
*!*		From ( Select *	From Mf_Detalii_An	Where Nvl(An_Ant,'nu')='nu' ;
*!*			   Union All;
*!*			   Select * From Mf_Detalii_An	Where Nvl(An_Ant,'nu')='DA' And;
*!*						  						  NrInv Not In ( Select NrInv From Mf_Detalii_An Where Nvl(An_Ant,'nu')='nu');
*!*			 ) m ;
*!*		Inner Join ( SELECT IdMF, MAX(nLuna) as maxLuna From Mf_Detalii_An Group By IdMf ) b;
*!*			On m.IdMF = b.IdMF ;  
*!*		Left Join CostBeginEnd_cu_reev_net c On c.IdMF = m.IdMF;      
*!*		Where m.nLuna = b.maxLuna;      	
*!*		Into Cursor Mf_Unice_cu_reev_net ReadWrite 	
*	
Select;
		RecNo() as NrCrt; 
		, m.*;
		, Cast(m.Cont  			As Char(18))		As Cont_MF;
		, Cast(Cont_SAFT(m.Cont) As Char(18))  		As AccountId;
		, Cast(m.Data			As Date)			As DateOfAcquisition;
		, Cast(m.Data_punere_functiune As Date)		As StartUpDate;
		, Cast(Nvl(m.CodCls,'')	As Char(30))		As ValuationClass;
		, Cast(c.Acq_Begin  	As Numeric(15,2))	As AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin;
		, Cast(c.Acq_End		As Numeric(15,2))	As AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd;	
		, Cast(0 				As Numeric(15,2))	As InvestmentSupport;
		, Cast(m.Durata_in_an 	As Numeric(15,2))	As AssetLifeMonth;		
		, Cast(0				As Numeric(15,2))	As AssetAddition;
		, Cast(0 				As Numeric(15,2))	As Transfers;
		, Cast(0 				As Numeric(15,2))	As AssetDisposal;
		, Cast(0 				As Numeric(15,2))	As BookValueBegin;		
		, Cast(m.Tip_Amortizare As Char(30))		As DepreciationMethod;	
		, Cast('' 				As Numeric(15,2))	As DepreciationPercentage;
		, Cast('' 				As Numeric(15,2))	As DepreciationForPeriod;
		, Cast('' 				As Numeric(15,2))	As AppreciationForPeriod;		
		, Cast('' 				As Numeric(15,2))	As ExtraordinaryDepreciationsForPeriod;
		, Cast('0' 				As Char(35))		As ExtraordinaryDepreciationMethod;
		, Cast(0				As Numeric(15,2))	As ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod;
		, Cast(m.AmortPl 		As Numeric(15,2))	As AccumulatedDepreciation;		
		, Cast(0 				As Numeric(15,2))	As BookValueEnd;	
	From;
		(;
			Select * From Mf_Detalii_An	Where Nvl(An_Ant,'nu')='nu' ;
		   Union All;
		   Select * From Mf_Detalii_An	Where Nvl(An_Ant,'nu')='DA' And;
					  						  NrInv Not In ( Select NrInv From Mf_Detalii_An Where Nvl(An_Ant,'nu')='nu');
		 ) m ;
	Inner Join ( SELECT IdMF, MAX(nLuna) As maxLuna From Mf_Detalii_An Group By IdMf ) b;
		On m.IdMF = b.IdMF ;  
	Left Join CostBeginEnd c On c.IdMF = m.IdMF;      
	Where m.nLuna = b.maxLuna;      	
	Into Cursor Mf_Unice ReadWrite 
*
*** m.fixe amortizate in anii anteriori nu apar aici si trebuie luate + dublare la cele care intra in dec anterior
*Browse Last title Str(RecCount())	
* caz (ESC) nrinv=100 Bar lemn -  amortizat complet in 2012 si reevaluat net in 31.12.2024 amortPl(amortizat)=0 dar amortizat integral 12434.
*     va trebuie sa pun amortPl=0 (atat si arata in situatia la zi in mijloacefixe si in balanta),
*	  pot lua ce exista in mijloace fixe acum dar nu e mereu ok ( ok-la reevaluari viitoare, NU E OK- la viitoare modernizari ca va calc. amortizare iar)
*  rezolvare cam asa: 1. ultima operatiune in amortizaridetaliat = reevaluare net pina la data<=data2   si 
*					  2. inregistrari in amortizari in anul curent=0 
*data2={31/12/2023}
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select ad.Id_MijlocFix, ad.Data, ad.Den_amortizari
		From ( Select Id_MijlocFix, Max(Data) As Data 
		       From AmortizariDetaliat Where Data<=?Data2 And Sters=0
		       Group By Id_MijlocFix 
		     ) a
		Left Join AmortizariDetaliat ad 
			On ad.Id_MijlocFix  = a.Id_MijlocFix And ad.Data = a.Data
			   -- daca am 2 operatiuni in aceiasi data nu face bine  
		Where ad.Denumire ='Reevaluare Net'
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [mf_cu_reev_net_ultima_oper])
*
Select * From mf_cu_reev_net_ultima_oper ;
	Where Id_MijlocFix In ( Select IdMF From Mf_Detalii_An WHERE An_ant='DA');
	Into Cursor reev_net_amortizate_complet
*
Update Mf_Unice;
	Set AccumulatedDepreciation = 0;
	From reev_net_amortizate_complet;
	Where reev_net_amortizate_complet.Id_MijlocFix = Mf_Unice.IdMF
****************************************************************************************




SELECT Mf_Unice
Update Mf_Unice;
	Set BookValueBegin = MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari.ValRam,;
		BookValueEnd   = MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari.ValRam;
	From MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari;
	Where MF_care_nu_apar_in_amortizari.IdMF = Mf_Unice.IdMF

*Browse Last  title ' Mf_Unice' fields nrinv, idmf, assetId  &&& ba e bine aici ????
*** ValuationClass pentru imobilizari necorporale poate lua valoarea 0 sau 99999999 (trece de Duk)
*
* Description in AssetsTransaction ( "Amortizare", "Casare", etc.) 
* Description in Assets ( "Cladire principala", "Dulap mare", etc.) 
Replace all Description With Denumire   
*

*** aflu  valoare amortizata si val. efectiva  mf in luna 1 si 12
*Data1={01/01/2023}
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	 Select 
	 		a.IdMF
	 		, Sum(Case When  l.nLuna=12 And l.nAnul=Year(?Data1)-1  Then  a.ValRam 		Else 0 End) As ValRam_L1
	 		, Sum(Case When  l.nLuna=12 And l.nAnul=Year(?Data1)    Then  a.ValRam 		Else 0 End) As ValRam_L12
	 		, Sum(Case When  				l.nAnul=Year(?Data1)    Then  -a.AmortLuna 	Else 0 End) As Amortiz_An
		From MijloaceFixe m
		Left Join Amortizari a  On a.IdMF=m.Id
		Inner Join LunaAnul  l  On l.Id=a.Id_luna_an  
		Where  
			m.Sters=0 
			And a.sters=0
			And l.Sters=0
			And ( l.nAnul=Year(?Data1)-1 Or l.nAnul=Year(?Data1) )
		Group By a.IdMF
		Order By a.IdMF
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [valoareMF_luna_1_si_12])
*
=MijloaceFixe_ADV('valoareMF_luna_1_si_12','')
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'Assets','valoareMF_luna_1_si_12'

*
***
Update Mf_Unice;
	Set BookValueBegin			= valoareMF_luna_1_si_12.ValRam_L1,;
		BookValueEnd 			= valoareMF_luna_1_si_12.ValRam_L12,;
		DepreciationForPeriod	= valoareMF_luna_1_si_12.Amortiz_An,;
		DepreciationPercentage	= IIF( AssetLifeMonth=0,  0, Round( 100/Durata_n, 2 ));
	From valoareMF_luna_1_si_12;
	Where valoareMF_luna_1_si_12.IdMF = Mf_Unice.IdMF
		
*
*
* AppreciationForPeriod (AmortizariDetaliatAn.reevaluare_dif) = reevaluari doar diferenta 
*** trebuie sa pun NC amortizare din luna respectiva in TransactionId
Update AmortizariDetaliatAn;
	Set TransactionId = Mf_Detalii_An.TransactionId;
	From Mf_Detalii_An;
	Where 	IsNullOrEmpty(AmortizariDetaliatAn.TransactionId) And ;
			AmortizariDetaliatAn.Id_luna_an = Mf_Detalii_An.Id_Luna_An 
*
Select AmortizariDetaliatAn
*
Scan For Atc( 'Reevaluare', Denumire)>0
	*
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select a.ValActual 
		From Amortizari a
		Inner join LunaAnul L On L.Id = a.Id_Luna_An
		Where
			a.IdMF = ?AmortizariDetaliatAn.Id_mijlocFix And
			l.nLuna =  Month(DATEADD(month, -1, ?AmortizariDetaliatAn.Data)) And
			l.nAnul =  Year(DATEADD( month, -1, ?AmortizariDetaliatAn.Data))
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [ValActual_Anterior])
	*
	=MijloaceFixe_ADV('ValActual_Anterior','aici trebuie revazut cind am xls reevaluari')
	*
	If RecCount('ValActual_Anterior')>0
		Replace Reevaluare_Dif With Valoare - ValActual_Anterior.ValActual  In AmortizariDetaliatAn
	EndIf
	*
EndScan
*
Select id_mijlocfix;
		, Sum( Iif( Reevaluare_Dif>0, Reevaluare_Dif, 0.00)) As Reevaluare_poz;
		, Sum( Iif( Reevaluare_Dif<0, Reevaluare_Dif, 0.00)) As Reevaluare_neg;
	From AmortizariDetaliatAn;
	Group By id_mijlocfix;
	Into Cursor Aprecieri_reevaluare
*
Update Mf_Unice;
	Set AppreciationForPeriod 				= Reevaluare_poz,;
		ExtraordinaryDepreciationsForPeriod = Reevaluare_neg;
	From Aprecieri_reevaluare;
	Where Aprecieri_reevaluare.Id_mijlocFix = Mf_Unice.IdMF	
*** reev_neg ar trebui in astea 3 de jos:
*!*	ExtraordinaryDepreciationsForPeriod		 ok	 reev_neg	Deprecieri extraordinare pentru acest activ în perioada *(ex.: ajustãri de depreciere cind mijlocul fix trece in conservare , etc.).
*!*	ExtraordinaryDepreciationMethod			 ok  '0'		Metoda de amortizare extraordinara în timpul perioadei de selectie.
*!*	ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod ok  			Valoarea amortizarii extraordinare în timpul perioadei de selectie (suma amortizata dupa reev_negativa): 

*								Calculez pina la prima operatiune din amortizariDetaliat( nu conteaza ce este; reev, modernizare, casare, vanzare, etc... pune amortizare cumulata si iese)
*															- amortizarea in perioada de reevaluare negativa
*data2={31/12/2023}
Select Id_MijlocFix;
		, Min(Data) as Data_min ;
		, Data2 As Data_max ;  &&& 31.12.2023
	From AmortizariDetaliatAn ;
	Where Atc( 'reevaluare', tip)>0 And Reevaluare_Dif<0 ; 
	Group by Id_MijlocFix ;
	Into Cursor Reevaluari_negative ReadWrite  
Scan
	*
	Select AmortizariDetaliatAn 
	Scan For Id_MijlocFix = Reevaluari_negative.Id_MijlocFix And Data > Reevaluari_negative.Data_Min
		* amortizare cu val. reevaluare din luna urmatoare
		Replace Data_max With AmortizariDetaliatAn.Data  In Reevaluari_negative
		*
		Select Sum(AmortLuna) As sum_amortiz;
			From Mf_Detalii_An;
			Where IdMF = Reevaluari_negative.Id_mijlocFix;
				 And Between( nLuna, MONTH(Reevaluari_negative.Data_Min), MONTH(Reevaluari_negative.Data_Max) );
			Group By Mf_Detalii_An.IdMF;	 
			Into Cursor Amortizari_dupa_reev_negativa	 
		*
		If RecCount('Amortizari_dupa_reev_negativa')>0
			Replace Amort_reev_negativa With Amortizari_dupa_reev_negativa.sum_amortiz In AmortizariDetaliatAn	
		ENDIF
		*
		Exit  &&& la prima pozitie( nu conteaza ce este; reev, modernizare, casare, vanzare, etc... pune amortizare cumulata si iese)
		*
	EndScan
	*
EndScan	
*

********************************************************************
*** 'Casare','Conservare','Modernizare','Reevaluare','Reevaluare Net','Vanzare', ....		
Select;
		Id_MijlocFix;
	,	Sum( IIf(Denumire='Modernizare', Valoare, 0.00)) As SModerniz;
	,	Sum( IIf(InList(Denumire, 'Casare', 'Vanzare'), Nvl(-Val_MF,0), 0.00)) As Iesiri;
	,	Sum( Amort_reev_negativa ) As Amort_reev_negativa ;
From AmortizariDetaliatAn;
Group By Id_MijlocFix;
Into Cursor Aprecieri ReadWrite
*
Update Mf_Unice;
Set;
		AssetAddition 								= Aprecieri.SModerniz;
	,	AssetDisposal								= Aprecieri.Iesiri;
	,	ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod	= Amort_reev_negativa ;
From Aprecieri;
Where;
	 Aprecieri.Id_mijlocFix = Mf_Unice.IdMF

Update Mf_Unice;
	Set ExtraordinaryDepreciationMethod	= 'Liniara';
	Where ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod<>0 Or ;
	      ExtraordinaryDepreciationsForPeriod<>0

****************** 'Casare','Conservare','Modernizare','Reevaluare','Reevaluare Net','Vanzare', ....		
*!*	AssetID								ok	IdMF sau NrInv ????????		Nr. unic mijloc fix
*!*	AccountID							ok								Cont contabil mijloc fix
*!*	Description							ok	CLADIRE PRINCIPALA			Descriere mijloc fix
*!*	DateOfAcquisition					ok								Data cumparare
*!*	StartUpDate							ok								Data punere in functiune (prima luna cu amortizare, si nu luna urmatoare achizitiei)
*!*	AssetValuationType					ok	'Contabil'					Descrie scopul raportarii: f.i. comercial, fiscal în tara 1, fiscal în tara 2 etc.
*!*	ValuationClass						ok	Amortizare.CodCls			Clasificarea activului în scopuri de raportare (fiscala)
*!*	AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin	ok	Amortizare.Val+ad.Valoare	Costurile achizitie si productie la 01.01.2023 ( achizitie + modernizari (fara reevaluari)... dar cu reev_nete)
*!*	AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd	ok  Amortizare.Val+ad.Valoare	Costurile achizitie si productie la 31.12.2023 ( achizitie + modernizari (fara reevaluari)... dar cu reev_nete)
*																		(sold final D ct 21xx)									
*!*	InvestmentSupport					ok	0-zero						Valoarea cheltuielilor ulterioare capitalizate in investitii pt.acest activ -*"in cazul mijloacelor fixe pentru care nu s-au primit subventii pentru investitii, valoarea de raportat este 0."							
*!*	AssetLifeYear						ok  se trece doar in luni 		Perioada de viata utila în ani (se exclude daca se foloseste AssetLifeMonth)
*!*	AssetLifeMonth						ok	Durata_in_an				Perioada de viata utila în luni (LUNI RAMASE LA INCEPUT AN (mf vechi) SI DURATA TOTALA (mf noi in an) )
*!*	AssetAddition						ok	AmortizareDetaliat.Valoare	'Modernizare' ...Valoarea contabila a achizitiei/productiei m.fix. - crestere (e.g. prin modernizare, nu include reevaluari), în cursul anului.
*!*	Transfers							ok	0-zero						Valoarea contabila transfer mijl.fix în anul curent sau invers;- transfer din categ. mijl.fix in categ. active circulante, sau invers *(inclusiv reclasificãri intre conturi diferite de mijloace fixe)
*!*	AssetDisposal						ok	MijloaceFixe.ValActual		'Casare', 'Vanzare' .... Valoarea contabila a iesirilor de mijloace fixe
*!*																		în timpul perioadei selectate (cu semn minus)
*																		(rulaj creditor al contului 21xx)
*!*	BookValueBegin						ok	Amortizare.ValRam (an-1_L12) Valoarea contabila la începutul perioadei de selectie (ramas de amortizat)
*																		 (sold initial ct D 21331-sold initial ct C 281)
*!*	DepreciationMethod					ok  MijloaceFixe.Tip_Amortizare	Metoda de amortizare normala în timpul perioadei de selectie- de exemplu „LINIARÃ”, „ACCELERATÃ”, „DEGRESIVÃ” sau „UNITATE DE PRODUS”)
*!*	DepreciationPercentage				ok	(100/ durata_n, 2)			Rata de amortizare normala pe an/luna (depinde de perioada de viata aleasa)
*!*	DepreciationForPeriod				ok	Amortizare.AmortLuna 		Suma totala a amortizarii normale în timpul perioadei de selectie - informatiile contabile cu privire la mijloacele fixe (inclusiv amortizarea).
*																		(Total rulaj C 281)
*!*	AppreciationForPeriod				ok	+AmortDet.Valoare-ValActual	Valoarea totala a aprecierii în timpul perioadei de selectie  *O astfel de situatie poate fi generata de reevaluarea mijloacelor fixe.
*																		( AmortizariDetaliat.Valoare (=reeevaluare) - ValActual  luna anterioara)
*																		*** (reevaluare pozitiva)
*!*	ExtraordinaryDepreciationsForPeriod	ok	-AmortDet.Valoare-ValActual	Deprecieri extraordinare pentru acest activ în perioada de selectie *(ex.: ajustãri de depreciere înregistrate cind mijlocul fix este trecut in conservare , etc.).
																		*** (reevaluare negativa)
*!*	ExtraordinaryDepreciationMethod		ok  0-zero						Metoda de amortizare extraordinara în perioada de selectie.Metoda de depreciere extraordinarã în timpul perioadei - 
*																		se completeazã cu metoda contabilã folositã pentru calcul ajustãrilor pentru deprecierea mijloacelor fixe.
*																		În cazul în care contribuabilul nu are o metodã de depreciere extraordinarã, se completeazã cu 0 (zero).
*!*	ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod ok  0-zero					Valoarea amortizarii extraordinare în timpul perioadei de selectie
*!*	AccumulatedDepreciation				ok	Amortizare.AmortPl 			Valoarea totala a amortizarii pentru acest activ (sold ct C 281 la 31.12.2022) 
*!*	BookValueEnd						ok	Amortizare.ValRam (an_L12)	Valoarea contabila la sfârsitul perioadei de selectie (sold final ct D 21xx - sold final ct C 281)

Select * From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts Into Cursor GLA_Caut_Sintetic_Cu_Analitice
*
Select;
	m.Cont_MF											As Saft_Cont,;
	m.Suma_In											As Saft_In,;
	m.Suma_Out											As Saft_Out,;
	;
	Iif(Suma_In = 0, Suma_Out - Suma_In, 0)				As Saft_RulajDB21x,;
	;
	-m.RulajCr21x										As Saft_RulajCr21x,;
	Cast( g.AccountId As C(20))							As Bal_Cont,;
	Nvl(g.OpeningDebitBalance, 0)						As Bal_Db_In,;  &&&	(sold initial D ct 21..)
	Nvl(g.ClosingDebitBalance, 0)						As Bal_Db_Out,; &&&	(sold final   D ct 21..)
	;
	Iif(;
			Nvl(g.OpeningDebitBalance,0) = 0,;
			Nvl(g.ClosingDebitBalance,0) - Nvl(g.OpeningDebitBalance,0),;
			0;
		)												As Bal_RulajDb21x,;&&&  (rulaj debitor  al contului 21..)
	;
	Nvl(g.Rulaj_C,0)									As Bal_RulajCr21x,;&&&  (rulaj creditor al contului 21..)
	Nvl(m.Suma_In,0)  - Nvl(g.OpeningDebitBalance,0)	As Dif_In,;
	Nvl(m.Suma_Out,0) - Nvl(g.ClosingDebitBalance,0)	As Dif_Out,;
	;
	Cast(0 As N(14, 2))									As Dif_RulajD,;
	;
	Nvl(-m.RulajCr21x,0) - Nvl(g.Rulaj_C,0)				As Dif_RulajC;
From (;
	Select;
		AccountId As Cont_MF,;
		Sum(AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin)			As Suma_In,;
		Sum(AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd)			As Suma_Out,;
		Sum(AssetDisposal)								As RulajCr21x;  &&& Iesiri/casari rulaj cr 21..
	From Mf_Unice;
	Group By AccountId;
		 ) m ;
	Full Outer Join GLA_Caut_Sintetic_Cu_Analitice g On g.AccountId = m.Cont_MF;	
Where;
	(;
		InList(Left(g.AccountId,2), '20','21');
			Or;
	    InList(Left(m.Cont_MF,2), '20','21');
	);
	And Iif( AreAnalitic( Cont, .F., 'GLA_Caut_Sintetic_Cu_Analitice')=.T., 'da', 'nu' )='nu' ;
Into Cursor Verif_MF_21xx_Val_Intrare_Si_Sf_An_Cu_RulajC ReadWrite
*
Update Verif_MF_21xx_Val_Intrare_Si_Sf_An_Cu_RulajC Set Dif_RulajD = Saft_RulajDB21x - Bal_RulajDb21x
*
If RecCount('Verif_MF_21xx_val_intrare_si_sf_an_cu_rulajC')>0
	Copy To lcDirectorSAFT + 'Verif_MF_21xx_val_intrare_si_sf_an_cu_rulajC' type xls
EndIf

*!*				   	Cast( g.AccountId as c(20)) As Gla_Cont,;
*!*					g.OpeningDebitBalance  As Gla_db_in,;  &&&	(sold initial D ct 21xx)
*!*					g.ClosingDebitBalance  As Gla_db_out,; &&&	(sold final   D ct 21xx)

*!*						Nvl(m.Suma_out,0) - Nvl(g.ClosingDebitBalance,0) As Dif_out;

*!*					 ) m ;

Select 	 m.Cont_Am	   				 As Saft_Cont,;
		-m.AmortAn					 As Saft_Amort_An,;
		 m.AmortTotal				 As Saft_Amort_Sold,;
	   	Cast( g.AccountId as c(20))  As Bal_Cont,;
		Nvl(g.Rulaj_C,0)			 As Bal_Amort_An,;  
		Nvl(g.ClosingCreditBalance,0) As Bal_Amort_Sold,;  
		-m.AmortAn    - Nvl(g.Rulaj_C,0)  			   As Dif_An,;
		 m.AmortTotal - Nvl(g.ClosingCreditBalance,0)  As Dif_Total;
	From (;
		Select;
			Cont_am,;
			Sum(DepreciationForPeriod)   As AmortAn,;   &&&  Amortizat in an (Total rulaj C 281) 
			Sum(AccumulatedDepreciation) As AmortTotal; &&&  Amortizat total (sold ct C 281 la 31.12.2022) 
		From Mf_Unice;
		Group By Cont_Am;
		 ) m ;
	Full Outer Join GLA_Caut_Sintetic_Cu_Analitice g On g.AccountId = m.Cont_Am;
	Where ( InList( Left(g.AccountId,3), '280', '281') Or ;
			InList( Left(m.Cont_Am,3)  , '280', '281');
		  );
		  And Iif( AreAnalitic( g.Cont, .F., 'GLA_Caut_Sintetic_Cu_Analitice')=.T., 'da', 'nu' )='nu' ;
	Group By m.Cont_Am;	  
	Into Cursor Verif_MF_Amort_An_Si_Total_RulajC_281
****
If RecCount('Verif_MF_Amort_An_Si_Total_RulajC_281')>0
	Copy To lcDirectorSAFT + 'Verif_MF_Amort_An_Si_Total_RulajC_281' type xls
EndIf

*** SELECT Cont, Cont_am From Mf_Unice GROUP BY Cont, Cont_am Order By Cont, Cont_am Into Cursor Conturi_MF
*!*	cont	cont_am    *** astea cu 280, 281 sint terenuri (nu se amortizeaza- ca altfel ii prindeam) 
*!*	2111	280
*!*	2111	281
*!*	2111	2811
*!*	2111	2813
*!*	2111	EROARE!!! Cont Amort
*!*	2121	2812
*!*	2131	2813
*!*	2132	2813
*!*	2133	2813
*!*	214		2813
*!*	214		2814
** nu pot face defalcat pe fiecare cont ( de ex. in 2813 intra la gramada 2131/2132/2133 ) as putea face defalcat pe vreo 3 conturi dar vad ca fiecare trece ce conturi vrea..
** voi face la gramada '281' si left(cont, 2)='21' and Not inlist( left(cont,3),'215','216','217' )

*** aici la ESC am diferente pt. ca am terenuri (nu se amortizeaza deloc) dar au ValRam=0 in loc de ValRam=Val  ~1468528.36 diferenta
Select 	m.Neamort_1					 As Saft_Neamort_init,;
		m.Neamort_12				 As Saft_Neamort_final,;
		g.init_Db_21 - g.init_cr_281 As Bal_Neamort_init,;  
		g.sold_Db_21 - g.sold_cr_281 As Bal_Neamort_final,;  
		m.Neamort_1  - (g.init_Db_21 - g.init_cr_281) As Dif_init,;
		m.Neamort_12 - (g.sold_Db_21 - g.sold_cr_281) As Dif_final;
	From ( Select '1' as leg,;
 				  Sum(BookValueBegin) As Neamort_1,; &&&  neAmortizat la 01.01.2022 (sold initial ct D 21331-sold initial ct C 281) 
				  Sum(BookValueEnd)   As Neamort_12; &&&  neAmortizat la 31.12.2023 (sold final   ct D 21331-sold final   ct C 281) 
				  Group By 1;
				From Mf_Unice; 
		 ) m ;
	Full Outer Join ( Select '1' as leg,;
								 Sum( Iif( InList(Left(AccountId,2), '20','21') And Len(AllTrim(AccountId))=3, Nvl(OpeningDebitBalance ,0), 0.00)) As init_Db_21 ,;
								 Sum( Iif( InList(cont,'281','280') And Len(AllTrim(AccountId))=3			 , Nvl(OpeningCreditBalance,0), 0.00)) As init_cr_281,;
								 Sum( Iif( InList(Left(AccountId,2), '20','21') And Len(AllTrim(AccountId))=3, Nvl(ClosingDebitBalance ,0), 0.00)) As sold_Db_21 ,;
								 Sum( Iif( InList(cont,'281','280') And Len(AllTrim(AccountId))=3 		     , Nvl(ClosingCreditBalance,0), 0.00)) As sold_cr_281; 
							 From GLA_Caut_Sintetic_Cu_Analitice ;
							 Group By 1;							 
						) g On g.Leg = m.Leg;
	Into Cursor Verif_MF_Neamortizat_An_Si_Total_21xD_28xC

EndIf

*!*	1. Verif_MF_21xx_val_intrare_si_sf_an_cu_rulajC.dbf
*!*		AcquisitionAndProductionCostsBegin = Val MF si an			&&& sold initial D ct 21..
*!*		AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd   = Val MF sf an			&&& sold final   D ct 21..

*!*	2. Verif_MF_Amort_An_Si_Total_RulajC_281.dbf
*!*		DepreciationForPeriod			   = amortizat in an		&&& Amortizat in an (Total rulaj C 281) 
*!*		AccumulatedDepreciation			   = amortizat pina la sf an&&& Amortizat total (sold ct C 281 la 31.12.2022)
*!*		
*!*	3. Verif_MF_Neamortizat_An_Si_Total_21xD_28xC.dbf
*!*		BookValueBegin					   = neAmortizat si an (sold initial ct D 21..-sold initial ct C 281) la 01.01.2022
*!*		BookValueEnd					   = neAmortizat sf an (sold initial ct D 21..-sold initial ct C 281) la 31.12.2022


***** la avd  gruparea nu imi face unice si nu stiu de ce 
***** posibil sa o las doar o data pe 2023  pina imi dau 12 fisiere xls corect si nu ian. 2023 multiplicat de 12 ori
If Upper(m.NumeDB) = "AVICOLADRAGOSVODA" And lcTipDeclaratie = 'A'
	Select * From Mf_Unice Group By IdMF, Denumire  Into Cursor Mf_unice_ADV ReadWrite
	*
	*-- 25.06.2024
	*
	Update Mf_unice_ADV;
	Set;
		AssetAddition 			= ad.TotalModernizari,;
		AssetDisposal 			= ad.ValActualMF,; 
		AppreciationForPeriod 	= Max(ad.TotalReev - ad.ValActual,0); 
	From (Select a.NrInv,;
					 Sum( Iif( Lower(a.Tip)='modernizare'				, a.Valoare  , 000000000.00)) As TotalModernizari ,;
					 Sum( Iif( InList(Lower(a.Tip), 'casare', 'vanzare'), b.ValActual, 000000000.00)) As ValActualMF,;
					 Sum( Iif( InList(Lower(a.Tip), 'reevaluare net', 'reevaluare brut'), a.Valoare  , 000000000.00)) As TotalReev,;
					 Iif(      InList(Lower(a.Tip), 'reevaluare net', 'reevaluare brut'), a.ValActual, 000000000.00)  As ValActual;
				From (cFileModernizariDinXLS) a;
				Inner Join Mf_unice_ADV b On b.NrInv=a.NrInv;
				Group By a.NrInv; 
			) ad;
	Where;
		ad.nrInv = Mf_unice_ADV.nrinv	
	*-- 25.06.2024
	*
	Replace All BookValueEnd   With Max( 0, BookValueBegin   + DepreciationForPeriod*12 )
	Replace All AccumulatedDepreciation  With ;
	          Min( AcquisitionAndProductionCostsEnd, AccumulatedDepreciation - DepreciationForPeriod*11 )			

	Select Mf_unice
	Zap
	Append From Dbf('Mf_unice_ADV')
EndIf

\								<ExtraordinaryDepreciationMethod><<Allt(ExtraordinaryDepreciationMethod)>></ExtraordinaryDepreciationMethod>
\								<ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod><<Allt(STR(ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod,18,2))>></ExtraordinaryDepreciationAmountForPeriod>
\							</ExtraordinaryDepreciationForPeriod>
\						</ExtraordinaryDepreciationsForPeriod>
\						<AccumulatedDepreciation><<Allt(STR(AccumulatedDepreciation,18,2))>></AccumulatedDepreciation>
\						<BookValueEnd><<Allt(STR(BookValueEnd,18,2))>></BookValueEnd>					
\					</Valuation>
\				</Valuations>
\			</Asset>
*
ENDSCAN
*
\		</Assets>

Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'14.Assets.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'14.Assets.xml'+[...])
If FORCE_REFRESH
	DoEvents FORCE
EndIf
ENDFUNC

*
Function _18SalesInvoices()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	18.SalesInvoices
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select
		  [InvoiceNo]				=	a.NumarDoc
		, [CustomerID]				=	Space(35)
		, [City]					=	c.Localitate
		, [Country]					=	c.Tara
		, [AccountID]				=	CAST( c.Cont As Char(30))
		, [StandardAccountID]		=	CAST( c.Cont As Char(30))
		, [InvoiceDate]				=	a.DataDoc
		, [InvoiceType]				=	Space(3)
		, [SelfBillingIndicator]	=	Space(3)
		, [DebitCreditIndicator]	=	Case When b.Valoare>=0 Then 'C' Else 'D' End
		, [Quantity]				=	b.Cantitate
		, [UnitPrice]				=	b.PretUnitar
		, [TaxPointDate]			=	a.DataDoc
		, [Description]				=	b.Denumire
		, [Amount]					=	b.Valoare
		, [CurrencyCode]			=	'RON'
		, [CurrencyAmount]			=	b.Valoare
		, [ExchangeRate]			=	Cast(1 As Numeric(18, 4))
		, [TaxType]					=	Space(3)
		, [TaxCode]					=	Space(6)
		, [TaxPercentage]			=	b.ProcTva
		, [TaxBase]					=	b.Valoare
		, [TaxBaseDescription]		=	b.DenumireTva
		, [TaxAmount]				=	b.Tva
		, [TaxCurrencyCode]			=	'RON'
		, [TaxCurrencyAmount]		=	b.Tva
		, [TaxExchangeRate]			=	Cast(1 As Numeric(18, 4))
		, [Name]					=	c.Denumire
		, [Id]						=	a.IdDoc
		, [Tip]			  			=	a.Tip
		, [TipFactura]				=	a.Tip
		, [DataDocReala]			=	a.DataDocReala
		, [Tip_Tert]				=	c.Tip_Tert
		, [LineAccountID]			=	b.Cont
		, [Cod_Fiscal]				=	c.Cod_Fiscal
		, [RegistrationNumber]		=	Space(21)
		, [CodTva]					=	b.CodTva
		, [VIES]					=	Cast(0 As Bit)
		, [IdUnic]		 			=	a.IdUnic
		, [IdLinie]		 			=	b.Id
		, [Firma]                   =   SPACE(3)		
		, [IsTvaIncasare]			=	a.IsTvaIncasare
		, [ProductCode]		 		=	IsNull(RTrim(LTrim(Str(b.IdArticol))), '0')
		, [IdFactura]		 		=	RTrim(Cast(a.IdDoc As Char(10))) + RTrim(Cast(a.IdUnic  As Char(10)))
		, [TipDocument]		 		=	'1.Iesiri'
		, [Total]					=	a.Total
		, [Tiparit]					=	a.BFTiparit
	From Iesiri a 
	Inner Join Conturi c On a.Cont=c.Cont
	Left join IesiriDetaliat b On a.IdDoc=b.IdDoc
	Where
		-- a.Tip Not In('A', 'B', 'C', 'E') And 
		a.DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2
		And IsNull(a.Anulat, 0)=0
		And IsNull(c.Sters, 0)=0
	Union All
	Select
		[InvoiceNo]					=	a.NumarDoc
		, [CustomerID]				=	Space(35)
		, [City]					=	c.Localitate
		, [Country]					=	c.Tara
		, [AccountID]				=	CAST( c.Cont As Char(30))
		, [StandardAccountID]		=	CAST( c.Cont As Char(30))
		, [InvoiceDate]				=	a.DataDoc
		, [InvoiceType]				=	Space(3)
		, [SelfBillingIndicator]	=	Space(3)
		, [DebitCreditIndicator]	=	Case When b.Valoare>=0 Then 'C' Else 'D' End
		, [Quantity]				=	b.Cantitate
		, [UnitPrice]				=	b.PretUnitar
		, [TaxPointDate]			=	a.DataDoc
		, [Description]				=	b.Denumire
		, [Amount]					=	b.Valoare
		, [CurrencyCode]			=	a.CodValuta
		, [CurrencyAmount]			=	b.ValoareValuta
		, [ExchangeRate]			=	a.Curs
		, [TaxType]					=	Space(3)
		, [TaxCode]					=	Space(6)
		, [TaxPercentage]			=	b.ProcTva
		, [TaxBase]					=	b.Valoare
		, [TaxBaseDescription]		=	b.DenumireTva
		, [TaxAmount]				=	b.Tva
		, [TaxCurrencyCode]			=	a.CodValuta
		, [TaxCurrencyAmount]		=	b.TvaValuta
		, [TaxExchangeRate]			=	a.Curs
		, [Name]					=	c.Denumire
		, [Id]						=	a.IdDoc
		, [Tip]			  			=	a.Tip
		, [TipFactura]				=	a.Tip
		, [DataDocReala]			=	a.DataDocReala
		, [Tip_Tert]				=	c.Tip_Tert
		, [LineAccountID]			=	b.Cont
		, [Cod_Fiscal]				=	c.Cod_Fiscal
		, [RegistrationNumber]		=	Space(21)
		, [CodTva]					=	b.CodTva
		, [VIES]					=	Cast(0 As Bit)
		, [IdUnic]		 			=	a.IdUnic
		, [IdLinie]		 			=	b.Id
		, [Firma]                   =   SPACE(3)										
		, [IsTvaIncasare]			=	a.IsTvaIncasare
		, [ProductCode]		 		=	IsNull(RTrim(LTrim(Str(b.IdArticol))), '0')
		, [IdFactura]		 		=	RTrim(Cast(a.IdDoc As Char(10))) + RTrim(Cast(a.IdUnic  As Char(10)))
		, [TipDocument]		 		=	'2.Export'
		, [Total]					=	a.Total
		, [Tiparit]					=	a.BFTiparit
	From Export a 
	Inner Join Conturi c On a.Cont=c.Cont
	Left Join ExportDetaliat b On a.IdDoc=b.IdDoc
	Where
		-- a.Tip Not In('A', 'n') And
		a.DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2
		And IsNull(a.Anulat, 0)=0
		And IsNull(c.Sters, 0)=0
	Order By a.DataDoc, a.NumarDoc, a.Tip, b.Id
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [crsSalesInvoices])

If lcTipDeclaratie = 'C'
	Return
EndIf

*
*
Function _19PurchaseInvoices()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	19.PurchaseInvoices
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
#if .f.
	=================
	PurchaseInvoices
	=================
	Contine informatii despre facturile de cumparare, precum 
		numarul de intrari/facturi, 
		total debit, 
		total credit, 
		informatii despre furnizor, 
		data facturii, 
		termen de plata, 
		liniile din factura, 
		indicatorul privind autofacturarea, codul de taxa etc.
	
	Toate facturile primite de la Furnizori pentru perioada de raportare la nivel de linie de factura. 
	(In D394 se raporteaza la nivel de total facturi per Furnizor/ per cota tva) 
	Vezi: 4. SourceDocuments > 4.1 SalesInvoices [4.2 PurchaseInvoices]>Invoice structure>InvoiceLine		
#endif
*
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select
		  [InvoiceNo]		= a.NumarDoc
		, [SupplierID]		= CAST( '' as Character(35))
		, [City]			= CAST(Isnull(c.Localitate,' ') as Char(40))		
		, [Country]			= c.Tara
		, [AccountID]		= c.Cont
		, [StandardAccountID]	= c.Cont
		, [InvoiceDate]		= a.DataDoc
		, [InvoiceType]		= '380'
		, [SelfBillingIndicator]= '0'
		, [DebitCreditIndicator]= IIF(b.ValoareLei<0, 'C', 'D')
		, [Quantity]		= b.Cantitate
		, [UnitPrice]		= b.PretUnitarLei
		, [TaxPointDate]	= a.DataDoc
		, [Description]		= b.Denumire
		, [Amount]			= b.ValoareLei
		, [CurrencyCode]	= a.codvaluta
		, [CurrencyAmount]	= b.ValoareValuta
		, [ExchangeRate]	= a.curs
		, [TaxType]			= Space(3)
		, [TaxCode]			= Space(6)
		, [TaxPercentage]   = b.ProcTva
		, [TaxBase]			= b.ValoareLei
		, [TaxBaseDescription]= b.DenumireTva
		, [TaxAmount]		= b.TvaLei
		, [TaxCurrencyCode]	= a.codvaluta
		, [TaxCurrencyAmount]=b.TvaValuta
		, [TaxExchangeRate] = a.curs
		, [LineAccountId]	= b.Cont
		, 
		  [Id]				= a.IdDoc
		, [Tip]			  	= a.Tip
		, [TipFactura]		= a.Tip
		/*
							Case
								When a.Tip=''  Then '1'
							    When a.Tip='A' Then '2'
							    When a.Tip='T' Then '3'
							    When a.Tip='U' Then '11'
							    When a.Tip='L' Then '17'
							    When a.Tip='N' Then '90'
							    When a.Tip='E' Then '91'
							    When a.Tip='n' Then '92'
							End
		*/
		, [DataDocReala]	= a.DataDocReala
		, [Furnizor]		= c.Denumire
		, [Vies]			= Cast(0 As Bit)  
		, [Cod_Fiscal]		= c.Cod_Fiscal
		, [RegistrationNumber]=Space(21)
		, [Tip_Tert]		= c.Tip_Tert
		, [ProcTva]			= b.ProcTva
		, [CodTva]			= b.CodTva
		, [Tip_ded]			= b.Tip_Ded
		, [cTip_ded]		= Space(3)
		, [D300_rind]		= Space(5)				
		, [IdUnic]			= a.IdUnic
		, [IdLinie]			= b.Id
		, [Firma]			= Space(3)
		, [IsTvaIncasare]			=	a.IsTvaIncasare
		, [ProductCode]		 		=	IsNull(RTrim(LTrim(Str(b.IdArticol))), '0')
		, [IdFactura]		 		=	RTrim(Cast(a.IdDoc As Char(10))) + RTrim(Cast(a.IdUnic  As Char(10)))
		, [TipDocument]		 		=	'2.Import'
		, [Total]					=	a.TotalLei
		, [TipImpozit]				=	IsNull(c.TipImpozit, '')
	From Import a
	Inner Join Conturi c On a.Cont=c.Cont
	Inner Join ImportDetaliat b on a.IdDoc=b.IdDoc
	Where
		a.Tip Not in( 'A','N','E' )
		And a.DataDoc>=?Data1 And a.DataDoc<=?Data2
		And IsNull(a.Sters, 0)=0
		And IsNull(c.Sters, 0)=0
		And IsNull(b.Sters, 0)=0
	Order By a.DataDoc, a.NumarDoc, a.Tip, b.Id
EndText	
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [crsPurchaseInvoicesImport])
*
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select
		  [InvoiceNo]       = a.NumarDoc
		, [SupplierID]      = Cast('' As Character(35))
		, [City]			= CAST(Isnull(c.Localitate,' ') as Char(40))		
		, [Country]			= c.Tara
		, [AccountID]		= c.Cont
		, [StandardAccountID]	= c.Cont
		, [InvoiceDate]		= a.DataDoc
		, [InvoiceType]		= '380'		
		, [SelfBillingIndicator]= '0'	
		, [DebitCreditIndicator]= IIF(b.Valoare<0, 'C', 'D')
		, [Quantity]		= b.Cantitate
		, [UnitPrice]		= b.PretStoc
		, [TaxPointDate]	= a.DataDoc
		, [Description]		= b.Denumire
		, [Amount]			= b.Valoare
		, [CurrencyCode]	= 'RON'
		, [CurrencyAmount]	= b.Valoare
		, [ExchangeRate]	= Cast( 1 as numeric(10,4))
		, [TaxType]			= Space(3)
		, [TaxCode]			= Space(6)
		, [TaxPercentage]   = b.ProcTva
		, [TaxBase]			= b.Valoare
		, [TaxBaseDescription]= b.DenumireTva
		, [TaxAmount]		= b.Tva
		, [TaxCurrencyCode]	= 'RON'
		, [TaxCurrencyAmount]=b.Tva
		, [TaxExchangeRate] = '1.0000'
		, [LineAccountId]	= b.Cont
		,
		  [Id]				= a.IdDoc
		, [Tip]			  	= 
							Case a.Tip
								When 1	Then ''
								When 2	Then 'A'
								When 3	Then 'T'
								When 4	Then 'G'
								When 5	Then 'F'
								When 6	Then 'P'
								When 7	Then 'I'
								When 8	Then 'B'
								When 9	Then 'C'
								When 10	Then 'e'
								When 11 Then 'U'
								When 12	Then 'H'
								When 13	then 'O'
								When 14	Then 'Z'
								When 15 Then 'Q'
								When 16	Then 'M'
								When 17	Then 'L'
								When 18 Then 'n'
							End
		, [TipFactura]		= -- LTrim(RTrim(Str(a.Tip)))
							Case a.Tip
								When 1	Then ''
								When 2	Then 'A'
								When 3	Then 'T'
								When 4	Then 'G'
								When 5	Then 'F'
								When 6	Then 'P'
								When 7	Then 'I'
								When 8	Then 'B'
								When 9	Then 'C'
								When 10	Then 'e'
								When 11 Then 'U'
								When 12	Then 'H'
								When 13	then 'O'
								When 14	Then 'Z'
								When 15 Then 'Q'
								When 16	Then 'M'
								When 17	Then 'L'
								When 18 Then 'n'
							End
		, [DataDocReala]	= a.DataDocReala
		, [Furnizor]		= c.Denumire
		, [Vies]	    	= Cast(0 As Bit)  
		, [Cod_Fiscal]		= c.Cod_Fiscal
		, [RegistrationNumber]=Space(21)		
		, [Tip_Tert]		= c.Tip_Tert
		, [ProcTva]			= b.ProcTva
		, [CodTva]   		= b.CodTva
		, [Tip_ded]  		= b.Tip_Ded
		, [cTip_ded]    	= Space(3)
		, [D300_rind]		= Space(5)	
	    , [IdUnic]		 	= a.IdUnic
		, [IdLinie]		 	= b.Id	    
 		, [Firma]        	= Space(3)
		, [IsTvaIncasare]			=	a.IsTvaIncasare
		, [ProductCode]		 		=	IsNull(RTrim(LTrim(Str(b.IdArticol))), '0')
		, [IdFactura]		 		=	RTrim(Cast(a.IdDoc As Char(10))) + RTrim(Cast(a.IdUnic  As Char(10)))
		, [TipDocument]		 		=	'1.Intrari'
		, [Total]					=	a.Total
		, [TipImpozit]				=	IsNull(c.TipImpozit, '')
	From Intrari a
	Inner Join Conturi c On a.Cont=c.Cont
	Inner Join IntrariDetaliat b on a.IdDoc=b.IdDoc
	Where
		a.Tip Not In( 2, 8 ) 						-- aici sa vedem ce nu luam   , 13, 14, 15
		And a.DataDoc>=?Data1 And a.DataDoc<=?Data2
		And IsNull(a.Sters, 0)=0
		And IsNull(c.Sters, 0)=0
		And IsNull(b.Sters, 0)=0			
	Order By a.DataDoc, a.NumarDoc, a.Tip, b.Id
EndText

					  						  cTip_Ded=  '50' And TaxPercentage=19 And     cSir_Servicii, '390701',;
