Function _20Payments()
*!*	*__________________________________________________________________________________
*!*	*
*!*	*//	20.Payments
*!*	*__________________________________________________________________________________
*!*	*
*  NU SE RAPORTEAZA IN "Payments"
* 1. 	654* Pierderi din creante 4118 Clienti incerti nefiind efectiv o plata/incasare nu se preia in 
* 2. Întrebare:
*	e) Speta incasarilor de la salariati pentru debite ale acestora catre entitate – retinere salariala
*	(entitatea emite facturi catre salariati, pe care le incaseaza prin retinere salariala)
*	4282 Creante personal 461* Debitori diversi/client
*	Rãspuns 7:
*	În cazul debitelor salariatilor catre societate, re.inute pe statul de platã apar in 4.3 Payments (una sau mai multe înregistrãri), folosind codurile:
*	· PaymentMechanism = „8” - Hold / Sumã re.inutã (re.inere) .i concomitent
*	· PaymentMethod = „03 - Non-Cash” pentru metoda de platã.
*	Detaliile sunt disponibile în Schema SAF-T pentru România, în tabela Nom_Mecanisme_plati.
*	Sumele retinute pe statul de plata pentru a fi platite tertilor (retinere impozite la sursa, sume retinute pentru a fi platite catre executori judecatoresti), nu se declara in sectiunea Payments.
*3. Miscarile între conturi proprii deoarece nu reprezinta plati sau incasari efectuate de catre contribuabil – DAR DACA RAPORTORUL VREA SE POT RAPORTA
*4. Întrebare:
*	Pentru operatiunile de factoring pentru care societatea efectueaza plata catre Banca si nu catre furnizorul care a emis factura, Banca platind factura in numele societa.ii,
*   ce se va declara in Payments, sub-sectiunea Payment Line, campul SupplierID (se va declara codul unic al clientului sau codul unic al Bancii) ?
*	Raspuns 15:
*	In cazul operatiunilor de factoring, in campul SupplierID se declara codul unic al finantatorului (in cazul de fata, banca), precum si contul contabil (AccountID) in care se 
*	evidentiaza datoria fata de acesta. De asemenea, se va declara inchiderea contului de furnizor cu contul in care se evidentiaza datoria catre finantator si in subsectiunea Payments.
* 5. nu se declara diferentele de curs inregistrate la incasarile /platile in valuta ..., efectuate prin banca , exemplu: 411 partener extern =765 diferenta de curs valutar.

Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select
		  [PaymentRefNo]	= IsNull(r.Ndp,'')
		, [TransactionDate] = r.Data
		, [PaymentMethod]	= Space(2)
		, [Description]		= Space(254)
		, [AccountID]		= Space(30)
		, [CustomerID]	    = Space(35)
		, [SupplierID]	    = Space(35)
		, [DebitCreditIndicator]=Space(1)
		, [Amount]			= IsNull(r.Suma,0)
		, [CurrencyCode]	= IsNull(r.Cod_Valuta,'RON')
		, [CurrencyAmount]	= IsNull(r.Suma_Val,0)
		, [ExchangeRate]	= Cast(IsNull(r.Curs,0.0000) As Numeric(18,4))
		, [TaxType]     	= Cast('000' as Character(3))
		, [TaxCode]      	= Cast('000000' as Character(6))
		, [TaxAmount]		= Cast(0 as Numeric(18,2))
		, [TaxCurrencyCode]	= IsNull(r.Cod_Valuta,'RON')
		, [TaxCurrencyAmount]=Cast(0 as Numeric(18,2))
		, [TaxExchangeRate]	= Cast(IsNull(r.Curs,0.0000) as Numeric(18,4))
  		,  			  
		  [Id]		 	 	= r.Id
		, [ParentId]		= r.Id		  
		, [Id_nota]			= r.Id_Nota
		, [Partener]		= Space(75) 	
		, [Document]  		= IsNull(r.Ndp,'')
		, [ContD]	 		= IsNull(r.ContD,'')
		, [ContC]	 		= IsNull(r.ContC,'')
		, [ContD_APB]   	= Space(1)	
		, [ContC_APB]   	= Space(1)				
		, [Explicatie]  	= IsNull(r.Explicatie,'')
		, [Cod_Fiscal]		= Space(20) 	
		, [Tara]			= Space(2)
		, [CUI_2_Comp]		= Space(20) 	
		, [TaxType_CR]  	= Space(3)
		, [TaxCode_CR]  	= Space(6)
		, [Erori]		    = Space(200)
		, [IdRecno]		    = Cast(0 as Numeric(18))
	From Registru r
	Where
		( ( LEFT(r.ContD, 3) In ('162','403','405','413','509','511','512','513','514','519','525','531','541')
			Or 
		    LEFT(r.ContC, 3) In ('162','403','405','413','509','511','512','513','514','519','525','531','541')
		  )
		  OR
		  (
		    ( LEFT(r.ContD, 3)='401' And LEFT(r.ContC, 3)='411') or (LEFT(r.ContD, 3)='411' And LEFT(r.ContC, 3)='401')
		  )
		)  
		And r.Data>=?Data1 And r.Data<=?Data2
		And IsNull(r.Sters, 0)=0
    Order By r.Data, r.Ndp
ENDTEXT
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [crsPayments])

Replace All CurrencyCode    With Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(CurrencyCode,'')))=0,'RON',CurrencyCode) &&& cod valuta
Replace All TaxCurrencyCode With Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(TaxCurrencyCode,'')))=0,'RON',TaxCurrencyCode) &&& cod valuta
*Replace All CurrencyCode    With Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(CurrencyCode,'')))=0 Or Nvl(ExchangeRate,0)=1, 'RON', CurrencyCode) &&& cod valuta
*Replace All TaxCurrencyCode With Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(TaxCurrencyCode,'')))=0,'RON',TaxCurrencyCode) &&& cod valuta
Replace All CurrencyCode    With 'RON' FOR ExchangeRate=1 And Amount = CurrencyAmount
Replace All TaxCurrencyCode With 'RON' FOR ExchangeRate=1 And Amount = CurrencyAmount

Replace All ExchangeRate    With Iif( CurrencyCode='RON', 1.0000,	ExchangeRate ),;
			TaxExchangeRate With Iif( CurrencyCode='RON', 1.0000,	TaxExchangeRate )
	
Update crsPayments Set PaymentMethod= ICase(;
											Left(ContD,3)='531' Or LEFT(ContC,3)='531',		'01',; &&& numerar
											(Left(ContD,3)='411' And LEFT(ContC,3)='401') Or ;
											(Left(ContD,3)='401' And LEFT(ContC,3)='411'),		'02',; &&& compensare
											InList(Left(ContD,3), '512', '519', '162') ;
											Or InList(Left(ContC,3), '512', '519', '162'),	'03',; &&& fara numerar( nu neaparat banca >> 4282 Creante personal 461* Debitori diversi/client )
											Left(ContD,3)='481' Or LEFT(ContD,3)='482',		'03',; &&& fara numerar( 481/482 la ecoaqua clientii incasati in cont sediu CL si transferati la Oltenita)
											Left(ContC,3)='481' Or LEFT(ContC,3)='482',		'03',; &&& fara numerar( 481/482 la ecoaqua clientii incasati in cont sediu CL si transferati la Oltenita)
											Left(ContD,3)='000' Or LEFT(ContC,3)='000',		'98',; &&& Definit de comun acord
											Left(ContD,3)='000' Or LEFT(ContC,3)='000',		'99',; &&& Instrument nedefinit
											Left(ContD,3)='427',								'99',; &&& Instrument nedefinit
											Left(ContD, 4)='654.' And m.NumeDB="ecuaqua",		'99',; &&& Instrument nedefinit
											Left(ContD, 4)='654.' And m.NumeDB="SCECOSALSERVICIIOLTENITASRL",		'99',; &&& Instrument nedefinit
											Left(ContD, 3)='473'  And m.NumeDB="ecuaqua",		'03',; &&& sume curs clarificare
											Left(ContD, 3)='462'  And m.NumeDB="ecuaqua",		'03',; &&& sume curs clarificare
											'??';
  										   ),;
  					   Description  = ICase(;
  					   						Left(ContC,3)='531',							'Plata in numerar'	    ,;
											Left(ContD,3)='531',							'Incasare in numerar'   ,;
	  										(Left(ContD,3)='411' And LEFT(ContC,3)='401')	Or ;
	  										(Left(ContD,3)='401' And LEFT(ContC,3)='411'),	'Compensare parteneri'   ,;
	  										InList(Left(ContC,3), '512', '519', '162'),		'Plata prin banca'	    ,;
	  										InList(Left(ContD,3), '512', '519', '162'),		'Incasare prin banca'   ,;
 	  										InList(Left(ContD,3),'481','482'),				'Incasare prin banca'   ,; 
 	  										InList(Left(ContC,3),'481','482'),				'Incasare prin banca'   ,;
	  										Left(ContD,3)='000',							'Non-cash'		 	    ,;
	  										Left(ContD,3)='000' Or LEFT(ContC,3)='000',		'Definit de comun acord',;
	  										Left(ContD,3)='000' Or LEFT(ContC,3)='000',		'Instrument nedefinit'  ,; 
  											Left(ContD,3)='427',							'Instrument nedefinit'  ,;
											Left(ContD, 4) ='654.' And m.NumeDB="ecuaqua",	'Instrument nedefinit'  ,;
											Left(ContD, 4) ='654.' And m.NumeDB="SCECOSALSERVICIIOLTENITASRL",	'Instrument nedefinit'  ,;
											Left(ContD, 3) ='473'  And m.NumeDB="ecuaqua",	'Incasare prin banca',;
											Left(ContD, 3) ='462'  And m.NumeDB="ecuaqua",	'Incasare prin banca',;
	  										'??';
  										   )

Update p;
Set;
	Cod_Fiscal= Nvl(t.Cod_Fiscal,'');
From crsPayments p;
Inner Join Terti t On t.Cont = p.ContD;
Where;
	PaymentMethod='02'
*	C = 411
Update p;
Set;
	CUI_2_Comp= Nvl(t.Cod_Fiscal,'');
From crsPayments p;
Inner Join Terti t On t.Cont = p.ContC;
Where;
	PaymentMethod='02'

Update crsPayments;
	Set DebitCreditIndicator = ICase(;
								InList( Left(ContD, 3), '401', '403', '404', '408', '462', '409') And Len(ContD)>3,		'C',;
						        InList( Left(ContC, 3), '411', '418', '419', '461')  	     		And Len(ContC)>3,	'D',;
						        InList( Left(ContC, 3), '512','531', '519', '162')		 		And Len(ContC)>3,		'C',;
						        InList( Left(ContD, 3), '512','531', '519', '162')		 		And Len(ContD)>3,		'D',;
						        InList( Left(ContC, 3), '481','482')  					 		And Len(ContD)>3,		'C',;
						        InList( Left(ContD, 3), '481','482')  					 		And Len(ContD)>3,		'D',;
						        ContC_APB='A',																			'C',; &&& incasare  &&& se schimba la compensare
								ContD_APB='P',																			'D',; &&& plata   
								ContD_APB='B' And ContC_APB='B',														'C',; &&& asta ce o trec ???
						        ContD = '654.02' And m.NumeDB="ecuaqua" And Len(ContD)>3,								'D',;
								'?';  
						        	)	
			*   DB    CR     plata/incasare
			*          Activ  incasare   (DebitCreditIndicator=D)
			*   Pasiv		  plata      (DebitCreditIndicator=C)

Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
	Select
		Cont, 
		TaxType, 
		[TaxCode]=Iif( Len(RTrim(LTrim(IsNull(TaxCode,'000000'))))=0, '000000', IsNull(TaxCode,'000000') )
	From Dbo.ConturiTax
	Where
		IsNull(TaxType,'0')<>'0'
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode])

Update CrsPayments;
	Set TaxType   = Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode.TaxType,;
	    TaxCode   = Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode.TaxCode ;
	From Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode;
	Where AllTrim(Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode.Cont) == AllTrim(CrsPayments.ContD) Or ; 
	      AllTrim(Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode.Cont) == AllTrim(CrsPayments.ContC) 
*					
Update CrsPayments;    
	Set TaxType_CR = Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode.TaxType,;
	    TaxCode_CR = Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode.TaxCode ;
	From Conturi_TaxType_TaxCode;
	Where Left(AllTrim(ContC),3)='411' And  ;
	      Left(AllTrim(ContD),3)='401'

		Len(AllTrim(Terti.Cont))=18

EndIf

Select Sum(Iif( DebitCreditIndicator='D', Amount, 00000000.00 )) As Payments_D,; &&& nu e corect - asta e doar catre furnizori / clienti dar poate fi si altfel
	   Sum(Iif( DebitCreditIndicator='C', Amount, 00000000.00 )) As Payments_C ;
From crsPayments;
Into Cursor cGetTotaluricrsPayments

	InList( Left(crsPayments.ContD,3),'401','403','404')  And Len(crsPayments.ContD)>3 And ;
	Left(crsPayments.ContC,3)='411' And Len(crsPayments.ContC)>3 ;
Into Cursor SumaCompensari

	Store '' to cAccountID2, cCustomerID2, cSupplierID2, cDebitCreditIndicator2
	For compensarea_egal_2_plati= 1 to 2 &&& 1 normal , 2-compensare/ SAU EX. 4315-4316 AMBELE CONTURI AU TAXTYPE (CAS SI SANATATE)

\				<PaymentLine>
*\					<LineNumber><<TRANSFORM(lnLineNumber)>></LineNumber>
\					<AccountID><< AllTrim(Iif( compensarea_egal_2_plati= 1, AccountId , cAccountID2 ))>></AccountID>   
\					<CustomerID><<AllTrim(Iif( compensarea_egal_2_plati= 1, CustomerID, cCustomerID2))>></CustomerID> 
\					<SupplierID><<AllTrim(Iif( compensarea_egal_2_plati= 1, SupplierID, cSupplierID2))>></SupplierID> 
\					<DebitCreditIndicator><<Iif( compensarea_egal_2_plati= 1, AllTrim(DebitCreditIndicator), cDebitCreditIndicator2)>></DebitCreditIndicator> 			
\					<PaymentLineAmount>
\						<Amount><<AllTrim(Str(Amount,15,2))>></Amount>
\						<CurrencyCode><<AllTrim(Upper(CurrencyCode))>></CurrencyCode>
\						<CurrencyAmount><<AllTrim(Str(CurrencyAmount,15,2))>></CurrencyAmount>
\						<ExchangeRate><<AllTrim(Str(ExchangeRate,10,4)))>></ExchangeRate>
\					</PaymentLineAmount>
\					<TaxInformation>
\						<TaxType><<AllTrim(TaxType)>></TaxType>
\						<TaxCode><<AllTrim(TaxCode)>></TaxCode>
\						<TaxAmount>
\							<Amount><<AllTrim(Str(TaxAmount,15,2))>></Amount>
\							<CurrencyCode><<AllTrim(Upper(TaxCurrencyCode))>></CurrencyCode>
\							<CurrencyAmount><<AllTrim(Str(TaxCurrencyAmount,15,2))>></CurrencyAmount>
\							<ExchangeRate><<AllTrim(Str(TaxExchangeRate,10,4))>></ExchangeRate>
\						</TaxAmount>	
\					</TaxInformation>
\				</PaymentLine>

		If compensarea_egal_2_plati=1 And ;
		   ( ( INLIST( LEFT(ContD,3),'401','403','404')  And Len(ContD)>3 And LEFT(ContC,3)='411' And Len(ContC)>3;
		     ) Or ; &&& caz 1 :zz= compensare
		     (Len(AllTrim(Nvl(TaxType_CR,'')))=3 And AllTrim(Nvl(TaxType_CR,''))<>'000') ;
		   )
		    &&& caz 2: contD si ContC ambele cu TaxCode
 			*    D     C    suma
			*zz 401   411    15  compensare				CustomerID =411		SupplierID =401
			*** la prima trecere  CustomerID= cCod_Fiscal si SupllierID='0' (Get_CustomerID_Payments/Get_SupllierID_Payments)
			*411, customerid,C  =creditdebitindicator
			*401,  suplierid,D	=creditdebitindicator

			cAccountID2  = Cont_Saft(ContC)
			cSupplierID2 = '0'
			cDebitCreditIndicator2 = 'D'
			cCustomerID2 = Get_SupplierID_Payments( ContD, ContC, CUI_2_Comp, Amount )
			*WAIT WINDOW 'CUI_2_Comp='+CUI_2_Comp+CHR(13)+;
			 			 'ContD='+ContD+CHR(13)+;
			 			 'ContC='+ContC+CHR(13)+;
			 			 'cCustomerID2='+cCustomerID2  
			Select Terti
			Locate For AllTrim(Cod_Fiscal)==AllTrim(cCustomerID2 )
			If Found()
				cCustomerID2 = Terti.RegistrationNumber
			EndIf
			&&& 
			Select SourceDocuments_Payments
		Else
			Exit
		EndIf		
	EndFor 
\			</Payment>
*	
EndScan
	*
\		</Payments>
Set TextMerge To
Set TextMerge Off
AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'20.Payments.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'20.Payments.xml'+[...])
If FORCE_REFRESH
	DoEvents FORCE
EndIf	
EndFunc

Function _21MovementOfGoods()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	21.MovementOfGoods
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
If lcTipDeclaratie = 'C'  And glPreluareDinEstoc = .T.
	*		
	Select 	RecNo() 			As NrCrt				,;
			MovementsL			As NumberOfMovementLines,;
			QuantityRe			As TotalQuantityReceived,;
			QuantityIs			As TotalQuantityIssued	,;
			;
			MovementDa			As MovementDate		 	,;	&&& data document
			MovementTy			As MovementType			,;	&&& Mi?carea exprim? tipul procesului pentru liniile subiacente, de exemplu, produc?ia, vânz?rile, cump?rarea, tabel predefinit este posibil
			DocumentTy			As DocumentType		 	,;  &&& 380-factura ????
			DocumentNu			As DocumentNumber	 	,;
			MovementRe			As MovementReference	,;
			LineNumber									,;
			Cont_SAFT(AccountID) As AccountID			,;
			Cast( Iif( CustomerID<>'0', GetRegistrationNumberByVies(CustomerID, Tara, Tip_Tert, Vies), CustomerID) As C(15)) As CustomerID	,;
			Cast( Iif( SupplierID<>'0', GetRegistrationNumberByVies(SupplierID, Tara, Tip_Tert, Vies), SupplierID) As C(15)) As SupplierID	,;
			ProductCod			As ProductCode			,;
			Quantity									,;
			GetUnitOfMeasure(UnitOfMeas) As UnitOfMeasure,;	
			UOMToUOMPh			As UOMToUOMPhysicalStockConversion,;	
			MovementSu			As MovementSubtype		,;	&&& Validare conform nomenclatorului Nomenclator stocuri(10-Achizitie, 20-productie, 30-vanzare ........ 180-alte tranzactii.
			Partener									 ;
		From MovementOfGoods_estoc;
		Into Cursor MovementOfGoods_Temp
	*
	Select MovementOfGoods
	Zap
	Append From Dbf('MovementOfGoods_Temp')	
	*
EndIf
*
Tabela_Erori_Saft('Stocuri')
*
If RecCount('MovementOfGoods')=0

	Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'21.MovementOfGoods.xml' Additive NoShow		
\		<MovementOfGoods></MovementOfGoods>
	Set TextMerge To
	Set TextMerge Off
	Return
EndIf

Select MovementOfGoods
Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'21.MovementOfGoods.xml' Additive NoShow

\			<TotalQuantityReceived><<AllTrim(Str(TotalQuantityReceived,15,2))>></TotalQuantityReceived>
\			<TotalQuantityIssued><<AllTrim(Str(TotalQuantityIssued,15,2))>></TotalQuantityIssued>
*
Scan &&& FOR RecNo()=1

*
Function _22AssetTransactions()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	22.AssetTransactions
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
If lcTipDeclaratie # "A"
	Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'22.AssetTransactions.xml' Additive NoShow
\		<AssetTransactions/>
	Set TextMerge To
	Set TextMerge Off
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'22.AssetTransactions.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'22.AssetTransactions.xml'+[...])
	Return
EndIf
*
	Select ;
		d.Id;
		, d.Id_mijlocfix 		As IdMF;
		, d.NrInv;
		, d.CodCls;
		, AllTrim(Str(d.NrInv))	As AssetID;  &&& , AllT(Str(d.Id_mijlocfix))	As AssetID
		, d.data				As AssetTransactionDate;
		, 'Contabil' 			As AssetValuationType;
		, 0 					As AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction;
		, 0						As BookValueOnTransaction;  &&& diferente intre amortpl
		, 0			 			As AssetTransactionAmount;  &&& diferente intre valram
		, Nvl(d.Tip,'') 		As TipAmortDet;
		, Cast( '' As Char(2)) 	As NrTipAmortDet;
		, Nvl(d.Nr,'') 			As Nr;
		, d.Data;
		, Nvl(d.Valoare,0)		As Val;
		, Nvl(d.Valoare,0)		As ValActual;		
		, d.Den_amortizari 		As Denumire;
		, Nvl(d.DataIesireConservare,{//}) As DataIesireConservare; 
		, a.ValRam;
		, a.AmortPl; 
		, ant.ValActual			As ValActualAnterior;
		, ant.ValRam			As ValRamAnterior;
		, ant.AmortPl			As AmortPlAnterior;
		, d.TransactionId		As TransactionId;
		, 'AmortDet' As Tabela;
		, Cast( Iif( Nvl(ant.ValActual,0)=0, 0.00, Round( Nvl(d.Valoare,0)/ant.ValActual, 5)) As N(12,5)) As CoefReevBrut;
	From AmortizariDetaliatAn d;
	Left Join Mf_Detalii_An a ;
			On a.IdMf=d.Id_mijlocFix And a.nLuna=Month(d.Data);
	Left Join Mf_Detalii_An ant ;
			On ant.IdMf=d.Id_mijlocFix And ant.nLuna=Month(d.Data)-1;	
	Into Cursor AmortDet ReadWrite 
	*
	*** Selectul de mai sus imi face 2 pozitii in loc de una, si sterg cea nula in transactionId care este asa: "MF-10"
	Delete From AmortDet Where Len(AllTrim(Nvl(TransactionId,'')))=0
	*	
	=MijloaceFixe_ADV('AmortDet','')
	*
	*data1={31/12/2022}
	*** pt. reevaluari care se fac in luna=1 in Mf_Detalii_An nu am luna=12 a anului anterior ( pt. a afla diferenta Amortizat anterior-amortizat total luna reevaluarii),
	*** de aceea mai fac acest select
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select 
			l.nLuna
			, l.nAnul
			, a.*
			From MijloaceFixe m
			Inner Join Amortizari a On a.IdMF=m.ID		 And a.Sters=0
			Inner Join LunaAnul l 	On l.Id=a.Id_luna_an And l.sters=0
			Where  
					m.Sters=0 
				And l.nAnul=Year(?Data1)-1
				And l.nLuna=12
			Order By NrInv	
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12])
	*
	=MijloaceFixe_ADV('Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12','')
	DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'AssetTransaction','Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12'
	*
	Update AmortDet ;
		Set ValActualAnterior	= Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12.ValActual,;
			ValRamAnterior		= Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12.ValRam,;
			AmortPlAnterior 	= Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12.AmortPl;
	From Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12;
	Where;
		Mf_Detalii_An_anterior_luna_12.IdMF = AmortDet.IdMF;
		And Month(AmortDet.Data)=1

	*** pt. 'Casare','Vanzare' si amortizate demult >>> nu mai exista in anul curent in amortizari/decembrie anterior acestea sint cu
	*** AmortDet.ValRam null sau empty si le iau din MijloaceFixe:
*!*		mySQLExec('Select * From MijloaceFixe','MijloaceFixe')
*!*		*
*!*		=MijloaceFixe_ADV('MijloaceFixe','')
*!*		DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'AssetTransactions','MijloaceFixe'

	*
	Update AmortDet;
	Set;
		ValRam				= MijloaceFixe.ValRam,;
		AmortPl				= MijloaceFixe.AmortPl,;
		ValActualAnterior	= MijloaceFixe.ValActual,;
		ValRamAnterior   	= MijloaceFixe.ValRam,;
		AmortPlAnterior		= MijloaceFixe.AmortPl;
	From MijloaceFixe;
	Where ;
			Tabela='AmortDet';
		And	(IsNullOrEmpty(AmortDet.ValRam) Or IsNullOrEmpty(AmortDet.AmortPl));
		And	InList( AmortDet.TipAmortDet, 'Casare', 'Vanzare');
		And	AmortDet.IdMF = MijloaceFixe.Id
	*
	Update AmortDet;
	Set;
		ValRam				= Nvl(ValRam	,0),;
		AmortPl				= Nvl(AmortPl	,0),;
		ValActualAnterior	= Nvl(ValActual	,0),;
		ValRamAnterior   	= Nvl(ValRam	,0),;
		AmortPlAnterior		= Nvl(AmortPl	,0) ;
	Where ;
			Tabela='AmortDet';
		And	Atc('Reevaluare', AmortDet.TipAmortDet)>0
	*		
	*** Trec ca pozitii separate modificarile din AmortizariDetaliat ( casare, modernizare, reeevaluare, etc....)
	Select *,;
			'Mf_Detalii_An'		As Tabela;
		,	Cast (0 As N(12,5))	As CoefReevBrut ;
	From Mf_Detalii_An;
	Where;
		An_Ant='nu';
	Order By;
		AssetId, DataOper;
	Into Cursor Mf_Assets_T ReadWrite
	*
	=MijloaceFixe_ADV('mf_Assets_T','')
	*
	Select Mf_Assets_T 
	Replace All AssetTransactionDate With DataOper
	Append From Dbf('AmortDet')
	*Browse Last
	***
	Replace All NrTipAmortDet With ;
					ICase(  TipAmortDet='Achizitie'						, '10',;  	&&&	10	Achizitie
							TipAmortDet='Vanzare'						, '20',;	&&&	20	Vânzare
							TipAmortDet='Conservare'					, '130',;	&&&	130	Alte tranzactii
							TipAmortDet='Amortizare'					, '30',;	&&&	30	Amortizare
							TipAmortDet=' '								, '30',;	&&&	30	Amortizare  aici se opresc toate TipAmortDet=''
							TipAmortDet='Casare'						, '50',;	&&&	50	Casare mijloace fixe
							Atc('Reevaluare', TipAmortDet)>0 And Val<0  , '60',;	&&&	60	Reevaluare negativa
							TipAmortDet='Modernizare' Or Atc('Reevaluare', TipAmortDet)>0 And Val>0, '70',;	&&&	70	Reevaluare pozitiva
							TipAmortDet=' '								, '40',;	&&&	40	Transfer intern
							TipAmortDet=' '								, '80',;	&&&	80	Plus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '								, '90',;	&&&	90	Minus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '								, '100',;	&&&	100	Ajustare de valoare negativa
							TipAmortDet=' '								, '110',;	&&&	110	Reversare ajustare de valoare
							TipAmortDet=' '								, '120';	&&&	120	Bunuri acordate cu titlu gratuit
						)	  					
							
	***	AssetTransactionType -- 'Casare','Conservare','Modernizare','Reevaluare','Reevaluare Net','Vanzare', ....	
	*
	Replace All; 
		AssetTransactionID   With Iif( Upper(m.NumeDB) = "AVICOLADRAGOSVODA",;
									   AssetTransactionID,;
									   Iif(InList(TipAmortDet,'Amortizare', 'Achizitie' ),'AM','AMD')+AllTrim(Str(Id));
									  ),;
		AssetTransactionType With NrTipAmortDet,;
		Description			 With TipAmortDet;
		In mf_Assets_T
	*
	*** Achizitie(Amortizare.Val) + modernizare pina la data pozitiei
	Replace all AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction With ;
					ICase(  TipAmortDet='Achizitie'	  , Val,; 
							TipAmortDet='Vanzare'	  , 0.00,;
							TipAmortDet='Conservare'  , 0.00,;	&&&	130	Alte tranzactii
							TipAmortDet='Amortizare'  , 0.00,;
							TipAmortDet='Modernizare' , Val,;
							TipAmortDet='Casare'	  , 0.00,;
							Atc('Reevaluare', TipAmortDet)>0, Val,;
							TipAmortDet=' '			  ,  Val,;	&&&	30	Amortizare  aici se opresc toate TipAmortDet=''
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	40	Transfer intern
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	80	Plus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	90	Minus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	100	Ajustare de valoare negativa
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	110	Reversare ajustare de valoare
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	120	Bunuri acordate cu titlu gratuit
							0.00;
						)	  				
	*
	Replace all BookValueOnTransaction With ;
					ICase(  TipAmortDet='Achizitie'	  , Val,; 
							TipAmortDet='Vanzare'	  , -ValRam,;
							TipAmortDet='Conservare'  , 0.00,;	&&&	130	Alte tranzactii - AICI NU SINT SIGUR
							TipAmortDet='Amortizare'  , -AmortLuna,;
							TipAmortDet='Modernizare' , Val,;
							TipAmortDet='Casare'	  , -ValRam,;
							TipAmortDet='Reevaluare Brut', ValActual - ValActualAnterior,;	&&&	 ???? am dubii aici
							TipAmortDet='Reevaluare Net' , ValActual - ValRamAnterior,;	&&&	identic cu  ValActual - (ValActualAnterior - AmortPl)  idem AssetTransactionAmount 
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	30	Amortizare  aici se opresc toate TipAmortDet=''
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	40	Transfer intern
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	80	Plus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	90	Minus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	100	Ajustare de valoare negativa
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	110	Reversare ajustare de valoare
							TipAmortDet=' ' 		  , 0.00,;	&&&	120	Bunuri acordate cu titlu gratuit
							0.00;
						)
	*
	Replace all AssetTransactionAmount With ;
					ICase(  TipAmortDet='Achizitie'	  , Val,; 
							TipAmortDet='Vanzare'	  , Val-ValRam,;
							TipAmortDet='Conservare'  , 0.00,;	&&&	130	Alte tranzactii
							TipAmortDet='Amortizare'  , -AmortLuna,;
							TipAmortDet='Modernizare' , Val,;
							TipAmortDet='Casare'	  , -ValRam,;
							TipAmortDet='Reevaluare Brut', Round(ValRam * CoefReevBrut,2) - ValRam,; &&&	identic cu  ValActual - (ValActualAnterior - AmortPl)
							TipAmortDet='Reevaluare Net' , ValActual - ValRamAnterior,;	&&&	identic cu  ValActual - (ValActualAnterior - AmortPl)
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	30	Amortizare  aici se opresc toate TipAmortDet=''
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	40	Transfer intern
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	80	Plus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	90	Minus de inventar
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	100	Ajustare de valoare negativa
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	110	Reversare ajustare de valoare
							TipAmortDet=' '			  , 0.00,;	&&&	120	Bunuri acordate cu titlu gratuit
							0.00;
						)		
	*!*	Number of asset transactions				Numarul de linii de miscare în perioada selectata
	*!*	AssetTransactionID		ok	TipAmortDet		Identificarea unica a tranzactiei - 'AM'+ID (Amortizari) sau 'AMD'+id (AmortizariDetaliat)
	*!*	AssetID					ok	IdMF			Identificator unic al activului	
	*!*	AssetTransactionType	ok	NrTipAmortDet	Codul pentru tipul tranzactiei
	*!*	Description				ok	TipAmortDet		Descrierea tipului tranzactiei
	*!*	AssetTransactionDate	ok 	Data			Data înregistrarii tipului de tranzactie	
	*!*	TransactionID			?????????			numãrul notei contabile din Registrul Jurnal (numãrul înregistrãrii). Poate contine mai multe niveluri diferite pentru a 
	***												identifica tranzac?ia. Acesta ar putea include centre de cost, cum ar fi societatea, divizia, regiunea, grupul ?i sucursala /departamentul.	
	*!*	AssetValuationType		ok	'Contabila'		Descrie scopul raportarii f.i. comerciale, fiscale în tara 1, fiscale în tara 2 etc.
	*!*	AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction	....  Costul de achizitie si/sau de productie a tranzactiei cu activele aferente la data tranzac?iei 
														  *Pentru alte tranzactii se raporteaza zero.*
	*!*	BookValueOnTransaction	....				Valoarea contabila a tranzactiei cu activele respective la data tranzactiei
													* reevaluare - cresterea/scaderea de valoare contabila
													* iesire     - valoarea neta contabila scoasa din gestiune
													* amortizare - valoarea amortizarii inregistrate, etc.
													* Scaderile de valoare se raporteaza ca sume negative. 
													* (valoarea initiala- 90.000 - 19.500 -valoarea cumulata a amortizarii) (sold initial 2131 - sold initial ct 281)
													*							70.500
	*!*	AssetTransactionAmount	....				Valoarea neta a tranzactiei cu activele respective, de exemplu venitul net din vânzare
													* valoarea NETÃ a tranzactiei cu activul / activele respective (de exemplu venitul net realizat din vânzarea mijlocului fix ,
													* , valoarea netã, fãrã TVA, cresterea neta sau scãdere neta de valoare înregistratã la reevaluare, etc.).
													* castig sau pierdere din vanzare, crestere neta sau scadere neta de valoare inregistrata la reevaluare, etc.. 
													* In cazul scaderilor de valoare, se raporteaza sume negative

	*!*	Va rugam sa ne asigurati suport pentru incadrarea la randurile: 
	***  SD.AT.16  AcquisitionAndProductionCostOnTransaction,
	***  SD.AT.17  BookValueOnTransaction,
	***  SD.AT.18  AssetTransactionAmount,  pentru urmatoarele exemple:
	*!*	intrebare 	A. Modernizare mijloc fix existent, prin punere in functiune din contul de investitii in curs, 10.000 lei
	*!*		Rasp.	A. SD.AT.16: 10.000 lei, SD.AT.17: 10.000 lei, SD.AT.18 :10.000 lei
	*!*	intrebare    	B. Inregistrare mijloc fix nou, prin punere in functiune din contul de investitii in curs 50.000 lei
	*!*		Rasp.		B. SD.AT.16: 50.000 lei, SD.AT.17: 50.000 lei, SD.AT.18: 50.000 lei
	*!*	intrebare	C. Achizitie materiale si servicii pentru realizarea unui proiect de investitii (intrari in contul de investitii in curs) 100.000 lei
	*!*		Rasp.	C. Nu se declara in subsectiunea Asset Transactions
	*!*	intrebare		D. Casare mijloc fix 15.000 lei valoare bruta, 10.000 lei amortizare, 5.000 lei valoare neta
	*!*		Rasp.	   	D. SD.AT.16: 0, SD.AT.17 - (5.000 lei) - valoarea neta contabila, SD.AT.18 - (5.000 lei) - pierderea din casare
	*!*	intrebare	E. Vanzare mijloc fix 20.000 lei valoare bruta, 19.000 lei amortizare, 1.000 lei valoare neta, venit din vanzare 2.500 lei
	*!*		Rasp.	E. SD.AT.16: 0, SD.AT.17 - (1.000 lei) - valoarea neta contabila, SD.AT.18 - 1.500 lei - castigul din vanzare
	*	
	*!* intrebare  F. Reevaluare mijloc fix :valoare initiala 10.000 lei valoare bruta, 9.000 lei amortizare, 1.000 lei valoare neta   (REEVALUARE BRUTA)
	*!*									   valoare reevaluata 11.000 lei valoare bruta, 9.900 lei amortizare, 1.100 lei valoare neta   (REEVALUARE BRUTA)
	*!*		Rasp.  F. Presupunand ca dupa reevaluare valoarea= 11.000 lei si amortizarea= 9.900 lei:
	*				  SD.AT.16 -    0 lei 
	*				  SD.AT.17 - 1000 lei - la reevaluare se raporteaza cresterea de valoare contabila (bruta sau neta);  aici avem o crestere a valorii brute si a amortizarii recalculate) ,
	*				  SD.AT.18 -  100 lei - crestere neta de val. înregistratã urmare reevaluarii.		****************************************************************************************************************************
	* reevaluat brut  ?????? in teste							val. mf                amortizat            ramas de amort.
	*					nrinv:62	trans ex.	31.08.2023		 3192.44				 598.59			       2593.85	  66.51
	*											30.09.2023		 3200   	reev. brut	 666.68	  -68.09       2533.32    66.51 ( a ramas asa dar A CALCULAT CU =60.53 !!!) 66.51-60.53=5.98
	*											31.10.2023       3200                    733.35                2466.65    66.67 
	*								 eu am pus      SD.AT.16=    0 
	*  												SD.AT.17= 3200-3192.44			= 7.56 
	*														  reev.brut =ValActual - ValActualAnterior  ( 3200-3192.44 =   7.56 ) (se modifica cu un coeficient si continua) 
	*												SD.AT.18= reev.brut =COEF * ValRam - ValRam		(1.00237*2533.32 - 2533.32 = 6.00) diferenta Ramas de amortizaT INAINTE SI DUPA REEV
	*	val			valactual	valram	 amortluna	amortpl	coef RB	       val Reac  ValRam Reac		Dif Reev_Val	Dif ValRam = VALOARE CONTABILA RAMASA									
	*	3,192.44	3,200.00	2,533.32	66.51	666.68	1.00237		    3200	  2539.32			7.56	        6.00
    *														3200/3192.44			 1.00237*2533.32					2539.32-2533.32=6		
	****************************************************************************************************************************
	* reevaluare net  ok asa ( ca in ex. fisier xls de la anaf)
	* 					nrinv:63	trans ex.	31.08.2023		 43193.28				 5399.16		       37794.12   
	*											30.09.2023		 44000 		reev.net	    0.00	   	       44000
	*											31.10.2023       44000	                 1073.17               42926.83    
	*								 eu am pus      SD.AT.16=    0 
	*  												SD.AT.17= 44000-(43193.28-5399.16) = 6205.88  vers. anaf fisier xls
	*														  44000 - 37794.12		   = 6205.88  vers. Costel
	*														  reev.net 	=ValActual - ValRamAnterior 	= 6205.88 (se anuleaza amortizarea si  incepe de la zero)
	*												SD.AT.18= reev.net  =ValActual - ValRamAnterior  	= 6205.88 (se anuleaza amortizarea si  incepe de la zero)
	*
	*Teoretic ar trebui sa apara diferenta neta de valoare (adica diferenta MF-diferenta amort), nu stiu cum s-a operat reevaluarea in contabilitate .
	*Deci ori treci diferenta neta, ori treci 2 tranzactii: +dif.MF si - dif.amortizare
	*La final, valoarea ramasa/neamortizata trebuie sa "bata" cu valoarea initiala ramasa/neamortizata + total valoare tranzactii din an (cresterile cu + si scaderile/amortizarile cu -)
	*In majoritatea cazurilor val."tranzactiilor" este negativa ... sunt putine "intrari" de valoare.
	*														
	***	intrebare	Amortizare lunara 123 lei
	***			Rasp.	SD.AT.16: 0, SD.AT.17 -(123) lei amortizare, SD.AT.18 -(123) lei amortizare.
	*
	Replace All Description With Elimin_Dia(Description) In mf_Assets_T
	*
	Select AssetTransactions 
	Zap
	Append From Dbf('mf_Assets_T') 
	Replace All NrCrt With RecNo()

	*--	25.06.2024
		** verific amortizari lunare cu balanta
		Select;
			Cont_am As Cont,;
			Sum(-BookValueOnTransaction) As Amort_luna,;
			NAnul,;
			NLuna As Luna;
		From mf_Assets_T;
		Where AllTrim(AssetTransactionType)='30';
		Group By Cont_am, NAnul, NLuna;
		Into Cursor Amortizari_Lunare_Saft
			
		*Browse Last
	*data1={01/01/2023}
		Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
			Select
				Cont_C 		As Cont,
				Sum(Suma) 	As Amort_luna,
				Month(Data) As Luna
			From Registru
			Where
				Sters=0
				And Year(Data) = Year(?Data1)
				And Left( Cont_C, 3)= '281'
			Group By Cont_C, Month(Data)
		EndText
		mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Amortizari_Lunare_Balanta])
		*
		Select 	l.Luna			As Saft_Luna	,;
				l.Cont	 		As Saft_Cont	,;
				l.Amort_Luna 	As Saft_Amort	,;
				b.Luna			As Bal_Luna		,;			
				b.Cont	 		As Bal_Cont		,;
				b.Amort_Luna 	As Bal_Amort	,;
				Cast( Nvl(l.Amort_Luna,0)  - Nvl(b.Amort_Luna,0) As n(12,2)) As Dif_Amort;
			From Amortizari_Lunare_Saft l;
			Full Outer Join Amortizari_Lunare_Balanta b	;
				On l.Cont = b.Cont And l.Luna=b.Luna;
			Into Cursor Verif_mf_amortizari_lunare	
		*
		If RecCount('Verif_MF_amortizari_lunare')>0
			Copy To lcDirectorSAFT + 'Verif_MF_amortizari_lunare' type xls
		EndIf
		**********************************************************************
		
		*** verific iesiri lunare 
	*data1={01/01/2023}
		Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
			Select
				Cont_C 		As Cont,
				Sum(Suma) 	As Iesiri,
				Month(Data) As Luna
			From Registru
			Where
				Sters=0
				And Year(Data) = Year(?Data1)
				And Left( Cont_C, 2) In ('20','21')
			Group By Cont_C, Month(Data)
		EndText
		mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Iesiri_MF_Lunare_Balanta])
		
		Select;
			m.Luna  As Saft_Luna,;
			m.Cont_MF								As Saft_Cont,;
			m.RulajCr21x							As Saft_RulajCr21x,;
			g.Luna As Bal_Luna,;
			Cast(g.Cont As C(20))					As Bal_Cont,;
			Nvl( g.Iesiri,0)						As Bal_RulajCr21x,;
			Nvl( m.RulajCr21x,0) - Nvl(g.Iesiri,0)	As Dif_RulajC;
		From (;
				Select;
					m.Cont	 						As Cont_MF,;
					Sum(m.ValActual)				As RulajCr21x,;
					Month(t.AssetTransactionDate) 	As Luna;  
				From mf_Assets_T t;
				Left Join MF_unice m on t.IdMF = m.IdMF;
				Where InList( AllTrim(t.AssetTransactionType), '20', '50');
				Group By t.AssetTransactionDate;
			 ) m ;
		Full Outer Join Iesiri_MF_Lunare_Balanta g ;
			On g.Cont = m.Cont_MF And m.Luna=g.Luna;	
		Into Cursor Verif_MF_21xx_RulajC_Iesiri_Lunare ReadWrite
		*
		If RecCount('Verif_MF_21xx_RulajC_Iesiri_Lunare')>0
			Copy To lcDirectorSAFT + 'Verif_MF_21xx_RulajC_Iesiri_Lunare' type xls
		EndIf
*-- 25.06.2024		
	*
	Select AssetTransactions 
	*
	Set TextMerge On To lcDirectorSAFT+'22.AssetTransactions.xml' Additive NoShow

\		<AssetTransactions>
\			<NumberOfAssetTransactions><<AllTrim(Str(RecCount('AssetTransactions')))>></NumberOfAssetTransactions>

	Scan

\				<AssetTransactionType><<AllTrim(AssetTransactionType)>></AssetTransactionType>

\				<TransactionID><<AllTrim(TransactionID)>></TransactionID>
\				<AssetTransactionValuations>
\					<AssetTransactionValuation>
\						<AssetValuationType><<ALLTRIM(AssetValuationType)>></AssetValuationType>
\						<AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction><<AllTrim(Str(AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction))>></AcquisitionAndProductionCostsOnTransaction>

\						<AssetTransactionAmount><<AllTrim(Str(AssetTransactionAmount))>></AssetTransactionAmount>
\					</AssetTransactionValuation>
\				</AssetTransactionValuations>
\			</AssetTransaction>
*
	EndScan
*
\		</AssetTransactions>

	Set TextMerge To
	Set TextMerge Off
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ lcDirectorSAFT+'22.AssetTransactions.xml' ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : Generare XML : " + [ ]+lcDirectorSAFT+'22.AssetTransactions.xml'+[...])

Function _16GeneralLedgerEntries()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	16.GeneralLedgerEntries
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
__Consola_Timing(Program() + [16.GeneralLedgerEntries...])

Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
Select
	--	<Journal_Structure>
		[JournalId]				=	r.Fel_D
	,	[Description]			=	'Registru jurnal'
	,	[Type]					=	r.Fel_D
	--		-	<Transaction_Structure>
	,	[TransactionId]			=	RTrim(Cast(r.Id As Char(10)))+' - '+RTrim(r.Fel_D)+' - '+RTrim(Cast(r.Id_Nota As Char(10)))
	,	[Period]				=	Month(r.Data)
	,	[PeriodYear]			=	Year(r.Data)
	,	[TransactionDate]		=	r.Data
	,	[TransactionDescription]=	IsNull(r.Explicatie, '')
	,	[SystemEntryDate]		=	r.Data
	,	[GLPostingDate]			=	r.Data
	-- ,	[CustomerID]			=	Cast('' As Char(35))
	-- ,	[SupplierID]			=	Cast('' As Char(35))
	--		-	</Transaction_Structure>	
	--	</Journal_Structure>
	,	[DataDocReala]			=	r.Data
	,	[NDP] = IsNull(r.NDP, '')
	,	r.ContD
	,	r.ContC
	,	r.Suma
	,	r.Cod_Valuta
	,	r.Curs
	,	r.Suma_Val
	,	[Cod_Fiscal]			=	Cast('' As Char(15))
	,	[Tara]					=	Space(2)
	,	[Tip_Tert]				=	Space(1)
	,	[Vies]					=	Cast(0 As Bit)
	,	[StatusTvaIncasare]		=	Cast(0 As Bit)
	,	[TipFactura]			=	Space(1)
	,	[IdTva]					= r.IdTva
	,	[CodTva]				=	Space(5)
	, 	[DenumireTva]			=	Space(254)
	,	[TaxBaseDescription]	=	Space(254)	
	, 	[ProcTva]				=	Cast( 0 As Numeric(2))
	, 	[CeCont]				=	Space(20) 
	,	[TaxDCA]				=	Space(3)
	,	[TaxType]				=	Space(3)
	,	[TaxCode]				=	Space(6)
	,	[TaxBase]				=	Cast(0 As Numeric(14,2))
	,	[TaxAmount]				=	Cast(0 As Numeric(14,2))
	,	[TipTva]				=	Space(5)
	,	[IdTva_9]				= r.IdTva
	,	[CodTva_9]				=	Space(5)
	, 	[DenumireTva_9]			=	Space(254)
	,	[TaxBaseDescription_9]	=	Space(254)	
	, 	[ProcTva_9]				=	Cast( 0 As Numeric(2))
	, 	[CeCont_9]				=	Space(20) 
	,	[TaxDCA_9]				=	Space(3)
	,	[TaxType_9]				=	Space(3)
	,	[TaxCode_9]				=	Space(6)
	,	[TaxBase_9]				=	Cast(0 As Numeric(14,2))
	,	[TaxAmount_9]			=	Cast(0 As Numeric(14,2))
	,	[TipTva_9]				=	Space(5)
	,	[IdTva_5]				= r.IdTva
	,	[CodTva_5]				=	Space(5)
	, 	[DenumireTva_5]			=	Space(254)
	,	[TaxBaseDescription_5]	=	Space(254)		
	, 	[ProcTva_5]				=	Cast( 0 As Numeric(2))
	, 	[CeCont_5]				=	Space(20) 
	,	[TaxDCA_5]				=	Space(3)
	,	[TaxType_5]				=	Space(3)
	,	[TaxCode_5]				=	Space(6)
	,	[TaxBase_5]				=	Cast(0 As Numeric(14,2))
	,	[TaxAmount_5]			=	Cast(0 As Numeric(14,2))
	,	[TipTva_5]				=	Space(5)
	,	r.IdCategorie
	,	r.Categorie
	,	r.PlanB
	,	r.CapitolB
	,	r.ArticolB
	,	[Tip_Ded]				= Space(4)
	,	[IsTvaIncasare]			= Cast(0 As bit)
	,	[Tranzactia]			= r.ID
	,	[Id_Nota]				= r.ID_Nota
	,	[IdFactura]				= Cast(0 As Int)
From Registru r
-- Left Join Intrari i On i.IdUnic = r.Id_Nota
-- Inner Join IntrariDetaliat d On d.IdDoc = i.IdDoc
Where
	Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2
	And IsNull(r.Sters, 0) = 0
	-- And IsNull(i.Sters, 0) = 0
	And IsNull(ContD, '') <> ''
	And IsNull(ContC, '') <> ''
EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [cGeneralLedgerEntries])
*
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ cursor cGeneralLedgerEntries])
Index On JournalID Tag JournalID
Index On CodTva Tag CodTva
Index On TransactionDescription Tag TDescript
Index On TransactionDate Tag TDate
Index On Tranzactia Tag Tranzactia
Index On Id_Nota Tag Id_Nota
Index On NDP Tag NDP
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Indexare cGeneralLedgerEntries])
*

*Replace All Cod_Valuta With Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Cod_Valuta,'')))=0 Or Nvl(Curs, 0)=1, 'RON', Cod_Valuta) 					&& cod valuta
Replace All Cod_Valuta WITH 'RON' FOR Curs = 1 And Suma = Suma_Val															&& 02.10.2024

Update cGeneralLedgerEntries Set TransactionID = AllTrim(TransactionId) + '-Nr.'+AllTrim(NDP) Where Not IsNullOrEmpty(NDP)	&& Vicos.18.07.2023
*
DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'GeneralLedgerEntries'
DO Saft_Ecosal_Preluare_Dbf     WITH 'GeneralLedgerEntries'
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [DO Saft_Ecoaqua_Preluare_All_DB WITH 'GeneralLedgerEntries'])

*** nic 08.03.2024
Replace;
	Curs		With Null,;
	Suma_Val	With Null,;
	Cod_Valuta	With Null;
For;
		Suma=Suma_Val;
	And	JournalId='Compensari';
In cGeneralLedgerEntries 							 
*
Update r;
Set;
		Cod_Fiscal			= t.Cod_Fiscal;
	,	Tara				= t.Tara;
	,	Tip_Tert			= t.Tip_Tert;
	,	Vies				= t.Vies;
	,	StatusTvaIncasare	= t.StatusTvaIncasare;
From cGeneralLedgerEntries r;
	Left Join Terti t On t.Cont=r.ContD;
Where;
	Not IsNullOrEmpty(t.Cod_Fiscal)
*
Update r;
Set;
	Cod_Fiscal			= t.Cod_Fiscal;
	, Tara				= t.Tara;
	, Tip_Tert			= t.Tip_Tert;
	, Vies				= t.Vies;
	, StatusTvaIncasare	= t.StatusTvaIncasare;
From cGeneralLedgerEntries r;
	Left Join Terti t On t.Cont=r.ContC;
Where;
	Not IsNullOrEmpty(t.Cod_Fiscal)
*
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Sync cu Terti'])

	
*-------------------------------------------------------
*	Vicos.28.07.2023
*	La achizitii nedeductibile se conta 6022 la 4426 si nu stia Furnizorul

Update r;
Set;
		Cod_Fiscal		= t.Cod_Fiscal;
	,	Tara				= t.Tara;
	,	Tip_Tert			= t.Tip_Tert;
	,	Vies				= t.Vies;
	,	StatusTvaIncasare = t.StatusTvaIncasare;
From cGeneralLedgerEntries r;
	Inner Join cGeneralLedgerEntries t On t.Id_Nota = r.Id_Nota;
Where;
		r.JournalID In('Intrari', 'Import');
	And	IsNullOrEmpty(r.Cod_Fiscal);
	And	Not IsNullOrEmpty(t.Cod_Fiscal);
	And	r.ContC = '4426'
	
*	Vicos.28.07.2023
*-------------------------------------------------------
*
&&AUTOFACTURA.19.09.2023
*
CF_AutoFactura = ICAS.oSoc.CodFiscal
*
*Update cGeneralLedgerEntries;
Set;
	Cod_Fiscal = CF_AutoFactura, Tara='RO', Tip_Tert='1', Vies=.T., StatusTvaIncasare=.F., DataDocReala=TransactionDate; 
Where;
	JournalID ='Iesiri';
	And IsNullOrEmpty(Cod_Fiscal);
	And ContC = '4427';
	And Atc('autofactura', lower(TransactionDescription))>0
Update g;
Set;
		Cod_Fiscal			= t.Cod_Fiscal;
	,	Tara				= t.Tara;
	,	Tip_Tert			= t.Tip_Tert;
	,	Vies				= t.Vies;
	,	StatusTvaIncasare	= t.StatusTvaIncasare;
	,	DataDocReala		= TransactionDate; 
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join Terti t On t.Cod_Fiscal=g.Cod_Fiscal;
Where;
		g.JournalID ='Iesiri';
	And	IsNullOrEmpty(g.Cod_Fiscal);
	And	g.ContC = '4427';
	And	Atc('autofactura', Lower(g.TransactionDescription))>0
*	
&&AUTOFACTURA.19.09.2023
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Vicos.28.07.2023: La achizitii nedeductibile se conta 6022 la 4426 si nu stia Furnizorul'])

*	Achitari facturi
*
Text To lcSQL_LEI NoShow TextMerge
	Select
			r.Id_Nota
		,	r.Ndp
		,	r.Data
		,	n.Suma
		,	r.Cod_Valuta
		,	r.Curs
		,	r.Explicatie
		,	r.Fel_D
	FROM REGISTRU r
		JOIN NOTEFACTURI n ON r.Id_Nota=n.IdNota
	WHERE
		n.IdFactura=?lnIdUnicFactura
		AND Not CONTAINS(r.Fel_D, 'TVA')
		-- AND n.IsStorno = 0
EndText
Text To lcSQL_Valuta NoShow TextMerge
	Select
			r.Id_Nota
		,	Ndp
		,	r.Data
		,	n.Suma
		,	r.Cod_Valuta
		,	r.Curs
		,	r.Explicatie
		,	r.Fel_D
	From REGISTRU r
		JOIN NOTEFACTURIVALUTA n ON r.Id_Nota=n.IdNota
	WHERE
			n.IdFactura=?lnIdUnicFactura
		AND Not CONTAINS(r.Fel_D, 'TVA')
		-- AND n.IsStorno=0
EndText
*** nic 30.03.2024
*data1={01/02/2024}
*data2={29/02/2024}
*
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge

	Declare @Data1 date = ?Data1;
	Declare @Data2 date = ?Data2;

	Select
			n.IdNota
		,	n.Data
		,	[Suma]	= Sum(n.Suma)
		,	[Tva24]	= Sum(n.Tva24)
		,	[Tva9]	= Sum(n.Tva9)
		,	[Tva5]	= Sum(n.Tva5)
		,	1 As IsTvaIncasare
		,	a.ProcTva
		,	a.CodTva
		,	a.DenumireTva
		,	[Baza_Tva_Factura]	= Sum(a.Baza_Tva)
		,	[Tva_Factura]		= Sum(a.Tva)
		,	[Total_Factura]		= Sum(a.Total)
		,	[Tip_Ded]			= a.Tip_Ded
		,	[Tabela]			= a.Tabela
		,	[IdFactura]			= a.IdUnic
		,	[NDP]				= a.NDP
	From NoteFacturi n
	Inner Join
			(
			Select
					[IdUnic]		= i.IdUnic
				,	[ProcTva]		= d.ProcTva
				,	[CodTva]		= d.CodTva
				,	[DenumireTva]	= d.DenumireTva
				,	[Baza_Tva]		= Sum(d.Valoare)
				,	[Tva]			= Sum(d.Tva)
				,	[Total]			= Sum(d.Total)
				,	[Tip_Ded]		= Case When IsNull(d.Tip_Ded, '') = '' Then '' When d.Tip_Ded = '50' Then 'N050' When d.Tip_Ded = '100' Then 'N100' End
				,	[Tabela]		= 'Intrari'
				,	[NDP]			= i.NumarDOC
			From Intrari i
				Join IntrariDetaliat d On d.IdDoc = i.IdDoc
			Where
				IsNull(i.IsTvaIncasare,0)	=	1
				And DataDoc					<=	@Data2
				And IsNull(i.Sters,0)		=	0
				And IsNull(d.Sters,0)		=	0
			Group By
					i.IdUnic
				,	d.ProcTva
				,	d.CodTva
				,	d.DenumireTva
				,	d.Tip_Ded
				,	i.NumarDOC
			Union All
			Select
					[IdUnic]		= i.IdUnic
				,	[ProcTva]		= d.ProcTva
				,	[CodTva]		= d.CodTva
				,	[DenumireTva]	= d.DenumireTva
				,	[Baza_Tva]		= Sum(d.Valoare)
				,	[Tva]			= Sum(d.Tva)
				,	[Total]			= Sum(d.Total)
				,	[Tip_Ded]		= '100'
				,	[Tabela]		= 'Iesiri'
				,	[NDP]			= i.NumarDOC
			From Iesiri i
				Join IesiriDetaliat d On d.IdDoc = i.IdDoc
			Where
				IsNull(i.IsTvaIncasare,0)	=	1
				And DataDoc					<=	@Data2
				And IsNull(Anulat,0)		=	0
			Group By
					i.IdUnic
				,	d.ProcTva
				,	d.CodTva
				,	d.DenumireTva
				,	i.NumarDOC	
			) a On a.IdUnic = n.IdFactura
	Where
		n.Data Between @Data1 And @Data2
		And a.IdUnic = n.IdFactura
	Group By
			n.IdNota
		,	n.Data
		,	a.ProcTva
		,	a.CodTva
		,	a.DenumireTva
		,	a.Tabela
		,	a.Tip_Ded
		,	a.IdUnic
		,	a.NDP
		
	Union All
	
	Select
			n.IdNota
		,	n.Data
		,	[Suma]	= Sum(n.Suma)
		,	[Tva24]	= Sum(n.Tva24)
		,	[Tva9]	= Sum(n.Tva9)
		,	[Tva5]	= Sum(n.Tva5)
		,	1 As IsTvaIncasare
		,	a.ProcTva
		,	a.CodTva
		,	a.DenumireTva
		,	[Baza_Tva_Factura]	= Sum(a.Baza_Tva)
		,	[Tva_Factura]		= Sum(a.Tva)
		,	[Total_Factura]		= Sum(a.Total)
		,	[Tip_Ded]			= a.Tip_Ded
		,	[Tabela]			= a.Tabela
		,	[IdFactura]			= a.IdUnic
		,	[NDP]				= a.NDP
	From NoteFacturiValuta n
	Inner Join
			(
			Select
					[IdUnic]		= i.IdUnic
				,	[ProcTva]		= d.ProcTva
				,	[CodTva]		= d.CodTva
				,	[DenumireTva]	= d.DenumireTva
				,	[Baza_Tva]		= Sum(d.ValoareLei)
				,	[Tva]			= Sum(d.TvaLei)
				,	[Total]			= Sum(d.TotalLei)
				,	[Tip_Ded]		= Case When IsNull(d.Tip_Ded, '') = '' Then '' When d.Tip_Ded = '50' Then 'N050' When d.Tip_Ded = '100' Then 'N100' End
				,	[Tabela]		= 'Import'
				,	[NDP]			= i.NumarDoc
			From Import i
				Join ImportDetaliat d On d.IdDoc = i.IdDoc
			Where
				IsNull(i.IsTvaIncasare,0)	=	1
				And DataDoc					<=	@Data2
				And IsNull(i.Sters,0)		=	0
				And IsNull(d.Sters,0)		=	0
			Group By
					i.IdUnic
				,	d.ProcTva
				,	d.CodTva
				,	d.DenumireTva
				,	d.Tip_Ded
				,	i.NumarDOC
			Union All
			Select
					[IdUnic]		= i.IdUnic
				,	[ProcTva]		= d.ProcTva
				,	[CodTva]		= d.CodTva
				,	[DenumireTva]	= d.DenumireTva
				,	[Baza_Tva]		= Sum(d.Valoare)
				,	[Tva]			= Sum(d.Tva)
				,	[Total]			= Sum(d.Total)
				,	[Tip_Ded]		= '100'
				,	[Tabela]		= 'Export'
				,	[NDP]			= i.NumarDoc
			From Export i
				Join ExportDetaliat d On d.IdDoc = i.IdDoc
			Where
				IsNull(i.IsTvaIncasare,0)	=	1
				And DataDoc					<=	@Data2
				And IsNull(Anulat,0)		= 0
			Group By
					i.IdUnic
				,	d.ProcTva
				,	d.CodTva
				,	d.DenumireTva
				,	i.NumarDOC
			) a On a.IdUnic = n.IdFactura
	Where
		n.Data Between @Data1 And @Data2
		And a.IdUnic = n.IdFactura
	Group By
			n.IdNota
		,	n.Data
		,	a.ProcTva
		,	a.CodTva
		,	a.DenumireTva
		,	a.Tip_Ded
		,	a.Tabela
		,	a.IdUnic
		,	a.NDP


EndText
mySQLExec(lcSQL, [cNF])

Select cNF
Index On IDNota			Tag IDNota
Index On IDFactura		Tag IDFactura
Index On NDP			Tag NDP
*
Update g;
Set;
	IdFactura = n.IDFactura;
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
Inner Join cNF n On n.IdNota = g.Id_Nota And n.Ndp = g.Ndp

*!*	*** verific daca o plata/ incasare ( tva platita/ incasata ) are 2 cote de tva pt. ca nu stiu sa le defalc
	*** update-urile de mai jos se fac peste acelasi cimp si va ramine ultima valoare adica tva9 !!!
	*** Rezolvare: 
	***   varianta 1. sa sparg in bucati plata din GLE ?
			*** coloane care se modifica in partea care se defalca :
			***  TransactionId					  Suma   Suma_Val   cod_tva, ProcTva  
			*** 441647 - Banca - 349392-Nr.309  228.00    228.00     21-22     19
			*** se defalca asa:
			*** 441647 - Banca - 349392-Nr.309  119.00    119.00     21-22     19
			*** 441647 - Banca - 349392-Nr.309  109.00    109.00      8-9       9
	***   varianta 2. sa fac 2 sectiuni TaxInformation ( una cu fiecare procent) ca este permis:
	*	TaxPercentage2, TaxType2, TaxCode2, TaxAmount2
	*	TaxPercentage3, TaxType3, TaxCode3, TaxAmount3
	***             - as putea sa mai adaug 4..5 coloane care se modifica in GLE unde se trec datele sectiunii 2:
*!*					<TaxInformation>
*!*						<TaxType>300</TaxType>
*!*						<TaxCode>301101</TaxCode>
*!*						<TaxAmount>
*!*							<Amount>10.25</Amount>
*!*							<CurrencyCode>RON</CurrencyCode>
*!*							<CurrencyAmount>0</CurrencyAmount>
*!*						</TaxAmount>
*!*					</TaxInformation>
		
*!*		Select;
*!*			n.*;
*!*			, g.TransactionDescription;
*!*			, g.ContD;
*!*		From cNF n;
*!*			Left Join cGeneralLedgerEntries g On n.IdNota=g.Id_Nota;
*!*		Where;
*!*				Iif( n.Tva24<>0,1,0) + Iif( n.Tva9<>0,1,0) + Iif( n.Tva5<>0,1,0) >= 2 ;
*!*			And	g.JournalId In('Banca', 'Casa', 'Deconturi', 'Compensari');
*!*			And	Left(g.ContD, 3) In('401', '403', '404', '408', '462');
*!*			And	Left(g.ContC, 1)='5';
*!*		Into Cursor CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare
	Select;
		n.*;
		, g.TransactionDescription;
		, g.ContD;
		, g.ContC;
	From cNF n;
		Left Join cGeneralLedgerEntries g On n.IdNota=g.Id_Nota;
		Inner Join (Selec IdNota, ProcTva From cNF Group By IdNota Having Count(ProcTva)>1) d On d.IdNota = n.IdNota;
	Into Cursor CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare
	*
	If RecCount('CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare')>0
		*MessageBox("Aveti o 'Tva platita/incasata' pe cote multiple de tva."+Chr(13)+;
				   "Nu se poate face o separare corecta.",64,"Info...")
		*Browse 
		Tabela_Erori_Saft('CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare')
	EndIf

#if .f.

https://static.anaf.ro/static/3/Anaf/20240821153823_760_com_notificari_saf-t.pdf
Un raspuns de la ANAF:
preluat de pe grupul de saft-t de pe FB
"Am vb cu cei de la Anaf si au spus ca 
notificarile pt lipsa GLE este din cauza ca am depus D406 înainte de actualizarea Duk integrator
Au spus sa actualizez duk si sa trimit din nou D406 verificata cu acesta. Nu aveam niciun attach.
Pt celelalte notificari, pt D406 trimis dupa actualizare DUK, au trimis deja erorile atasate.
Deci, unele notificari au attach, altele nu au."
1. Cei care aveti erori de tip "Testul 2"
Acelea pot proveni de la reduceri primite (ca e 609, ca este alt cont nu prea conteaza). 
Acolo valoarea TVA aferenta reducerii este pusa cu valoare negativa în SAFT si baza pozitiva de aici problema 
- se rezolva daca actualizati, re-generati si depuneti.
Poate sa apara si de la avize, dar acolo e o chestie ciudata si fara logica. 
Pentru ca ANAF pare ca ignora baza TVA si ia suma notei legata de 418.
La aviz programul face
418=707
418=4428
La factura asociata avizului, SAGA va face
4111=418...cu valoarea INTEGRALA, 
si nota asta e cea care apare în CSV, dar în XML trimite la aceasta nota valoarea fara TVA pe baza, cota aplicabila si TVA. 
Iar ANAF face verificarea pe suma notei, ignorând câmpul baza.

IdNota	Data		Suma	Tva24	Tva9	Tva5	IsTvaIncasare	ProcTva	CodTva	DenumireTva		Baza_Tva_F	Tva_F	Total_F	Tip_Ded		Tabela
366614	2024-07-10	3609.60	562.40	7.20	0.00	1				9		8-9  	9% Achiz...		80.00		7.20	87.20		''		Intrari
366614	2024-07-10	3609.60	562.40	7.20	0.00	1				19		6-7  	19%  Achiz...	2960.00		562.40	3522.40		''		Intrari

Update g;
Set;
	TaxAmount	= g1.Suma,;
	CeCont		= g.ContD,;
	CodTva		= '6-7',;
	TaxCode		= '301101',;
	TipTva		= '1',;
	TaxDCA		= 'DCD';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cGeneralLedgerEntries g1 On g1.IdTVA = g.Id_Nota;
Where;
		g1.Type In('Deconturi', 'Casa', 'Banca');
	And Left(g.ContD, 1)='6';
	And Occurs([(], g1.TransactionDescription)<>0 And Occurs([)], g1.TransactionDescription)<>0;
	And Round( g1.Suma / (  Val(SubStr(g1.TransactionDescription, At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', g1.TransactionDescription)-At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)-1)) - g1.Suma ), 2 ) >= 0.19
*
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Update g CodTva: 6-7])	
Update g;
Set;
	TaxAmount	= g1.Suma,;
	CeCont		= g.ContD,;
	CodTva		= '8-9',;
	TaxCode		= '301102',;
	TipTva		= '1',;
	TaxDCA		= 'DCD';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
Inner Join cGeneralLedgerEntries g1 On g1.IdTVA=g.Id_Nota;
Where;
		g1.Type In('Deconturi', 'Casa', 'Banca');
	And Left(g.ContD, 1)='6';
	And Occurs([(], g1.TransactionDescription)<>0 And Occurs([)], g1.TransactionDescription)<>0;
	And Round( g1.Suma / (  Val(SubStr(g1.TransactionDescription, At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', g1.TransactionDescription)-At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)-1)) - g1.Suma ), 2) > 0.05;
	And Round( g1.Suma / (  Val(SubStr(g1.TransactionDescription, At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', g1.TransactionDescription)-At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)-1)) - g1.Suma ), 2) < 0.19
*
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Update g CodTva: 8-9])
Update g;
Set;
	TaxAmount	= g1.Suma,;
	CeCont		= g.ContD,;
	CodTva		= '19-20',;
	TaxCode		= '301103',;
	TipTva		= '1',;
	TaxDCA		= 'DCD';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
Inner Join cGeneralLedgerEntries g1 On g1.IdTVA=g.Id_Nota;
Where;
		g1.Type In('Deconturi', 'Casa', 'Banca');
	And Left(g.ContD, 1)='6';
	And Occurs([(], g1.TransactionDescription)<>0 And Occurs([)], g1.TransactionDescription)<>0;
	And Round( g1.Suma / (  Val(SubStr(g1.TransactionDescription, At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', g1.TransactionDescription)-At('(', g1.TransactionDescription)-1)) - g1.Suma ), 2) <= 0.05

Select Top 1 TipFactura From cGeneralLedgerEntries Where Empty(TipFactura) And JournalID='Intrari' Order By TipFactura Into Array aGetTipFacturaEmpty
*
If _Tally>0

	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select
			Case Tip
				When 1	Then ''
				When 2	Then 'A'
				When 3	Then 'T'
				When 4	Then 'G'
				When 5	Then 'F'
				When 6	Then 'P'
				When 7	Then 'I'
				When 8	Then 'B'
				When 9	Then 'C'
				When 10	Then 'e'
				When 11 Then 'U'
				When 12	Then 'H'
				When 13	then 'O'
				When 14	Then 'Z'
				When 15 Then 'Q'
				When 16	Then 'M'
				When 17	Then 'L'
				When 18 Then 'n'
			End As Tip,
			IdUnic
		From Intrari
		Where
			DataDoc>=?Data1 And DataDoc<=?Data2
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [cGetTipFactura])

	Update g;
	SET;
		TipFactura	= f.Tip;
	From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cGetTipFactura f On f.IdUnic=g.Id_Nota;
	Where;
		Empty(TipFactura);
		And JournalID='Intrari'

EndIf

Where;
	Right(AllTrim(TransactionDescription), 1)='}';
	And !IsNullOrEmpty(TransactionDescription)

Update cGeneralLedgerEntries;
Set;
	CeCont			=	Get_CodTva_Cont(TransactionDescription, 2);
Where;
	Right(AllTrim(TransactionDescription), 1)='}' And !IsnullOrEmpty(TransactionDescription)
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Update cGeneralLedgerEntries.CeCont])

Where;
	Not Empty(CodTva)
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Update cGeneralLedgerEntries.ProcTva])

	Not Empty(CodTva)

Update cGeneralLedgerEntries;
Set;
	TaxBase			=	GetBazaTva(Suma, ProcTva);
Where;
	Not Empty(CodTva);
	And JournalID ='Iesiri';
	And ContC = '4427';
	And Atc('autofactura', Lower(TransactionDescription))>0
&&AUTOFACTURA.19.09.2023
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Update TaxBase])

Update cGeneralLedgerEntries Set	TaxDCA			=	'DCN' Where InList(JournalID, 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Intrari', 'Import' )

Update cGeneralLedgerEntries Set	TaxDCA			=	'DCD' Where InList(JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import')	And (CeCont=ContD Or CeCont=ContC)

	Cote Tva
	>=0.19					24%
	>=0.16					19%
	Intre 0.05 si 0.16		9%

Update g;
Set ;
	TaxAmount	= t.Suma,;
	CeCont		= g.ContD,;
	CodTva		= ;
		ICase(;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))>=0.12, '21-22',;	&& 19
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))<=0.05, '25-26',;	&& 5
		'23-24'),;																													&& 9
	TaxCode		=;
		ICase(;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))>=0.12, '301101',;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))<=0.05, '301103',;
		'301102'),;	
	ProcTva = ;
		ICase(;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))>=0.12, 19,;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))<=0.05, 5,;
		9),;	
	TipTva		= '1',;
	TaxDCA		= 'DCD';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cGeneralLedgerEntries t On t.IdTVA=g.Id_Nota;
Where;
	g.JournalId In('Banca', 'Casa', 'Deconturi');
	And Left(g.ContC, 3)='531';
	And t.IdTVA>0

*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ DECONTURI:  cGeneralLedgerEntries.TaxAmount, CeCont, CodTva Where JournalId In('Banca', 'Casa', 'Deconturi')... ])
	
	
*	MONETARE etc.
Update g;
Set ;
	TaxAmount	= t.Suma,;
	CeCont		= g.ContC,;
	CodTva		= ;
		ICase(;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))>=0.12, '21-22',;	&& 19
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))<=0.05, '25-26',;	&& 5
		'23-24'),;																													&& 9
	TaxCode		=;
		ICase(;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))>=0.12, '310309',;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))<=0.05, '310311',;
		'310310'),;
	ProcTva = ;
		ICase(;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))>=0.12, 19,;
		t.Suma/Val(SubStr(t.TransactionDescription, At('(', t.TransactionDescription)+1, At(')', t.TransactionDescription)-At('(', t.TransactionDescription)-1))<=0.05, 5,;
		9);
		,;	
	TipTva		= '2',;
	TaxDCA		= 'DCC';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cGeneralLedgerEntries t On t.IdTVA=g.Id_Nota;
Where;
	g.JournalId In('Banca', 'Casa', 'Deconturi');
	And Left(g.ContD, 3)='531';
	And t.IdTVA>0
	
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ MONETARE: Update cGeneralLedgerEntries.TaxAmount, CeCont, CodTva, TaxCode, ProcTva, TipTva, TaxDCA Where JournalId In('Banca', 'Casa', 'Deconturi')... ])

*!*	Update cGeneralLedgerEntries;
*!*	Set;
*!*		TaxType='000', TaxCode='000000';
*!*	Where;
*!*		ContC In (Select Cont From Terti Where Left(RegistrationNumber, 2)='03');
*!*		And Type='Intrari'

Update cGeneralLedgerEntries;
Set;
		TaxType	= '000';
	,	TaxCode	= '000000';
Where;
	ContC In (Select Cont From Terti Where Left(RegistrationNumber, 2)='03' And Not InList(TipImpozit, ' ', '0', '2', '3'));
	And Type='Intrari'

lnCotaTvaStandard	= GetCotaTva(Data1, '6-7')
lnCotaTvaRedusa1	= GetCotaTva(Data1, '8-9')
lnCotaTvaRedusa2	= GetCotaTva(Data1, '19-20')

&& 27Feb2025
*
lnCotaTvaStandard20 = 20
lnCotaTvaStandard24 = 24									
*
Update g;
Set ;
	CeCont							= Cont_SAFT(ContD),;
	CodTva							= '21-22',;
	TaxCode							= ICase( n.Tip_Ded='100', '309105',;   &&& Regularizari taxa dedusa 24%
											 n.Tip_Ded='50' , '399105',;    
											 '309105';
										   ),; 				  
	TipTva							= '34',;       
	TaxDCA							= 'DCC';
	, TaxBase						= ICase( n.Tva24=0, 0, Abs(n.Total_Factura)>Abs(n.Suma), Round(GetBazaTva(n.Tva24, n.ProcTva),2), n.Baza_Tva_Factura);
	, TaxAmount						= 0;
	, ProcTva						= n.ProcTva;
	, TaxBaseDescription			= n.DenumireTva;
	, TaxType						= '300';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cNF n On;
						n.IdNota	= g.Id_Nota;
					And n.IdFactura	= g.IdFactura;
Where;
	JournalId In('TVA - platita');
	And g.Id_Nota					In(Select Distinct Id_Nota From cGeneralLedgerEntries Where JournalID='TVA - platita');
	And n.ProcTva					In(lnCotaTvaStandard20, lnCotaTvaStandard24);
	And g.ContD						= '4426'
*

	, TaxAmount						= 0;
	, ProcTva						= n.ProcTva;
	, TaxBaseDescription			= n.DenumireTva;
	, TaxType						= '300';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cNF n On;
						n.IdNota	= g.Id_Nota;
					And n.IdFactura	= g.IdFactura;
Where;
		JournalId In('TVA - platita');
	And g.Id_Nota In(Select Distinct Id_Nota From cGeneralLedgerEntries Where JournalID='TVA - platita');

	, TaxBaseDescription 			= n.DenumireTva;
	, ProcTva						= n.ProcTva;

From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cNF n On;
						n.IdNota	= g.Id_Nota;

		JournalId						In('TVA - incasata');

						n.IdNota	= g.Id_Nota;
					And n.IdFactura	= g.IdFactura;
Where;
		JournalId						In('TVA - incasata');
	And g.Id_Nota					In(Select Distinct Id_Nota From cGeneralLedgerEntries Where JournalID='TVA - incasata');
	And n.ProcTva					= lnCotaTvaRedusa1;
	And g.ContC						= '4427'
*
**, TaxAmount						= Min(n.Tva9,n.Tva_Factura);
*
Update g;
Set ;
	TaxAmount_5						= 0,; 
	CeCont_5						= Cont_SAFT(ContC),;
	CodTva_5						= '25-26',;
	TaxCode_5						= '310311',;
	TipTva_5						= '11',;
	TaxDCA_5						= 'DCC';
	, TaxType_5						= '300';
	, DenumireTva_5					= n.DenumireTva;
	, TaxBaseDescription_5 			= n.DenumireTva;
	, ProcTva_5						= n.ProcTva;
	, TaxBase_5						= ICase( n.Tva5=0, 0, Abs(n.Total_Factura)>Abs(n.Suma), Round(GetBazaTva(n.Tva5, n.ProcTva),2), n.Baza_Tva_Factura);
	, ProcTva						= n.ProcTva;
	, TaxType						= '300';
From cGeneralLedgerEntries g;
	Inner Join cNF n On;
						n.IdNota	= g.Id_Nota;
					And n.IdFactura	= g.IdFactura;
Where;
		JournalId						In('TVA - incasata');
	And g.Id_Nota					In(Select Distinct Id_Nota From cGeneralLedgerEntries Where JournalID='TVA - incasata');
	And n.ProcTva					= lnCotaTvaRedusa2;
	And g.ContC						= '4427'
*
**, TaxAmount						= Min(n.Tva5,n.Tva_Factura);
*

*** compensare 401-411  -update pt. corectare cod fiscal la 401 
*** aici vine asa :
***	 cont 		Cont corespondent	cod fiscal
*** 401.1022   		4111.567		NL810785201B01  
*** 4111.567   		401.1022		NL810785201B01  
*!*	Update gl; 
*!*	Set;
*!*		Cod_Fiscal=t.Cod_Fiscal;
*!*	From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii gl;
*!*	Inner Join Terti t On t.Cont=gl.Cont;
*!*	Where;
*!*		AllTrim(gl.TransactionDescription)='Compensari'
*	
* 01.08.2023
*__Consola_Timing(Program() + [ Compensari])

Update gl; 
Set;
	Cod_Fiscal=t.Cod_Fiscal;
From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii gl;
Inner Join Terti t On t.Cont=gl.Cont;
Where;
	AllTrim(gl.JournalID)='Compensari'
**	AllTrim(gl.TransactionDescription)='Compensari'  &&& nu stiu de ce acum in transactionDescription nu am cuvintul "Compensari"   acum am asa  "BO RZBR3AH 0551813" la aceasta compensare 


*SI IMEDIAT DUPA ADAUGA :

*** 23-09-2024 Compensare prin nota contabila
***	 cont 		Cont corespondent	cod fiscal
*** 404.330		401.328				5368365
*** 401.328		404.330				5368365
Update gl; 
Set;
	Cod_Fiscal=t.Cod_Fiscal;
From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii gl;
Inner Join Terti t On t.Cont=gl.Cont;
Where;
	JournalId='Note contabile';
	And ;
		( ( Left(gl.Cont,2)='40' And Left(gl.ContCorespondent,2)='40' ) ;
			Or;
		  ( Left(gl.Cont,2)='41' And Left(gl.ContCorespondent,2)='41' ) ;
		)

Update cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii Set TaxBaseDescription='' Where IsNull(TaxBaseDescription)

&& nic  02.04.2024
*** caz: ESC Factura 27/29.02.2024 - fac update pt. modificare
*	408.000002	-	401.404633	2431.00   	*Factura la aviz OGOARELE MUNTENIEI COOPERATIVA AGR{8-9,371.002} 
*	408.000002	-	4428.Avize	 218.79	  	*Tva fact.(aviz) OGOARELE MUNTENIEI COOPERATIVA AGR{8-9,371.002} 
*	4426		-	4427		 218.79		*Tva-TI fact.(aviz)OGOARELE MUNTENIEI COOPERATIVA A{8-9,371.002} 
Update g;
	Set g.TaxAmount = a.TvaAvize,;
	    g.CeCont    = g.AccountId; &&& 408.000002
From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii g;
Inner Join (;
			Select;
				Id_Nota, ProcTva,; 
				SUM( Cast( Iif(Atc('Tva fact.(aviz)',TransactionDescription)>0, Amount, 0.00 ) As N(15,2))) As TvaAvize;
			From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii;
			Where;
				Atc('Tva fact.(aviz)',TransactionDescription)>0;
				And Left(AccountId,3)='408';
			Group By ProcTva, Id_nota;
		   ) a On g.Id_Nota = a.Id_Nota;		
Where;
	g.ProcTva<>0;
	And g.ProcTva= a.ProcTva;
	And !IsNullOrEmpty(g.CodTva);
	And Empty(g.TaxAmount);
	And Atc('Factura la aviz', g.TransactionDescription)>0 ;
	And Left(g.AccountId,3)='408'
&& nic  02.04.2024

#if .F.

A avut cineva care lucreaza in SAP notificare pentru erori saf-t pentru operatiunile generate la transferul tva la incasare din neexigibil in exigibil? 
Pentru aceste operatiuni este alocat un cod de taxa in sistem si completeaza tax amount pe contul 4426-si se pare ca acesta nu trebuie sa aiba sume. 

Lucian Rosca Admin
	taxAmount se completeaza la conturile care semnifica baza de calcul (ex, 371, 628, 707...)
	
Nora Birzan	Author
	Lucian Rosca da, exista un cod de taxa pentru tva la incasare care duce sumele in 4428 dar apoi cand se muta din 4428 in 4426, 
la plata facturilor trebuie evidentiat tva ul care devine din neexigibil - exigibil...prin alt cod de taxa...
si la operatiunea 4428=4426 nu am nici o baza pe care sa trec codul de taxa.
Reply
Lucian Rosca Admin
	Nora Birzan baza a fost la momentul facturii cu codul de taxa aferent t.i.
Reply
Nora Birzan Author
	Lucian Rosca atunci va trebui sa schimbam configurarea. Multumesc.
	
HOTFOX:

Sunt interesat indeosebi la contarile referitaoare la tva la incasare, si cum raportam baza acestora
CUM SE MAI INREGISTREAZA ACHIZITIA CU TVA LA INCASARE ?,


Nu mai regasesc codul 301301 in xls cum era inainte,mai este valabila secventa de mai jos ?

Întrebare:
Conturile 4426 si 4428 se vor mapa la SAFT_T? Eu nu le regasesc in nomenclatoarele de achizitii.
Raspuns 16:
Contul 4426 se mapeaza cu Taxtype 300 si Tax code-urile existente in nomenclatoarele de achizitii
Contul 4428 se va mapa cu Taxtype 300 si Tax codurile nou introduse.
Va rugam sa aveti in vedere si cazurile de utilizare de mai jos.


2. Note contabile specifice contribuabililor care aplica sistemul TVA la incasare
Primirea Facturii cu TVA la incasare:
% 		= 401	119
6xx 			100
4428			19

transpusa in SAF T ca:
401			
	Credit amount 119 
	Tax Type 300 
	Tax Code 301301 
	Tax Amount=0 							; sau Tax Type 000 Tax Code 000000 Tax amount 0
6xx
	Debit amount 100
	Tax Type 300
	Tax Code 301301
	Tax amount=19
4428
	Debit amount 19
	Tax Type 300
	Tax Code 301301
	Tax amount=0 							; sau Tax Type 000 Tax Code 000000 Tax amount 0

Deducerea TVA la plata furnizorului:

4426 = 4428 transpusa in SAF-T ca:
4426
		Debit Amount 19
		Tax Type 300
		Tax Code 301101,
		Tax Amount=0
4428
		Credit amount 19
		Tax Type 300
		Tax Code 301301,
		Tax Amount=0					; sau Tax Type 000 Tax Code 000000 Tax amount 0	


Cele de pe 51,53,54 tb golite
Pt 4428. numai Amount = -TaxBase + Amount
#endif

*
Function _07TaxTable()
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*
*//	07.TaxTable
*__________________________________________________________________________________
*

&& 28Feb2025
*
*!*	Select Distinct;
*!*	    g.TaxType;
*!*		, Cast(ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', 'Nedefinit', i.Denumire) As C(254)) As Description;
*!*		, g.TaxCode;
*!*		, Cast(ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', 000.00, g.TaxPercentage) As N(18,3)) As TaxPercentage;
*!*		, Cast(1.00 As N(6,4)) As BaseRate;
*!*		, 'RO' As Country;
*!*		, ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', Space(5),  g.CodTva)   As CodTva;
*!*		, ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', Space(80), i.Denumire) As Denumire;
*!*		, Evl(Nvl(l.Descriere, Space(0)), A.Denumire) Descriere_TaxCode;
*!*	From (Select Distinct TaxType, TaxCode, TaxPercentage, CodTva, JournalID From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii) g;
*!*	Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_TaxCode_Livrari l On L.TaxCode=g.TaxCode And L.ProcTva=g.TaxPercentage And g.JournalID In('Iesiri', 'Export');
*!*	Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_TaxCode_Achizitii a On A.TaxCode=g.TaxCode And A.ProcTva=g.TaxPercentage And g.JournalID In('Intrari', 'Import');
*!*	Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_Impozite_Si_Taxe i On i.TaxType=g.TaxType;
*!*	Where;
*!*		Not IsNullOrEmpty(g.Taxtype);
*!*	Into Cursor MasterFiles_TaxTable ReadWrite


*!*	Select Distinct;
*!*	    g.TaxType;
*!*		, Cast(ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', 'Nedefinit', i.Denumire) As C(254)) As Description;
*!*		, g.TaxCode;
*!*		, Cast(ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', 000.00, g.TaxPercentage) As N(18,3)) As TaxPercentage;
*!*		, Cast(1.00 As N(6,4)) As BaseRate;
*!*		, 'RO' As Country;
*!*		, ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', Space(5),  g.CodTva)   As CodTva;
*!*		, ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', Space(80), i.Denumire) As Denumire;
*!*		, Evl(Nvl(l.Descriere, Space(0)), A.Denumire) Descriere_TaxCode;
*!*	From (Select Distinct TaxType, TaxCode, TaxPercentage, CodTva, JournalID From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii Where Not IsNullOrEmpty(TaxType)) g;
*!*	Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_TaxCode_Livrari l On L.TaxCode=g.TaxCode And L.ProcTva=g.TaxPercentage And g.JournalID In('Iesiri', 'Export');
*!*	Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_TaxCode_Achizitii a On A.TaxCode=g.TaxCode And A.ProcTva=g.TaxPercentage And g.JournalID In('Intrari', 'Import');
*!*	Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_Impozite_Si_Taxe i On i.TaxType=g.TaxType;
*!*	Where;
*!*		Not IsNullOrEmpty(g.Taxtype);
*!*	Into Cursor MasterFiles_TaxTable ReadWrite

Select Distinct;
    g.TaxType;
	, Cast(ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', 'Nedefinit', i.Denumire) As C(254)) As Description;
	, g.TaxCode;
	, Cast(ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', 000.00, g.TaxPercentage) As N(18,3)) As TaxPercentage;
	, Cast(1.00 As N(6,4)) As BaseRate;
	, 'RO' As Country;
	, ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', Space(5),  g.CodTva)   As CodTva;
	, ICase(g.TaxType='000' And g.TaxCode='000000', Space(80), i.Denumire) As Denumire;
	, Evl(Nvl(l.Descriere, Space(0)), A.Denumire) Descriere_TaxCode;
From ;
	(;
	Select Distinct TaxType,	TaxCode,	TaxPercentage,		CodTva,		JournalID From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii Where Not IsNullOrEmpty(TaxType);
	Union ALL;
	Select Distinct TaxType_9,	TaxCode_9,	TaxPercentage_9,	CodTva_9,	JournalID From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii Where Not IsNullOrEmpty(TaxType_9);
	Union ALL;
	Select Distinct TaxType_5,	TaxCode_5,	TaxPercentage_5,	CodTva_5,	JournalID From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii Where Not IsNullOrEmpty(TaxType_5);
	) g;
Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_TaxCode_Livrari l On L.TaxCode=g.TaxCode And L.ProcTva=g.TaxPercentage And g.JournalID In('Iesiri', 'Export');
Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_TaxCode_Achizitii a On A.TaxCode=g.TaxCode And A.ProcTva=g.TaxPercentage And g.JournalID In('Intrari', 'Import');
Left Join Anaf_Nomenclator_Impozite_Si_Taxe i On i.TaxType=g.TaxType;
Where;
	Not IsNullOrEmpty(g.Taxtype);
Into Cursor MasterFiles_TaxTable ReadWrite
