* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function CreareCursoare
	*
	If Not Used('PlanCont')
		Use Dbf\PlanCont && In 0 Exlusive
	EndIf
	*
	Select PlanCont
	Set Order To Cont
	*
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : ")
	GetBalantaSAFT(Data1, Data2, .F.)
	*
	&&: Comentat VICOS.30.09.2024 pt ca este cuprins in GetBalantaSAFT()

	*!*		*** nic 08-04-2024			&&: PAI si cu DACOSOFT cum facem care are acelasi rulaj in luna cu + si -
	*!*		Delete;
	*!*		From Con_Temp;
	*!*		Where;
	*!*				Nvl(Deb_Init, 	0)	= 0;
	*!*			And	Nvl(Cred_Init,	0)	= 0;
	*!*			And	Nvl(Fin_D,		0)	= 0;
	*!*			And	Nvl(Fin_C,		0)	= 0;
	*!*			And	Nvl(Rulaj_D,	0)	= 0;
	*!*			And	Nvl(Rulaj_C,	0)	= 0
	*!*		*** nic 08-04-2024
	*
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => BalantaSAFT fara totaluri pe sintetic ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ GetBalantaSAFT fara totaluri pe sintetic...])	
	*SAFT_Refresh(oSAFT_Declaratie)
	*
	Select;
		Distinct PadR(Left(Cont, 3), 30, ' ') As Cont;
	From Con_Temp;
	Where;
		(;
		Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
		Or ;
		(;
			Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3;
			And;
				(;
				InList(Cont, '101', '103', '104', '106', '108', '109', '117', '141', '149', '151', '161', '162', '166', '168', '169') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '207', '211', '213', '267', '269', '280', '281,290', '291', '293', '296') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '202', '392', '394', '395') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '401', '403', '405', '408', '409') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '411', '413', '418', '419') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '428', '431', '437', '438', '441', '442', '445', '448', '451', '453', '455', '458', '466', '475', '490') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '508', '509', '511', '512', '518', '519', '531', '532', '541', '542') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '602', '605', '623', '642', '645', '646', '651', '658', '664', '665', '681', '686') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '701', '741', '751', '758', '761', '764', '765', '781', '786') ;
				);
		);	
		Or ;
			(;
			Len(AllTrim(Cont))=4;
			And;
				(;
				InList(Cont, '4091', '4092', '4093', '4094') Or ;
				InList(Cont, '4111', '4118') ;
				);
			);
		);	
		And  Not "."$Cont;
	Into Cursor cCheckConturi
	*
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ cCheckConturi...])	
	Select;
		Cast(c1.Cont As c(20)) As Cont;
	From cCheckConturi c1;
		Inner Join Con_Temp c On c1.Cont=c.Cont;
	Where;
		 Not InList(c1.Cont, '411');
		 And (Deb_Init<>0 Or Cred_Init<>0 Or Fin_D<>0 Or Fin_C<>0 Or Rulaj_D<>0 Or Rulaj_C<>0);
	Into Cursor C_Conturi_Err
	***  nic 30-03-2024  daca activezi cea de jos comentezi mai sus "...Into Cursor cCheckConturi" si "...Into Cursor C_Conturi_Err"
*!*		If lcTipDeclaratie <> 'A'	
*!*			***	1. afla sold initial
*!*			data1={01/02/2024}
*!*			data2={29/02/2024}
*!*			Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
*!*				Select a.Cont, Sum(a.Suma) As Sold_Prec
*!*				From (	Select ContD As Cont, Sum(Suma) As Suma	 From Registru Where	Data < ?Data1 And ContD<>' ' And Sters=0	Group By ContD
*!*						Union All
*!*						Select ContC As Cont, Sum(-Suma) As Suma From Registru Where	Data < ?Data1 And ContC<>' ' And Sters=0	Group By ContC
*!*					 ) a
*!*				Group by a.Cont	
*!*				Having Sum(a.suma)<>0
*!*			EndText
*!*			mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Conturi_Cu_Sold_Initial_In_Perioada])
*!*			***
*!*			***	2. afla conturi din luna
*!*			Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
*!*				Select a.Cont
*!*				From (	Select ContD As Cont From Registru Where Data >= ?Data1 And Data <= ?Data2 And Sters=0 And Suma<>0 Group By ContD
*!*						Union All
*!*						Select ContC As Cont From Registru Where Data >= ?Data1 And Data <= ?Data2 And Sters=0 And Suma<>0 Group By ContC
*!*					 ) a
*!*				Group by a.Cont	 	
*!*			EndText
*!*			mySQLExec(lcSQL, [conturi_cu_rulaje_in_perioada])
*!*			***
*!*			Select Cont From Conturi_Cu_Sold_Initial_In_Perioada;
*!*			Union All;
*!*			Select Cont From conturi_cu_rulaje_in_perioada;
*!*			Group By 1;
*!*			Into Cursor conturi_active_in_perioada
*!*			***		   
*!*			Select Cont;
*!*				From conturi_active_in_perioada;
*!*				Where AreAnalitic( Cont, .F., 'conturi_active_in_perioada')=.T.;
*!*				Into Cursor C_Conturi_Err
*!*		Else
*!*			Select Cont From Con_Temp Where 0=9 Into Cursor C_Conturi_Err
*!*		EndIf
*!*		****** nic 30.03.2024  *************
	
	
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ C_Conturi_Err...])	
	*
	Tabela_Erori_Saft('CreareCursoare_C_Conturi_Err')
	*
	*
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => Verificare folosire sintetice de grad I  cand acestea au sintetice de grad II...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => Verificare folosire sintetice de grad I  cand acestea au sintetice de grad II...])
	*
	Select;
		Distinct PadR(Cont, 20, ' ') As Cont;
		, Denumire;
		, Cast('' As C(50)) As Localitate;
		, Cont As Cont_Orig;
	From Con_Temp;
	Into Cursor Bal_Curenta ReadWrite
	*
	Update Bal_Curenta Set Cont = Cont_Saft(Cont,.T.)
	*
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select
			 IsNull(Cont, '') As Cont
			, IsNull(Denumire, '') As Denumire
			, '#Localitate necompletata' As Erori
		From Conturi
		Where
			Sters=0
			And 
			(
				(Left(Cont, 3) In('401', '403', '404', '408', '462') And Len(Cont)>3)
				Or 
				(Left(Cont, 3)='409' And Len(Cont)>4)
				Or 
				(Left(Cont, 3) In('411', '418', '419', '461') And Len(Cont)>3)
			)
			And IsNull(Cod_Fiscal, '')<>'' 
			And IsNull(Localitate, '')=''
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Lipsa_LocalitateSQL])
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ Lipsa_LocalitateSQL...])	
	*Delete From Lipsa_Localitate Where Cont Not In (Select Cont From Con_Temp)
		
	*** nic 21.03.2024 inlocuit asa:
	*** Select * From Lipsa_LocalitateSQL Where Cont In (Select Cont From Con_Temp) Into Cursor Lipsa_Localitate
	Select *;
	From Lipsa_LocalitateSQL ;
	Where;
		Cont In (Select Cont From Con_Temp Where (Deb_Init<>0 Or Cred_Init<>0 Or Fin_D<>0 Or Fin_C<>0 Or Rulaj_D<>0 Or Rulaj_C<>0) );
	Into Cursor Lipsa_Localitate
    *** nic 21.03.2024

	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ Lipsa_Localitate...])	
	*
	Select;
		Cont,;
		Denumire,;
		Cast(;
				Iif( Empty(Cont),		'#Cont'      ,  Space(0))+; 
			   	Iif( Empty(Denumire),	'#Denumire'  ,  Space(0)); 
		    As C(100);
		    ) As Erori;
	From Bal_Curenta;
	Where;
		IsNullOrEmpty(Cont);
		Or ;
		IsNullOrEmpty(Denumire);
	Into Cursor Lipsa_Coloane ReadWrite
	*
	Append From Dbf([Lipsa_Localitate])
	*
    Select;
    	Cont_Orig As Cont;
    	, '#Cont inexistent in '+ICase(vTipConta='A', 'PlanConturiBalSocCom', vTipConta='IFRS', 'PlanConturiIFRS', 'PlanConturiBalSocCom') As Erori;
    From Bal_Curenta;
    Where;
    	Atc('#Err', Cont)>0;
    Into Cursor Cont_Eronat ReadWrite
   	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ Cont_Eronat ...])	
    *
    Select Lipsa_Coloane
    Append From Dbf('Cont_Eronat')

	************************************************************	
*!*		lcMsgErrorOrWarning = ''
*!*		*
*!*		If RecCount('Lipsa_Coloane')>0
*!*			Scan
*!*				lcMsgErrorOrWarning = lcMsgErrorOrWarning + AllTrim(Cont)+' - '+AllTrim(Erori)+Chr(13)
*!*			EndScan
*!*		EndIf
*!*		*
*!*		If .Not. Empty(lcMsgErrorOrWarning)
*!*			lcMsgErrorOrWarning = "Conturi cu probleme:"+Chr(13)+lcMsgErrorOrWarning
*!*			If Not Directory(ExpDir + 'TMP')
*!*				Md ExpDir + 'TMP'
*!*			EndIf	
*!*			lcCaleTemp = ExpDir + 'TMP'
*!*			lcNumeFisier = lcCaleTemp+"ERORI_D406_"+ICas.oSoc.CodFiscal+"_"+StrTran(DToC(Data1), ".", "-")+"_"+DToC(Data2)+".txt"
*!*			StrToFile(lcMsgErrorOrWarning, lcNumeFisier)
*!*			__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + lcNumeFisier + Chr(13)+lcMsgErrorOrWarning)	
*!*			ShellExecute(0, "open", "wordpad", lcNumeFisier, "", 1)
*!*			*
*!*	*!*			Cancel
*!*	*!*			Return .F.
*!*		EndIf
	*
	Tabela_Erori_Saft('CreareCursoare_Lipsa_Coloane')
	*
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => Conturi cu probleme, lipsa denumire, localitate sau nu exista in planul de conturi...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => Conturi cu probleme, lipsa denumire, localitate sau nu exista in planul de conturi...])
	*
	*Wait Window TYPE("_Screen.ActiveForm.Pagini.Setari.Explicatii") = "O"
	*_Screen.ActiveForm.Pagini.Setari.Explicatii.Refresh
	 *DOEVENTS
	*	
	*******	GetBalantaSAFT(Data1, Data2)
	GetBalantaSAFT(Data1, Data2, .T.)
	*
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => GetBalantaSAFT cu totaluri pe sintetic...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => GetBalantaSAFT cu totaluri pe sintetic...])
	*	
	If Nvl(ICAS.oSoc.IdBanca, 0)>0
		*
		Text To lcSQL NoShow Pretext 15 TextMerge
			Select
				Cont
				, [Banca]		= Denumire
				, [Filiala]		= Localitate
				, [ContBanca]	= IsNull(ContBanca, '')
			From Conturi
			Where
				Id=?ICAS.oSoc.IdBanca
		EndText
		*
	Else
		*
		Text To lcSQL NoShow Pretext 15 TextMerge
			Select Top 1
				Cont
				, [Banca]		= Denumire
				, [Filiala]		= Localitate
				, [ContBanca]	= IsNull(ContBanca, '')
			From vBanci
			Where
				Case 
					When Len(?ICAS.oConturi.Banca)=4 And Left(Cont, 4)=?ICAS.oConturi.Banca Then 1 
					When Len(?ICAS.oConturi.Banca)>4 And Cont=?ICAS.oConturi.Banca Then 1 Else 0 
				End=1
				And IsNull(ContBanca, '')<>''
				And Moneda='RON'
			Order By ID
		EndText
		*
	EndIf	
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [cGetBanca1])
	*
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => Date banca principala ...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => Date banca principala ...])
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
			Deb_Init	= Deb_Init-Cred_Init;
		,	Cred_Init	= 0;
	Where;
		InList(Len(AllTrim(Cont)), 3, 4);
		And Sintetic;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Deb_Init>=Cred_Init))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Sold_In_D	= Sold_In_D-Sold_In_C, Sold_In_C=0;
	Where;
		InList(Len(AllTrim(Cont)), 3, 4);
		And Sintetic;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Sold_In_D>=Sold_In_C))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Fin_D	= Total_Deb-Total_Cred, Fin_C=0;
	Where;
		InList(Len(AllTrim(Cont)), 3, 4);
		And Sintetic;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Total_Deb>=Total_Cred))
	*
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Cred_Init=Cred_Init-Deb_Init, Deb_Init=0;
	Where;
		InList(Len(AllTrim(Cont)), 3, 4);
		And Sintetic;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Cred_Init>Deb_Init))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Sold_In_C	= Sold_In_C-Sold_In_D, Sold_In_D=0;
	Where;
		InList(Len(AllTrim(Cont)), 3, 4);
		And Sintetic;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Sold_In_C>Sold_In_D))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Fin_C	= Total_Cred-Total_Deb, Fin_D=0;
	Where;
		InList(Len(AllTrim(Cont)), 3, 4);
		And Sintetic;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Total_Cred>Total_Deb))
	*
	*****	Update Con_Temp Set RulajT_D=Total_Deb-Deb_Init, RulajT_C=Total_Cred-Cred_Init		&& reactivat pe 19.07.2014 pt. ca la TMUCB nu facea totaluri(RulajT_D si C) pe 117 sintetic
	*
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => Finalizare GetBalantaSAFT cu totaluri pe sintetic...], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => Finalizare GetBalantaSAFT cu totaluri pe sintetic...])

		*
		GetTertiSAFT()
		*
		AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => GetTertiSAFT()... ], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
		__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => GetTertiSAFT()...])		
		*
	EndIf

	If lcTipDeclaratie <> 'A'	
		TryToRepair_Localitate_Necompletata()
	EndIf
	*
	*	And ContD<>'' And ContC<>'' 
*!*		Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
*!*			Select Distinct [Cont]=ContD From Registru Where Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Suma, 0)<>0 And Sters=0
*!*			Union
*!*			Select Distinct [Cont]=ContC From Registru Where Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Suma, 0)<>0 And Sters=0
*!*			Order By 1
*!*		EndText

	&& Vicos.03.10.2023, 
	&& existau situatii cand aparea in Intrari cu o factura cu + si - cu total 0 si nu facea NC	
	&& ESC 2023.08 DACODASOFT
	*
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select Distinct [Cont]=ContD From Registru Where Sters=0 And Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Suma, 0)<>0 And IsNull(ContD, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=ContC From Registru Where Sters=0 And Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Suma, 0)<>0 And IsNull(ContC, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Intrari Where Sters=0 And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Iesiri Where IsNull(Anulat, 0)=0 And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Import Where Sters=0 And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Export Where IsNull(Anulat, 0)=0 And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, "cRegistruJurnal")	
	
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => Conturi folosite in Jurnal... ], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => Conturi folosite in Jurnal... ])
	*From cRegistruJurnal r;
	*Inner Join Con_Temp c On c.Cont=r.Cont;
	*

	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => Extrag Clienti... ])

	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => Banci Terti... ], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
	__Consola_Timing("SAFT.prg : CreareCursoare : " + [ => Banci Terti... ])
	*
	Select * From Terti Where InList(Left(Cont,3), '401', '403', '404', '408', '409', '462') Into Cursor crsFurnizori
	* Or Left(Cont, 4)='4091' And Len(Cont)>4
	AddToLog(Transform(Seconds()-_LastTime, [999.9999])+[ => Extrag Furnizori... ], lcLogFile, .T., .T.)
