							&& Prestari de servicii intracomunitare care nu beneficiaza de scutire in statul membru 
							&& in care taxa este datorata (servicii cf reg B2B)
							*
							*lcTaxCode='310301'			&& INCORECT!!!! IN ICAS duc pe D300.Rd01 INCORECT, tb. pe R03	&&Vicos.03.11.2023	
							***lcTaxCode='310306'		&& ASA ESTE CORECT DAR IN ICAS duc pe D300.Rd01					&&Vicos.03.11.2023	
							lcTaxCode='310307'   		&& ASA ESTE CORECT DAR IN ICAS duc pe D300.Rd01					&&Vicos.03.11.2023
							*
						Else
							*
							&& D300.Rd01
							&& lcTaxCode='310301'
							lcTaxCode='310304'			&& Nic.11.04.2025-EUROTEHNICGRUPSRL	D300.Rd03		????
							*
							#if .f.
							
							*Livrari intracomunitare de bunuri, scutite conform art. 294 alin.(2) lit.b) si c) din Codul Fiscal
							 "livrari de mj transport NOI sau produse accizabile catre un partener UE care nu comunica VAT"
							 
							(2) Sunt, de asemenea, scutite de taxa urmatoarele:
							b) livrarile intracomunitare de mijloace de transport noi catre un cumparator 
								care nu îi comunica furnizorului un cod valabil de înregistrare în scopuri de TVA;
							c) livrarile intracomunitare de produse "accizabile" catre o persoana impozabila sau catre o persoana juridica neimpozabila 
								care nu îi comunica furnizorului un cod valabil de înregistrare în scopuri de TVA, 
							în cazul în care transportul de bunuri este efectuat conform art. 407 si 408 sau art. 370, 371 si 375 
							de la titlul VIII, cu exceptia:
								1. livrarilor intracomunitare efectuate de o întreprindere mica;
								2. livrarilor intracomunitare supuse regimului special privind bunurile second-hand, opere de arta, obiecte de colec?ie ?i antichita?i, conform prevederilor art. 312 ;
							
							ART. 407 - Documente de rezerva la destinatie sau în cazuri de export
							ART. 408 - Dovezi alternative ale primirii si ale iesirii
							ART. 370 - Reguli generale
								(1) Persoana care inten?ioneaza sa primeasca, în cadrul activita?ii sale, produse accizabile care se deplaseaza în regim suspensiv de accize dintr-un alt stat membru trebuie sa se autorizeze în calitate de destinatar înregistrat.
								(2) Destinatarul înregistrat nu are dreptul de a produce, de a transforma, de a detine, de a depozita sau de a expedia produse accizabile în regim suspensiv de accize.								
							ART. 371 - Cererea de autorizare ca destinatar înregistrat
							(1) Destinatarul înregistrat poate sa îsi desfasoare activitatea în aceasta calitate numai în baza autoriza?iei valabile emise de autoritatea prevazuta la art. 359 alin. (1), potrivit prevederilor din normele metodologice. Norme metodologice
							(2) Destinatarul înregistrat care primeste doar ocazional produse accizabile poate sa î?i desfa?oare activitatea în aceasta calitate numai în baza autoriza?iei valabile emise de autoritatea competenta. Autorizarea prevazuta la art. 336 pct. 8 se face pentru o cantitate-limita de produse accizabile primita de la un singur expeditor ?i pentru o perioada de timp limitata, potrivit prevederilor din normele metodologice.
							(3) În vederea obtinerii autorizatiei de destinatar înregistrat, solicitantul trebuie sa depuna o cerere la autoritatea competenta, în modul ?i în forma prevazute în normele metodologice.								
							ART. 375 - Obliga?iile destinatarului înregistrat tot despre accize
							#endif
							
							*
						EndIf
						*
					*
					&&	Cod taxa=L4
					&& Livrare de bunuri pentru care locul livrarii este in afara Romaniei (in UE sau non-UE)
					*Case Empty(tcTipFactura) And (tcTip_Tert='2' And tlVies=.F. Or tcTip_Tert='3') And InList(tcCont, '701', '702', '703', '707', '7583')
					Case Empty(tcTipFactura) And (tcTip_Tert='2' Or tcTip_Tert='3') And lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri		
						&& ??? Nu ar tb. sa am tcTip_Tert='3'
						lcTaxCode='310304'
					*
					&&	Cod taxa=L5
					&& Prestare de servicii pentru care locul prestarii este in afara UE
					Case Empty(tcTipFactura) And tcTip_Tert='3' And !lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri							
						&& ??? Nu ar tb. sa am tcTip_Tert='3'
						lcTaxCode='310305'
					*
				EndCase
			*
			Case tcCodTva='16'														
				&& ProcTva=0
				&& Alte livrari scutite cu drept de deducere (SDD     )
				Do Case
					*
					*# Vicos.11.08.2023
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, "S", "U", "H") .Or. (InList(tcTipFactura, "D", "d") .And. tdDataDoc<CToD("01.08.2023"))
						&& D300.R14
						&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (1) lit a) si b) din Codul Fiscal  (Exporturi)
						lcTaxCode='310314'
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, "D") .And. tdDataDoc>=CToD("01.08.2023")
						&& D300.R14.1
						&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (5) lit a) si b) din Codul Fiscal  
						lcTaxCode='310341'
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, "d") .And. tdDataDoc>=CToD("01.08.2023")
						&& D300.R14.2
						&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (5) lit c) si d) din Codul Fiscal
						lcTaxCode='310342'
					*# Vicos.11.08.2023	
					*
					Case InList(tcTipFactura, 'T', 'S', 'U', 'E') And tcTip_Tert='2' 
						&& D300.R13
						&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii supuse masurilor de simplificare (taxare inversa)
						lcTaxCode='310312'
					Case Empty(tcTipFactura) And tcTip_Tert='3'
						&& D300.R14
						&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (1) lit a) si b) din Codul Fiscal  (Exporturi)
						lcTaxCode='310313'
					Case Empty(tcTipFactura) And tcTip_Tert#'3'
						&& D300.R14
						&& Livrari de bunuri si /sau prestari de servicii scutite cu drept de deducere, altele decat Exporturile					
						lcTaxCode='310314'

					OtherWise
						lcTaxCode='#TaxCode'
					*
				EndCase
			*
			Case tcCodTva='17'
				&& ProcTva=0
				&& Livrari scutite fara drept de deducere, 
				Do Case
					&& D300.R15
					&& Livrari de bunuri ?i prestari de servicii scutite fara drept de deducere
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' 
						lcTaxCode='310326'
					OtherWise
						lcTaxCode='#TaxCode'
					*
				EndCase
			*
			Case tcCodTva='18' Or tcTipFactura='n'
				&& ProcTva=0
				&& Op neimpozabile ????
				Do Case
					&& D300.NU SE DECLARA, 
					&& ICAS duce pe D300.R14 ???	*lcTaxCode='310314'
					&& Operatiuni care nu intra in sfera de aplicare a TVA
					*
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' 
						&& D300.NU SE DECLARA, 
						&& Operatiuni care nu intra in sfera de aplicare a TVA
						lcTaxCode='310324'
						&& Livrari de bunuri si /sau prestari de servicii
						&& scutite cu drept de deducere,
							&& altele decat Exporturile
					*						
					*Case tcTip_Tert='3'			
						&& ??? NIC
						&& La exporturi nu exista "18"-Operatiuni neimpozabile
						&& sel alege codul "16"
						&& D300.R14
						*lcTaxCode='310314'
						*lcTaxCode='#TaxCode'
					*
				EndCase
			*
			Case Tva_Art=0	&& AR FI MAI OK SA-L PUN MAI LA INCEPUT?
				Do Case
*!*						Case InList(tcTipFactura, 'S', 'U', 'H')
*!*							&& D300.R14
*!*							&& Livrari de bunuri si /sau prestari de servicii scutite cu drept de deducere, altele decat Exporturile 
*!*							&& si operatiunile prevazute la art 294 alin (5) din Codul Fiscal
*!*							lcTaxCode = '310314'
					*
					*# Vicos.11.08.2023
					Case tcTipFactura='T'
						*
						If IsNullOrEmpty(Tip_Tert)
							&& D300.R13
							&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii supuse masurilor de simplificare (taxare inversa)
							lcTaxCode='310312'
						Else
							&& D300.R14
							&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (1) lit a) si b) din Codul Fiscal  (Exporturi)
							lcTaxCode='310314'
						EndIf					
					*
					Case tcTipFactura='n' Or tcTipFactura='f'											&& NIC.01.10.2024
						&& D300.NU SE DECLARA, 
						&& Operatiuni care nu intra in sfera de aplicare a TVA
						lcTaxCode='310324'
					*
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, "S", "U", "H") .Or. (InList(tcTipFactura, "D", "d") .And. tdDataDoc<CToD("01.08.2023"))
						&& D300.R14
						&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (1) lit a) si b) din Codul Fiscal  (Exporturi)
						lcTaxCode='310314'
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, "D") .And. tdDataDoc>=CToD("01.08.2023")
						&& D300.R14.1
						&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (5) lit a) si b) din Codul Fiscal  
						lcTaxCode='310341'
					Case tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, "d") .And. tdDataDoc>=CToD("01.08.2023")
						&& D300.R14.2
						&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere cf Art. 294 alin (5) lit c) si d) din Codul Fiscal
						lcTaxCode='310342'
					*# Vicos.11.08.2023	
					*
					OtherWise
						*
						Do Case
							*
							Case tcTip_Tert='2'		&&Intracomunitar
								*
								Do Case
									*
									Case tcTipFactura='E'
										*
										Do Case
											*
											Case InList(tcCeCont, '7583') And IsNullOrEmpty(tcCodFiscal)
												&& D300.R03
												&& Livrari intracomunitare de bunuri, 
												&& scutite conform art. 294 alin.(2) lit.b) ?i c) din Codul Fiscal
												lcTaxCode = '310303'
											*
											Case InList(tcCeCont, '701', '702', '703', '707', '419', '7583')
												
												If tdDataDocReala<Data1
													&& D300.R02
													&& Regularizari livrari intracomunitare scutite 
													lcTaxCode = '310302'
												Else
													&& D300.R01
													&& Livrari intracomunitare de bunuri, scutite 
													lcTaxCode = '310301'
												EndIf
											*
											Case  Not InList(tcCeCont, '701', '702', '703', '707', '7583', '419', '446')
												&& D300.R03
												&& Prestari de servicii intracomunitare 
												&& care beneficiaza de scutire in statul membru in care taxa este datorata
												lcTaxCode = '310306'
											*/
											Case tcCeCont='446'
												&& D300.R14
												&& Livrari de bunuri si /sau prestari de servici
												&& scutite cu drept de deducere, 
												&& altele decat Exporturile
												lcTaxCode = '310314'
										EndCase
									*	
									Case (tcTipFactura='N' Or tcTipFactura='') And Not IsNullOrEmpty(tcCodFiscal) And Not InList(tcCeCont, '701', '702', '703', '707', '7583', '419', '446')
										If tdDataDocReala<Data1
											&& D300.R04
											&& Regularizari privind prestari de servicii intracomunitare
											&& care nu beneficiaz? de scutire in statul membru in care taxa este datorata
											&& (servicii cf reg B2B)
											lcTaxCode = '310308'
										Else
											&& D300.R03/03.1
											&& Prestari de servicii intracomunitare
											&& care nu beneficiaza de scutire in statul membru in care taxa este datorata
											&& (servicii cf reg B2B)
											lcTaxCode = '310307'
										EndIf
									*
									Case (tcTipFactura='N' .Or. tcTipFactura='') .And. InList(tcCeCont, '701', '702', '703', '707', '7583', '419')
										If tdDataDocReala<Data1
											&& D300.R02
											&& Regularizari livrari intracomunitare scutite 
											lcTaxCode = '310302'
										Else
											&& D300.R02
											&& Livrari intracomunitare de bunuri,
											&& scutite 
											lcTaxCode = '310301'
										EndIf
									*
									Case tcCeCont='446'
										&& D300.R14
										&& Livrari de bunuri si /sau prestari de servicii
										&& scutite cu drept de deducere, 
										&& altele decat Exporturile
										lcTaxCode = '310314'
									*OtherWise
										&& D300.R03
										&& Livrare de bunuri 
										&& pentru care locul livrarii este in afara Romaniei
										&& (in UE sau non-UE)
										lcTaxCode = '310304'
								EndCase
							*	
							Case tcTip_Tert='E'
								*
								If tcTipFactura='E'
									*
									If InList(tcCeCont, '701', '702', '703', '707', '7583', '419')
										&& D300.R03
										&& Livrare de bunuri 
										&& pentru care locul livrarii este in afara Romaniei 
										&& (in UE sau non-UE)
										lcTaxCode = '310304'
									Else
										&& D300.R03
										&& Prestare de servicii 
										&& pentru care locul prestarii este in afara UE
										lcTaxCode = '310305'
									EndIf
									*
								Else
									&& D300.R14
									&& Livrari de bunuri scutite cu drept de deducere
									&& cf Art. 294 alin (1) lit a) si b) din Codul Fiscal
									&&  (Exporturi)
									lcTaxCode = '310313'
								EndIf
							*
							OtherWise
								&& D300.R15
								&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii
								&& scutite fara drept de deducere
								lcTaxCode = '310326'
						EndCase
						*
				EndCase
				*
			*
			*** OtherWise
			*** 	lcTaxCode='#TaxCode'
			*	
		EndCase
		*
	EndIf
	*
	Return lcTaxCode
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetTipFactura(tnId_Nota, tcJournalId)
*!*		Local lnWorkArea As Integer
*!*		lnWorkArea=Select()
*!*		lnId_Nota=tnId_Nota
*!*		lcTabela=tcJournalId
*!*		lcWhere=Iif(InList(lcTabela, 'Iesiri', 'Export'), ' IsNull(Anulat, 0)=0 ', ' Sters=0 ')
*!*		Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
*!*			Select Tip
*!*			From <<lcTabela>>
*!*			Where
*!*				<<lcWhere>>
*!*				And IdUnic=?lnId_Nota
*!*		EndText
*!*		mySQLExec(lcSQL, [cGetTipFactura])
	*
	&& Am IdUnic ca si valoare unica pt fiecare inregistrare
	*
	lcTipFactura=''
	Select Tip From crsSalesInvoices	Where IdUnic = tnId_Nota;
	Union All;
	Select Tip From crsPurchaseInvoices	Where IdUnic = tnId_Nota;	
	Into Array aGetTipFactura
	*
	If _Tally>0
		lcTipFactura=aGetTipFactura(1)
	EndIf
	*
	Return lcTipFactura
	
	
*!*		Use In ([cGetTipFactura])
*!*		Select (lnWorkArea)
	*
	*If lcTabela='Intrari'
	If tcJournalId='Intrari'
		*
		Do Case
			Case lcTipFactura='1'
				lcTipFactura=' '		&& Factura
			Case lcTipFactura='2'
				lcTipFactura='A'		&& Aviz
			Case lcTipFactura='3'
				lcTipFactura='T'		&& Taxare inversa
			Case lcTipFactura='4'
				lcTipFactura='G'		&& Factura pe mai multe gestiuni
			Case lcTipFactura='5'			
				lcTipFactura='F'		&& Factura imobilizari
			Case lcTipFactura='6'
				lcTipFactura='P'		&& Fara drept de deducere partial (pro-rata)
			Case lcTipFactura='7'
				lcTipFactura='I'		&& Fara drept de deducere integral (pro-rata
			Case lcTipFactura='8'
				lcTipFactura='B'		&& Bonuri Fiscale FARA CIF
			Case lcTipFactura='9'
				lcTipFactura='C'		&& Bonuri Fiscale CU CIF (cu valoare de factura simplificata)
			Case lcTipFactura='10'
				lcTipFactura='e'		&& Exceptii utilizare AMEF
			Case lcTipFactura='11'
				lcTipFactura='U'		&& TURISM - Servicii cf. art. 311
			Case lcTipFactura='12'
				lcTipFactura='H'		&& SECOND HAND - Achizitii cf. art. 312
			Case lcTipFactura='13'
				lcTipFactura='O'		&& Borderouri de achizitii
			Case lcTipFactura='14'
				lcTipFactura='Z'		&& File carnete de comercializare
			Case lcTipFactura='15'
				lcTipFactura='Q'		&& Contracte
			Case lcTipFactura='16'
				lcTipFactura='M'		&& Factura simplificata (exceptand bonuri fiscale)
			Case lcTipFactura='17'
				lcTipFactura='L'		&& Factura emisa in numele furnizorului
			Case lcTipFactura='18'
				lcTipFactura='n'		&& Operatiuni neimpozabile
		EndCase
		*
	EndIf
	*
	Return lcTipFactura
	
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function Get_CodTva_Cont(tcExplicatie, tnType)
	Local lcExpr As String, lcReturn As String
	lcExpr=StrExtract(tcExplicatie, '{', '}')
	lcReturn=''
	*
	Do Case
		*
		Case tnType=1			&& CodTva
			lcReturn=GetWordNum(lcExpr, 1, ',')
		*
		OtherWise				&& Cont
			lcReturn=GetWordNum(lcExpr, 2, ',')
	EndCase
	*
	Return lcReturn
	*
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function Get_ProcTva_Denumire_From_CodTva(tcCodTva, tdData, tcJournalId, tnType)
	If IsNullOrEmpty(tnType)
		lnType=1
	Else
		lnType=tnType	
	EndIf	
	*
	lcCodTva=tcCodTva
	ldData=tdData
	lnTypeTva=ICase(InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import'), 1, 2)
	lcField=Iif(lnType=1, 'ProcTva', 'Denumire')
	*Return SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=<<lcField>> From Tva Where CodTva=?lcCodTva And TipTva=?lnTypeTva And ?ldData BETWEEN dStart And dStop"))
	If lnType=1
		Select ProcTva From Tva		Where CodTva=lcCodTva And TipTva=lnTypeTva And Between(ldData, dStart, dStop) Into Array aGetTva
	Else
		Select Denumire From Tva	Where CodTva=lcCodTva And TipTva=lnTypeTva And Between(ldData, dStart, dStop) Into Array aGetTva
	EndIf
	*
	lnTally=_Tally
	If lnTally>0
		Return aGetTva(1)
	Else
		If lnType=1
			Return 0
		Else
			Return ''
		EndIf
	EndIf
	*	
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetTaxType(tdData)
	lcModPlataTva=GetModPlataTva(tdData)
	lcTaxType='000'
	*
	Do Case
		Case lcModPlataTva=1		&& Lunar
			*lcTaxType='301'
			lcTaxType='300'
		Case lcModPlataTva=2		&& Trimestrial
			*lcTaxType='302'
			lcTaxType='300'
		Case lcModPlataTva=3		&& Semestrial
			*lcTaxType='303'
			lcTaxType='300'
		Case lcModPlataTva=4		&& Anual
			*lcTaxType='304'
			lcTaxType='300'
		OtherWise					&& Neplatitor
			lcTaxType='000'
	EndCase
	*
	Return lcTaxType
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function Get_CustomerID(tcCont, tcContCorespondent, tcCod_Fiscal, tcTara, tcTip_Tert, tlVies, tcDebitCredit)
	lcReturn='0'
	Do Case
		Case;
				(;
				InList( Left(tcCont, 3), '411', '418', '419', '461');
				Or InList( Left(tcContCorespondent, 3), '411', '418', '419', '461');
				);
				And Nvl(tcCod_Fiscal, Space(1))<>Space(1)
			lcReturn=GetRegistrationNumberByVies(tcCod_Fiscal, tcTara, tcTip_Tert, tlVies)	
	EndCase
	Return lcReturn
	
	lcReturn='0'
	cContD=ICase(tcDebitCredit='D', tcCont, tcContCorespondent)
	cContC=ICase(tcDebitCredit='C', tcCont, tcContCorespondent)
	cCod_Fiscal=tcCod_Fiscal
	*
	If tcDebitCredit='D'
		*
		Do Case
			*
			Case;
					(InList( Left(cContD, 3), '411', '418', '419', '461') And Len(cContD)>3);
					Or;
				 	(InList( Left(cContC, 3), '411', '418', '419', '461') And Len(cContC)>3)
				 cCustomer_Supplier_ID=cCod_Fiscal	&& Factura sau incasare Client
			*
			OtherWise
				 cCustomer_Supplier_ID='0'
			* 
		EndCase
		*
	Else
		*
		Do Case
			*
			Case;
					(InList( Left(cContC, 3), '411', '418', '419', '461') And Len(cContC)>3);
					Or;
				 	(InList( Left(cContD, 3), '411', '418', '419', '461') And Len(cContD)>3)
				cCustomer_Supplier_ID=cCod_Fiscal
			*
			OtherWise
				 cCustomer_Supplier_ID='0'
			* 
		EndCase
		*		 
	EndIf
	*
	lcReturn= ICase( cCustomer_Supplier_ID=='0', '0', cCustomer_Supplier_ID=CUI_Raportor, CUI_Raportor, GetRegistrationNumberByVies(cCustomer_Supplier_ID, tcTara, tcTip_Tert, tlVies) )
	Return lcReturn
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function Get_SupplierID(tcCont, tcContCorespondent, tcCod_Fiscal, tcTara, tcTip_Tert, tlVies)
	lcReturn='0'
	Do Case
		Case;
				(;
				InList( Left(tcCont, 3), '401', '403', '404', '408', '462', '409');
				Or InList( Left(tcContCorespondent, 3), '401', '403', '404', '408', '462', '409');
				);
				And Nvl(tcCod_Fiscal, Space(1))<>Space(1)
			lcReturn=GetRegistrationNumberByVies(tcCod_Fiscal, tcTara, tcTip_Tert, tlVies)	
	EndCase
	Return lcReturn

	lcReturn='0'
	cContD=ICase(tcDebitCredit='D', tcCont, tcContCorespondent)
	cContC=ICase(tcDebitCredit='C', tcCont, tcContCorespondent)
	cCod_Fiscal=tcCod_Fiscal
	*
	Do Case
		*
		Case;
				(InList( Left(cContD, 3), '401', '403', '404', '408', '462', '409') And Len(cContD)>3);
				Or;
			 	(InList( Left(cContC, 3), '401', '403', '404', '408', '462', '409') And Len(cContC)>3)
			 cCustomer_Supplier_ID=cCod_Fiscal	&& Factura sau incasare Client
		*
		OtherWise
			 cCustomer_Supplier_ID='0'
	EndCase
	*
	lcReturn= ICase( cCustomer_Supplier_ID=='0', '0', cCustomer_Supplier_ID=CUI_Raportor, CUI_Raportor, GetRegistrationNumberByVies(cCustomer_Supplier_ID, tcTara, tcTip_Tert, tlVies) )
	Return lcReturn
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetRegistrationNumberByVies(tcCod_Fiscal, tcISO, tcTip_Tert, tlVies, tcPJPF) HelpString 'Folosit si pentru CustomerID'
	*
	**	DUREAZA MULT !!!!!
	*
	#if .f.
		Cod unic pentru client este format astfel: tip (doua cifre zecimale) urmat de codul unic al clientului, dupa cum urmeaza:
		1. 00 urmat de CUI - unde tipul este 00, iar CUI este codul unic de identificare  pentru operatorii economici înregistrati în România. 
		Codul este un numar întreg zecimal, cu 1 pâna la 9 cifre, urmat de o cifra de control - Exemplu: 004221306 - pentru Ministerul Finantelor Publice
		Atentie! Nu se trece si atributul fiscal ”RO” pentru platitorii de TVA
		2. 01 urmat de codul de tara (conform ISO 3166-1 - 2 litere) si de Codul unic de identificare pentru TVA din statul membru respectiv
		- pentru operatorii economici din statele membre ale UE, mai putin România, 
		verificate conform sistemului VIES (VAT Information Exchange System) 
		- Exemplu: 01EL123456789 sau 01HU12345678
		3. 02 urmat de codul de tara si de codul unic de identificare pentru TVA din statul respectiv, care nu este nici România, nici stat membru UE 
		- pentru operatorii economici din alte state care nu sunt România sau membre UE 
		- Exemplu: 02TK123005284
		4. 03 urmat de CNP pentru persoane fizice cetateni români sau 03 urmat de codul unic personal pentru persoane fizice rezidente în România 
		(acelasi format cu CNP-ul, dar la care prima cifra este 7 sau 8) 
		5. 04 urmat de cod client asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic, pentru pers. fizice care nu îsi declara CNP-ul pe tranzactii 
		(exemplu: comert online).
		6. 05 urmat de codul de tara si de cod client asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic 
		- pentru operatorii economici care nu sunt înregistrati în scopuri de TVA din statele membre ale UE, mai putin România 
		7. 06 urmat de codul de tara si de cod client asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic 
		- pentru operatorii economici care nu sunt înregistrati în scopuri de TVA din statele non-UE
		8. 08 urmat de 13 cifre zero (080000000000000) pentru clientii care NU SE IDENTIFICA cu cod fiscal în tranzactiile de la punctele de vânzare, 
		precum statii de distributie de carburanti-lubrefianti sau magazine cu vânzare în detaliu. 
		Acest cod este utilizat NUMAI pentru astfel de tranzactii si nu este un inlocuitor universal în raportarea facturilor si platilor, etc. 
		Acest cod NU SE UTILIZEAZA pentru elementul SupplierID - deoarece identitatea furnizorului pe baza de cod fiscal este mereu cunoscuta

		Furnizor (“SupplierID”) si Client (“CustomerID”) – se raporteaza astfel: 
		pentru   tranzactiile si liniile de tranzactii din sectiunea 3.GeneralLedgerEntries
		care nu reprezinta înregistrari de datorii si creante pentru care, conform reglementarilor contabile aplicabile, 
		exista obligatia de contabilizare pe fiecare persoana fizica sau juridica, 
		în câmpurile “SupplierID” si “CustomerID” se va completa codul unic al contribuabilului raportor (CUI_Raportor)
        CASE EMPTY(mCodFiscal)
            mCodSAFT = "04"+ALLTRIM(STRTRAN(STRTRAN(mCont, '.', ''), ' ', ''))			
        CASE mAnalitic='5' .AND. IsFurnizor=.F.
            mCodSAFT = '080000000000000'
        CASE mAnalitic='5' .AND. IsFurnizor=.T.
			mCodSAFT = '040000000000000'
	#endif
	*
	tcCod_Fiscal=AllTrim(tcCod_Fiscal)
	*
	If IsNullOrEmpty(tcCod_Fiscal) Or IsNullOrEmpty(tcISO)		&& ??? Ce se intampla daca nu au completat Cod_Fiscal sau Tara ?
		Return CUI_Raportor
	EndIf
	*
	Local lcTara As String, lcCIF As String
	lcTara = tcISO
	lcTara = IIf(lcTara='GR', 'EL', lcTara)
	*lcCIF = OnlyNumber(tcCod_Fiscal)
	lcCIF  = OnlyNumberCIF(tcCod_Fiscal, tcISO)	
	*
	&& 27Feb2025
	*
	If Not Empty(tcPJPF) And tcPJPF='PF' And Not ValidCNP(tcCod_Fiscal)

		Return '04'+lcCIF										&& . 04 urmat de cod client asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic, 
																&& pentru pers. fizice care nu îsi declara CNP-ul
																&& pe tranzactii (exemplu: comert online).
	EndIf
	*
	&& 27Feb2025
	*
	*
	Do Case
		*Case VerifCF(tcCod_Fiscal)																&& Functia verifica si CNP-urile
		*Case lcTara='RO' And Len(lcCIF)<13
		Case lcTara='RO' And Len(lcCIF)<13 And VerifCF(lcCIF)
			Return '00'+lcCIF
		Case tcTip_Tert='2' And tlVies=.T.
			Return '01'+lcTara+lcCIF
		Case tcTip_Tert='3' And !IsNullOrEmpty(lcCIF)		&& AICI se mai poate verifica in corelatie cu tva-ul din documente si '06'
			* 02 urmat de codul de tara si de codul unic de identificare pentru TVA din statul respectiv, care nu este nici România, nici stat membru UE 
			* - pentru operatorii economici din alte state care nu sunt România sau membre UE 
			Return '02'+lcTara+lcCIF
		Case Len(tcCod_Fiscal)=13 And ValidCNP(tcCod_Fiscal)
			Return '03'+lcCIF
		*Case !ValidCNP(tcCod_Fiscal) And lcTara='RO'											&& ????
		*
		*////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
		*
		*	https://static.anaf.ro/static/10/Anaf/I ... 200422.pdf
		*	pag 64-65, Intrebare/raspuns 4,ex:
		*
		*	04 urmat de codul clientului/ furnizorului asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic, 
		*	pentru pers. fizice care nu îsi declara CNP-ul pe tranzac?ii (exemplu: comert online).
		*
		Case (!ValidCNP(tcCod_Fiscal) Or Empty(tcCod_Fiscal) Or Len(AllTrim(lcCIF))='15') And lcTara='RO'	&& 01.08.2023 inclus si PJPF='PF' && ????
				*4 urmat de cod client asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic, pentru pers. fizice care nu îsi declara CNP-ul pe tranzactii 
				*(exemplu: comert online).
			Return '04'+lcCIF		&&lcCIF = Analiticul contului
		*
		*////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
		*
		Case tcTip_Tert='2' And tlVies=.F.
			* 6. 05 urmat de codul de tara si de cod client asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic 
			* - pentru operatorii economici care nu sunt înregistrati în scopuri de TVA din statele membre ale UE, mai putin România 			
			Return '05'+lcTara+lcCIF
		*Case !Cod_UE(lcTara) And Empty(GetStrFromStr(tcCod_Fiscal))								&& ????
		Case tcTip_Tert='3'  And IsNullOrEmpty(lcCIF)			&& AICI se mai poate verifica in corelatie cu tva-ul din documente si '02'
			*06 urmat de codul de tara si de cod client asociat în mod unic de catre operatorul economic 
			*- pentru operatorii economici care nu sunt înregistrati în scopuri de TVA din statele non-UE
			Return '06'+lcTara+lcCIF
		OtherWise
			Return '08'+Replicate('0', 13)
	EndCase
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetTaxAmount(tnId_Nota, tcCont, tcCodTva, tcCeCont, tnIdCategorie, tcJournalId, tcTip_Ded, tcCategorie, tnAmount)
	&&-- Folosita numai din cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii
	*
	If IsNullOrEmpty(tnIdCategorie)
		tnIdCategorie = 0
	EndIf
	*
	If IsNullOrEmpty(tcCategorie)
		tcCategorie = ''
	EndIf
	*
	*Sum(ICase(Contcorespondent=tcCeCont, -Amount, Amount)) As Amount
	
	Select;
		Sum(Cast( ICase(tcTip_Ded='50', Amount/2, tcTip_Ded='100', 0,  Amount) As N(14, 2))) As Amount;
	From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii;
	Where;
		Id_Nota=tnId_Nota;
		And;
			(Left(Cont, 4)		=  Iif(InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import'), '4426',  '4427') ;
			Or;
			Left(Cont, 4)		=  Iif(InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import'), ICAS.oConturi.TvaNEEXPLATITA,  ICAS.oConturi.TvaNEEXINCASATA) ;
			);
		And CodTva				= tcCodTva;
		And CeCont				= tcCeCont;
		And Nvl(IdCategorie, 0)	= tnIdCategorie;
		And Nvl(Categorie, '')	= tcCategorie;
		And Occurs('tva nedeductibil', Lower(TaxBaseDescription))=0;
		And ICase(CeCont In ('609','767', '709','667'), .T., tnAmount>=0, Amount>=0, Amount<0);
	Into Array aGetTaxAmount
	*
	Return Iif(_Tally>0, aGetTaxAmount(1), 0)
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function Get_CustomerID_Payments(cContD, cContC, cCod_Fiscal, cValoareLei)
    &&& cCod_Fiscal= crspayments.cCod_Fiscal  (nu contine litere de tara in el)
    
	*Cu regula cã întotdeauna plata se face de la Customer cãtre Supplier.
	*1. Daca incasez de la un Client  , utilizati CustomerID si raportati SupplierID cu valoarea 0 (zero).
	*2. Daca platesc       un Furnizor, utilizati SupplierID si raportati CustomerID cu valoarea 0 (zero).
	*3. Daca NU este o plata/încasare catre un Furnizor/Client se completeaza CustomerID/ SupplierID cu codul unic al contribuabilului raportor
	*4. La returnarea unei plati    – CustomerID este CIF Furnizorului dvs. iar SupplierID se completeazã cu 0.  pagina 162
	*5. La returnarea unei incasari - SupplierID este CIF Clientului   dvs. iar CustomerID se completeazã cu 0.
	*
	* DB    CR	  Suma   
	*a) 5121  411    45  incasez				CustomerID =411		SupplierID =0
	*b) 5121  411   -45  returnare incasare 	CustomerID =0		SupplierID =411
	*c) 411   5121   45  returnare incasare		CustomerID =411		SupplierID =0
	*x) 411   5121  -45  returnare incasare		'NC eronata'        'NC eronata'
	*************************************
	*d) 401   5121   77  platesc				CustomerID =0		SupplierID =401
	*e) 401   5121  -77  returnare plata		CustomerID =401		SupplierID =0   
	*f) 5121   401   77  returnare plata		CustomerID =0		SupplierID =401 
	*y) 5121   401  -77  returnare plata        'NC eronata'        'NC eronata'
	*************************************
	*zz 401   411    15  compensare				CustomerID =411		SupplierID =401
	*   404   411    15  compensare             CustomerID =404		SupplierID =401
	*ww 411   401    15  eronata				'NC eronata'		'NC eronata'
	*************************************
	cCustomerID='0'
	*
	If Len(AllTrim(Nvl(cCod_Fiscal, '')))>0
	    cCustomerID= ICase( INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'401','403','404')  And Len(cContD)>3 And LEFT(cContC,3)='411' And Len(cContC)>3, '0',; &&& zz) compensare
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404')  And Len(cContC)>3 And LEFT(cContD,3)='411' And Len(cContD)>3,'NC eronata',; &&& ww)
							INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'411','418','419','461')        And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount>=0, cCod_Fiscal,; &&& a) incasez 411			 ok
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'411','418','419','461')	       And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount< 0, cCod_Fiscal,; &&& b)   ????????????? before 15.03.2023 = '0'
				            INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'411','418','419','461')	       And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount>=0, cCod_Fiscal,; &&& c) returnare incasez 411
				            INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'411','418','419','461')	       And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount< 0, cCod_Fiscal ,; &&&  'NC eronata' &&& x)
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462')  And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount>=0 , 	    '0',	; &&& d) platesc 401			 ok
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462')  And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount< 0,		'0',	; &&& e) returnare platesc 401   ok
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462')  And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount>=0,		'0',	; &&& f) returnare platesc 401   ok
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462')  And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount< 0,       '0'     ; &&& 'NC eronata'	 &&& y)
						  )										   
	EndIf
	*
*!*							    INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'411','418','419','461')	       And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount< 0,'NC eronata',; &&& x)
*!*							    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462')  And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount< 0, 'NC eronata'	; &&& y)

	*
	Return cCustomerID
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function Get_SupplierID_Payments(cContD, cContC, cCod_Fiscal, cValoareLei)
	cSupplierID='0'
	*
	If Len(AllTrim(Nvl(cCod_Fiscal, '')))>0
		cSupplierID= ICase( INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'401','403','404')  And Len(cContD)>3 And LEFT(cContC,3)='411' And Len(cContC)>3, cCod_Fiscal,; &&& zz) compensare 
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404')  And Len(cContC)>3 And LEFT(cContD,3)='411' And Len(cContD)>3,'NC eronata',; &&& ww)
							INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'411','418','419','461')	      And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount>=0,	       '0',; &&& a) incasez 411				ok
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'411','418','419','461')	      And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount< 0,         '0',; &&& b) returnare incasez 411 ??????  before 15.03.2023 = cCod_Fiscal
							INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'411','418','419','461')		  And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount>=0,         '0',; &&& c) returnare incasez 411 
							INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'411','418','419','461')		  And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount< 0,         '0',; &&& 'NC eronata' &&& x)							
							INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462') And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount>=0, cCod_Fiscal,; &&& d) platesc 401				ok
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462') And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount< 0, cCod_Fiscal,; &&& e) returnare platesc 401   ok
						    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462') And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount>=0, cCod_Fiscal,; &&& f) returnare platesc 401   ok
							INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462') And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount< 0, cCod_Fiscal ; &&& 'NC eronata'  &&& y)						    
						  )
	EndIf
	*

*!*	    						INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'411','418','419','461')		  And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount< 0,'NC eronata',; &&& x)
*!*							    INLIST( LEFT(cContC,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462') And Len(cContC)>3 And Amount< 0,'NC eronata' ; &&& y)

	*INLIST( LEFT(cContD,3),'401','403','404','408','409','462') And Len(cContD)>3 And Amount< 0,         '0',; &&& e) returnare platesc 401   ok	Vicos.20.07.2023
	*							    
	Return cSupplierID
EndFunc

	If TYPE("Saft.Pagini.Setari.Explicatii")="O"	
	*	Saft.Pagini.Setari.Explicatii.Value = lcText2 + lcCHR13 + Saft.Pagini.Setari.Explicatii.Value

		Saft.Pagini.Setari.Explicatii.Refresh 
	EndIf
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetBalantaSAFT(tdData1, tdData2, tlWithSumare)
	*
	Release;
		mDeb_Init, mCred_Init, mDeb_Prec, mCred_Prec, mSold_In_D, mSold_In_C,;
		mRulaj_D, mRulaj_C, mRulajT_D, mRulajT_C, mTotal_Deb, mTotal_Cred, mFin_D, mFin_C
	Public;
		mDeb_Init, mCred_Init, mDeb_Prec, mCred_Prec, mSold_In_D, mSold_In_C,;
		mRulaj_D, mRulaj_C, mRulajT_D, mRulajT_C, mTotal_Deb, mTotal_Cred, mFin_D, mFin_C
	*
	Data2=tdData2
	Data1=tdData1
	*
	BalantaSintetica(1, 0, [], .T.)
	*
	Select Con_Temp	
	Delete For InList(Left(Cont, 1), "8", "9")
	*
	Update Con_Temp Set Sold_In_D=Deb_Prec-Cred_Prec	Where Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Deb_Prec>=Cred_Prec)
	Update Con_Temp Set Sold_In_C=Cred_Prec-Deb_Prec	Where Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Cred_Prec>Deb_Prec)
	Update Con_Temp Set Total_Deb=Rulaj_D+Deb_Prec,		Total_Cred=Rulaj_C+Cred_Prec
	Update Con_Temp Set Fin_D=Total_Deb-Total_Cred		Where Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Total_Deb>=Total_Cred)
	Update Con_Temp Set Fin_C=Total_Cred-Total_Deb		Where Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Total_Cred>Total_Deb)
	*
	Select Con_Temp
	Replace RulajT_D With Total_Deb-Deb_Init, RulajT_C With Total_Cred-Cred_Init All
	*	
	If tlWithSumare
		*Sumare Conturi sintetice de gradul II la Conturi sintetice de gradul I
		*
		If InList(Lower(m.NumeDB), 'electra')  Or ICAS.oSettings.GenerareBalantaVeche
			*
			Select;
				Left(Cont, 3) As Cont1,;
				Sum(Deb_Init) As Deb_Init,;
				Sum(Cred_Init) As Cred_Init,;
				Sum(Deb_Prec) As Deb_Prec,;
				Sum(Cred_Prec) As Cred_Prec,;
				Sum(Sold_In_D) As Sold_In_D,;
				Sum(Sold_In_C) As Sold_In_C,;
				Sum(Rulaj_D) As Rulaj_D,;
				Sum(Rulaj_C) As Rulaj_C,;
				Sum(RulajT_D) As RulajT_D,;
				Sum(RulajT_C) As RulajT_C,;
				Sum(Total_Deb) As Total_Deb,;
				Sum(Total_Cred) As Total_Cred,;
				Sum(Fin_D) As Fin_D,;
				Sum(Fin_C) As Fin_C;
			From Con_Temp;
			Where;
				Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
				And  Not ("."$Cont);
				And Empty(Categorie);
			Group By Cont1;
			Into Cursor CurSum ReadWrite
			*
			Select;
				Left(Cont, 3) As Cont1;
			From Con_Temp;
			Where 0=9;
			Into Cursor CurSum4 ReadWrite
			*			
		Else
			*
			***	26 Ianuarie 2015
			* Pe BalantaSintetica Noua se dublau sumele pe sintetice de 3
			*And ICase(Len(AllTrim(Cont))=4 And IsAnalitic(Cont), .F., .T.)=.T.
			***	26 Ianuarie 2015
			*
			Select;
				Left(Cont, 3) As Cont1,;
				Sum(Deb_Init) As Deb_Init,;
				Sum(Cred_Init) As Cred_Init,;
				Sum(Deb_Prec) As Deb_Prec,;
				Sum(Cred_Prec) As Cred_Prec,;
				Sum(Sold_In_D) As Sold_In_D,;
				Sum(Sold_In_C) As Sold_In_C,;
				Sum(Rulaj_D) As Rulaj_D,;
				Sum(Rulaj_C) As Rulaj_C,;
				Sum(RulajT_D) As RulajT_D,;
				Sum(RulajT_C) As RulajT_C,;
				Sum(Total_Deb) As Total_Deb,;
				Sum(Total_Cred) As Total_Cred,;
				Sum(Fin_D) As Fin_D,;
				Sum(Fin_C) As Fin_C;
			From Con_Temp;
			Where;
				Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
				And  Sintetic=.T.;
				And  Not ("."$Cont);
			Group By Cont1;
			Union All;
			Select;
				Left(Cont, 3) As Cont1,;
				Sum(Deb_Init) As Deb_Init,;
				Sum(Cred_Init) As Cred_Init,;
				Sum(Deb_Prec) As Deb_Prec,;
				Sum(Cred_Prec) As Cred_Prec,;
				Sum(Sold_In_D) As Sold_In_D,;
				Sum(Sold_In_C) As Sold_In_C,;
				Sum(Rulaj_D) As Rulaj_D,;
				Sum(Rulaj_C) As Rulaj_C,;
				Sum(RulajT_D) As RulajT_D,;
				Sum(RulajT_C) As RulajT_C,;
				Sum(Total_Deb) As Total_Deb,;
				Sum(Total_Cred) As Total_Cred,;
				Sum(Fin_D) As Fin_D,;
				Sum(Fin_C) As Fin_C;
			From Con_Temp;
			Where;
				Len(AllTrim(Cont))>3;
				And  Sintetic=.F.;
				And  ("."$Cont);
			Group By Cont1;
			Into Cursor CurSum1 ReadWrite
			*
			Select;
				Cont1,;
				Sum(Deb_Init) As Deb_Init,;
				Sum(Cred_Init) As Cred_Init,;
				Sum(Deb_Prec) As Deb_Prec,;
				Sum(Cred_Prec) As Cred_Prec,;
				Sum(Sold_In_D) As Sold_In_D,;
				Sum(Sold_In_C) As Sold_In_C,;
				Sum(Rulaj_D) As Rulaj_D,;
				Sum(Rulaj_C) As Rulaj_C,;
				Sum(RulajT_D) As RulajT_D,;
				Sum(RulajT_C) As RulajT_C,;
				Sum(Total_Deb) As Total_Deb,;
				Sum(Total_Cred) As Total_Cred,;
				Sum(Fin_D) As Fin_D,;
				Sum(Fin_C) As Fin_C;
			From CurSum1;
			Group By Cont1;
			Into Cursor CurSum ReadWrite
			*
			***	26 Ianuarie 2015
			*
			* 04 August 2015 Insumez si Sinteticele de 4
			*
			Select;
				Left(Cont, 4) As Cont1,;
				Sum(Deb_Init) As Deb_Init,;
				Sum(Cred_Init) As Cred_Init,;
				Sum(Deb_Prec) As Deb_Prec,;
				Sum(Cred_Prec) As Cred_Prec,;
				Sum(Sold_In_D) As Sold_In_D,;
				Sum(Sold_In_C) As Sold_In_C,;
				Sum(Rulaj_D) As Rulaj_D,;
				Sum(Rulaj_C) As Rulaj_C,;
				Sum(RulajT_D) As RulajT_D,;
				Sum(RulajT_C) As RulajT_C,;
				Sum(Total_Deb) As Total_Deb,;
				Sum(Total_Cred) As Total_Cred,;
				Sum(Fin_D) As Fin_D,;
				Sum(Fin_C) As Fin_C;
			From Con_Temp;
			Where;
				Len(AllTrim(Cont))>4;
				And  Sintetic=.T.;
				And  Not ("."$Cont);
			Group By Cont1;
			Union All;
			Select;
				Left(Cont, 4) As Cont1,;
				Sum(Deb_Init) As Deb_Init,;
				Sum(Cred_Init) As Cred_Init,;
				Sum(Deb_Prec) As Deb_Prec,;
				Sum(Cred_Prec) As Cred_Prec,;
				Sum(Sold_In_D) As Sold_In_D,;
				Sum(Sold_In_C) As Sold_In_C,;
				Sum(Rulaj_D) As Rulaj_D,;
				Sum(Rulaj_C) As Rulaj_C,;
				Sum(RulajT_D) As RulajT_D,;
				Sum(RulajT_C) As RulajT_C,;
				Sum(Total_Deb) As Total_Deb,;
				Sum(Total_Cred) As Total_Cred,;
				Sum(Fin_D) As Fin_D,;
				Sum(Fin_C) As Fin_C;
			From Con_Temp;
			Where;
				Len(AllTrim(Cont))>4;
				And  Sintetic=.F.;
				And  ("."$Cont);
			Group By Cont1;
			Into Cursor CurSum2 ReadWrite
			*
			Select;
				Cont1,;
				Sum(Deb_Init) As Deb_Init,;
				Sum(Cred_Init) As Cred_Init,;
				Sum(Deb_Prec) As Deb_Prec,;
				Sum(Cred_Prec) As Cred_Prec,;
				Sum(Sold_In_D) As Sold_In_D,;
				Sum(Sold_In_C) As Sold_In_C,;
				Sum(Rulaj_D) As Rulaj_D,;
				Sum(Rulaj_C) As Rulaj_C,;
				Sum(RulajT_D) As RulajT_D,;
				Sum(RulajT_C) As RulajT_C,;
				Sum(Total_Deb) As Total_Deb,;
				Sum(Total_Cred) As Total_Cred,;
				Sum(Fin_D) As Fin_D,;
				Sum(Fin_C) As Fin_C;
			From CurSum2;
			Where  Not ("."$Cont1);
			Group By Cont1;
			Into Cursor CurSum4 ReadWrite
			*
		EndIf
		*
		Select Con_Temp
		*
		Scan For Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3 And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie)	&& 19.07.2014
			*
			Select CurSum
			Locate For Cont1==AllTrim(Con_Temp.Cont)
			*
			If Found()
				*
				Select Con_Temp
				Replace;
					Deb_Init	With Deb_Init	+CurSum.Deb_Init,;
					Cred_Init	With Cred_Init	+CurSum.Cred_Init,;
					Deb_Prec	With Deb_Prec	+CurSum.Deb_Prec,;
					Cred_Prec	With Cred_Prec	+CurSum.Cred_Prec,;
					Sold_In_D	With Sold_In_D	+CurSum.Sold_In_D,;
					Sold_In_C	With Sold_In_C	+CurSum.Sold_In_C,;
					Rulaj_D		With Rulaj_D	+CurSum.Rulaj_D,;
					Rulaj_C		With Rulaj_C	+CurSum.Rulaj_C,;
					Total_Deb	With Total_Deb	+CurSum.Total_Deb,;
					Total_Cred	With Total_Cred	+CurSum.Total_Cred,;
					Fin_D		With Fin_D		+CurSum.Fin_D,;
					Fin_C		With Fin_C		+CurSum.Fin_C
				*
			EndIf
			*
		EndScan
		*
		ICAS.oLogger.LogElapsedMilestone([Command4.Click()->ThisForm.chkSum.Value=1->Insumare analitice la sintetice])
		*
		Select Con_Temp
		*
		*If ICAS.oSettings.GenerareBalantaVeche
			*
			Scan For Len(AllTrim(Cont))=4 And Sintetic And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie)	&& 04.08.2015
				*
				Select CurSum4
				Locate For Cont1==AllTrim(Con_Temp.Cont)
				*
				If Found()
					*
					Select Con_Temp
					Replace;
						Deb_Init	With Deb_Init	+CurSum4.Deb_Init,;
						Cred_Init	With Cred_Init	+CurSum4.Cred_Init,;
						Deb_Prec	With Deb_Prec	+CurSum4.Deb_Prec,;
						Cred_Prec	With Cred_Prec	+CurSum4.Cred_Prec,;
						Sold_In_D	With Sold_In_D	+CurSum4.Sold_In_D,;
						Sold_In_C	With Sold_In_C	+CurSum4.Sold_In_C,;
						Rulaj_D		With Rulaj_D	+CurSum4.Rulaj_D,;
						Rulaj_C		With Rulaj_C	+CurSum4.Rulaj_C,;
						Total_Deb	With Total_Deb	+CurSum4.Total_Deb,;
						Total_Cred	With Total_Cred	+CurSum4.Total_Cred,;
						Fin_D		With Fin_D		+CurSum4.Fin_D,;
						Fin_C		With Fin_C		+CurSum4.Fin_C
					*
				EndIf
				*
			EndScan
			*
			ICAS.oLogger.LogElapsedMilestone([Command4.Click()->ThisForm.chkSum.Value=1->Insumare analitice la sintetice de 4])
			*
		*EndIf
		*
	EndIf
	*	
	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Deb_Init	= Deb_Init-Cred_Init, Cred_Init=0;
	Where;
		Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Deb_Init>=Cred_Init))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Sold_In_D	= Sold_In_D-Sold_In_C, Sold_In_C=0;
	Where;
		Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Sold_In_D>=Sold_In_C))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Fin_D	= Total_Deb-Total_Cred, Fin_C=0;
	Where;
		Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='A' Or (Tip='B' And Total_Deb>=Total_Cred))
	*
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Cred_Init	= Cred_Init-Deb_Init, Deb_Init=0;
	Where;
		Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Cred_Init>Deb_Init))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Sold_In_C	= Sold_In_C-Sold_In_D, Sold_In_D=0;
	Where;
		Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
		And (Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Sold_In_C>Sold_In_D))
	*	
	Update Con_Temp;
	Set;
		Fin_C=Total_Cred-Total_Deb, Fin_D=0;
	Where;
		Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3;
		And IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie);
	And (Tip='P' Or (Tip='B' And Total_Cred>Total_Deb))
	*
	Replace RulajT_D With Total_Deb-Deb_Init, RulajT_C With Total_Cred-Cred_Init All	&& reactivat pe 19.07.2014 pt. ca la TMUCB nu facea totaluri(RulajT_D si C) pe 117 sintetic
	*
	*
	*
	Select Con_Temp
	*	AICI am modificat deb_prec si cred_prec sa fie identice cu deb_init si cred_init
	* * calculeaza pe analitice ca sold iar pe sinteticul de 3 aduna subanalitice
	*
	If InList(Upper(m.NumeDB), Upper('Stentor'))
		*lcWhereStentor=" And Cont Not In ('4091', '4092', '4095', '4096', '4097', '4098', '4099', '4480', '4481')"
		*
		If Rulaj('4091', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4091', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4091 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4092', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4092', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4092 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4095', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4095', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4095 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4096', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4096', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4096 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4097', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4097', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4097 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4098', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4098', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4098 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4099', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4099', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4099 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4480', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4480', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4480 '
		EndIf
		*
		If Rulaj('4481', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'D')=0 And Rulaj('4481', {01.01.2000}, Data2, 'C')=0
			Delete From Con_Temp Where Cont='4481 '
		EndIf
		*
	EndIf
	*
	llIsFirstDayOfYear = m.Data1=CToD('01.01.'+AllTrim(Transform(Year(m.Data1))))
	*
	If llIsFirstDayOfYear
		Update Con_Temp Set Deb_Prec=Deb_Init, Cred_Prec=Cred_Init
	EndIf
	*
	If RecCount([Con_Temp])>0
		*
		Sum;
			Nvl(Deb_Init, 0), Nvl(Cred_Init, 0), Nvl(Deb_Prec, 0), Nvl(Cred_Prec, 0),;
			Nvl(Sold_In_D, 0), Nvl(Sold_In_C, 0), Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0), Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0),;
			Nvl(RulajT_D, 0), Nvl(RulajT_C, 0), Nvl(Total_Deb, 0), Nvl(Total_Cred, 0),;
			Nvl(Fin_D, 0), Nvl(Fin_C, 0);
		To;
			mDeb_Init, mCred_Init, mDeb_Prec, mCred_Prec, mSold_In_D, mSold_In_C,;
			mRulaj_D, mRulaj_C,	mRulajT_D, mRulajT_C, mTotal_Deb, mTotal_Cred, mFin_D, mFin_C;
		For Len(AllTrim(Cont))=3 .And. IsNullOrEmpty(Categorie)
		*
	EndIf
	*
	*#	-	Nu arata conturile care aveau SI=0, sf=0, rulaje=0 deoarece in luna avea suma cu + si -
	*Delete All For;
		(Nvl(Deb_Init, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Cred_Init, 0)=0);
		.And. (Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0)=0);
		.And. (Nvl(Deb_Prec, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Cred_Prec, 0)=0);
		.And. (Nvl(Total_Deb, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Total_Cred, 0)=0);
		.And. Validat=0
	*
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select Distinct [Cont]=ContD From Registru Where Sters=0 And Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2
		Union All
		Select Distinct [Cont]=ContC From Registru Where Sters=0 And Data Between ?Data1 And ?Data2
	EndText
	
	&& Vicos.03.10.2023, 
	&& existau situatii cand aparea in Intrari cu o factura cu + si - cu total 0 si nu facea NC	
	&& ESC 2023.08 DACODASOFT
	*
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select Distinct [Cont]=ContD From Registru	Where Sters=0	And Data	Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(ContD, '')<>'' And IsNull(Cont_D, '')<>'%' And IsNull(Cont_C, '')<>'%'
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=ContC From Registru	Where Sters=0	And Data	Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(ContC, '')<>'' And IsNull(Cont_D, '')<>'%' And IsNull(Cont_C, '')<>'%'
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Intrari	Where Sters=0	And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Iesiri		Where IsNull(Anulat, 0)=0	And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Import		Where Sters=0	And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
		Union
		Select Distinct [Cont]=Cont From Export		Where IsNull(Anulat, 0)=0	And DataDoc Between ?Data1 And ?Data2 And IsNull(Cont, '')<>''
	EndText	
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [cGetConturiRulajePerioada])
	*
	Select Con_Temp
	*
	Delete;
	From Con_Temp;
	Where;
		Validat=0;
		And Sintetic		= .F.;
		And Nvl(Fin_C, 0)	= 0;
		And Nvl(Fin_D, 0)	= 0;
		And Cont			Not In(Select Distinct Cont From cGetConturiRulajePerioada)
	*
	*Delete All For;
		(Nvl(Deb_Init, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Cred_Init, 0)=0);
		.And. (Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0)=0);
		.And. (Nvl(Deb_Prec, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Cred_Prec, 0)=0);
		.And. (Nvl(Total_Deb, 0)=0 .And. Nvl(Total_Cred, 0)=0);
		.And. Validat=0;
		And Sintetic=.T.
	&&  Vicos.28.08.2023
	Delete;
	From Con_Temp;
	Where;
				(Nvl(Deb_Init, 0)	= 0 .And. Nvl(Cred_Init, 0)		= 0);
		.And.	(Nvl(Rulaj_D, 0)	= 0 .And. Nvl(Rulaj_C, 0)		= 0);
		.And.	(Nvl(Deb_Prec, 0)	= 0 .And. Nvl(Cred_Prec, 0)		= 0);
		.And.	(Nvl(Total_Deb, 0)	= 0 .And. Nvl(Total_Cred, 0)	= 0);
		.And.	Validat				= 0;
		.And.	Sintetic			= .T.;
		.And.	Cont				Not In(Select Distinct Cont From cGetConturiRulajePerioada)
	*
	&&  Vicos.28.08.2023
	*
	*Delete For  .Not. ( .Not. ("."$Cont) .Or. Analitic) .And. Empty(Categorie)		&& Stergea Analiticele
	*
	mMesajEroare=""
	*
	Scan For InList(Cont, "6", "7", "581", "4426", "4427") .And. (Fin_D<>0 .Or. Fin_C<>0)
		mMesajEroare=mMesajEroare+IIf(Empty(mMesajEroare), "", ",")+AllTrim(Cont)
	EndScan
	*
	If FORCE_REFRESH
		DoEvents FORCE
	EndIf	
	Return mMesajEroare
	*
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetLetterFromStr(tcTestString)
	Local lcGoodChars
	lcGoodChars = '1234567890~!@#$%^&*()_+-=\[].,/?'
	*Return Len(	ChrTran(Upper(tcTestString), lcGoodChars, Space(0))) <> 0
	Return ChrTran(Upper(tcTestString), lcGoodChars, Space(0))
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________	
Function SAFT_Refresh(toForm)
	Return .T.
	*
	If Type('toForm')<>'O'
		Return
	EndIf
	*	
 	With toForm
		.LockScreen=.T.
 		.Show()
 		.Pagini.Setari.Actualizare.SetFocus()
		.Refresh()
		.LockScreen=.F.
	EndWith	
EndProc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________	
Function TryToRepair_Localitate_Necompletata()
	Select;
		t.Cod_Fiscal, l.*;
	From Lipsa_Coloane l;
	Inner Join Terti t On AllTrim(t.Cont)==AllTrim(l.Cont);
	Where;
		Erori='#Localitate necompletata';
		And Nvl(Tara, 'RO')='RO';
		And Len(AllTrim(t.Cod_Fiscal))<13;
	Into Cursor cGetLipsaLocalitate
	*
	*lcJsonRequest = '[' 		&& + Chr(13)
	lnReccount = RecCount([cGetLipsaLocalitate])
	lcDate = Transform(DToS(Date()), '@R ####-##-##')
	lcCIFList = ''
	*
	Scan
		lcComma = ICase(RecNo([cGetLipsaLocalitate])=lnReccount, '', ',')
		*lcJsonRequest = lcJsonRequest + '{ "cui": ' + AllTrim(Cod_Fiscal) +', "data":' + '"' + lcDate + '"' + '}' + lcComma && + Chr(13)
		lcCIFList = lcCIFList +  AllTrim(Cod_Fiscal) + lcComma 
	EndScan
	*
	*lcJsonRequest = lcJsonRequest + ']' 
	*
	Anaf_Rest(lcCIFList)
	*	
	If Used('Anaf')
		Select;
			 Cast(l.Cod_Fiscal As C(15)) As Cod_Fiscal, Cast(a.sDenumire_Localitate As C(70)) As Localitate, Cast(l.Cont As C(20)) As Cont;
		From Anaf a;
		Inner Join cGetLipsaLocalitate l On Val(l.Cod_Fiscal)=a.CUI;
		Into Cursor Localitati_ANAF
		*
		Scan
			m.Cont = AllTrim(Localitati_ANAF.Cont)
			m.Localitate = AllTrim(Localitati_ANAF.Localitate)
			mySQLExec([Update Conturi Set Localitate=?m.Localitate Where Cont=?m.Cont And Sters=0])
			__Consola_Timing( "SAFT.prg : Completare localitati lipsa cu cele de pe site-ul ANAF: Cont : " + m.Cont + ' Localitate: ' + m.Localitate )
		EndScan
		*
		Update t Set;
			Localitate = a.sDenumire_Localitate;
		From Terti t;
		Inner Join Anaf a On a.CUI = Val(t.Cod_Fiscal)
		*
	EndIf 
	*
	Select;
		t.Cod_Fiscal, l.*;
	From Lipsa_Coloane l;
	Inner Join Terti t On AllTrim(t.Cont)==AllTrim(l.Cont);
	Where;
		Erori='#Localitate necompletata';
		And Nvl(Tara, 'RO')<>'RO';
	Into Cursor cGetLipsaLocalitate_EXTERNI	
	*
	Scan
		m.Cont = AllTrim(cGetLipsaLocalitate_EXTERNI.Cont)
		m.Localitate = 'NECOMPLETATA'
		mySQLExec([Update Conturi Set Localitate=?m.Localitate Where Cont=?m.Cont And Sters=0])
		__Consola_Timing( "SAFT.prg : Completare localitati lipsa cu 'NECOMPLETATA' pt tertii din afara ROMANIEI: Cont : " + m.Cont + ' Localitate: ' + m.Localitate )
	EndScan
	*	
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________	
Function Get_FullPath_SAFT_File(tdData1, tdData2, tcTip_SAFT, tnSegmente)
	*
	If Empty(tnSegmente)
		tnSegmente = 1
	EndIf
	*	
	Data1 			= tdData1
	Data2	  		= tdData2
*	lcTip_SAFT		= tcTip_SAFT  &&& NIC 08.03.2024
	lnLuna			= Month(Data2)
	lnAn			= Year(Data2)
	lcCodDeclaratie	=	"406"
	lcIndice		=	"D"
	lcDirectorSAFT	=;
		ExpDir;
		+ [Declaratii\];
		+ lcIndice;
		+ lcCodDeclaratie;
		+ "\";
		+ AllTrim(myMonth(myFirstDay(lnLuna, lnAn)));
		+ AllTrim(Str(lnAn))+"\";
		+ GetNumeDir();
		+ "\"
	*
	If Not Directory(lcDirectorSAFT)
		Md (lcDirectorSAFT)
	EndIf
	*
	lcTipDecl = Iif(tcTip_SAFT='A', "Active", Iif(tcTip_SAFT='C', 'Cerere', 'Inf'))
	lcFiserD406	= lcDirectorSAFT+'D406_' + ICAS.oSoc.CodFiscal + '_' + StrTran(DToC(Data2), ".", "-") + '_' + lcTipDecl + Iif(tnSegmente>1, "_p"+AllTrim(Str(tnSegmente, 1, 0)), "") + '.xml'
	*
	Return lcFiserD406
	*
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________	
Function Tabela_Erori_Saft(tcParam1)
	*
	&& TREBUIE SA LASI CREAREA TABELEI DAR SA STERGI PARTEA CU CREAREA SI AFISAREA ERORII (brow / wordpad)
	*
	If tcParam1='Creare_Tabela_Erori'		
		Select;
			Cast( '' As  C(100))	As Sectiune,;
			Cast( '' As C(254))		As Erori_User;
		From dbf\PlanCont; 
		Where;
			0=9;
		Into Cursor Erori_Saft ReadWrite
	EndIf
	*
	*
	***1. Utilizare incorecta a sinteticelor de grad I cand au sintetice de grad II
	*  1. Utilizare sintetice cind exista analitice
	*
	If tcParam1='CreareCursoare_C_Conturi_Err' 
		Select C_Conturi_Err
		Scan
		    *Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		   	*Values ('#Utilizare incorecta sintetice:', "Utilizare sintetice cind exista analitice: "+AllTrim(C_Conturi_Err.Cont))
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Sintetic gr.I care are sintetic gr.II:', "Utilizare incorecta a sinteticelor de grad I cand au sintetice de grad II: "+AllTrim(C_Conturi_Err.Cont))
		EndScan
	EndIf
	*
	*2. Lipsa localitate, cont... 
	IF tcParam1='CreareCursoare_Lipsa_Coloane'
		Select Lipsa_Coloane
		Scan
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    	Values ('#Lipsa_Coloane:', AllTrim(Lipsa_Coloane.Cont)+' '+AllTrim(Lipsa_Coloane.Denumire)+' - '+AllTrim(Lipsa_Coloane.Erori))
		EndScan
	EndIf
	*
	*3. Lipsa lipsa clienti in customers (lipsa cod fiscal/cnp)...... 
	If tcParam1='Customers'
		Select Lipsa_CIF_Customers
		Scan
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);

		Select ;
       		Cast( StrExtract( StrExtract( g.TransactionDescription, '{', '}'), ',') As C(30)) As Cont_Expl,;
		    g.JournalId,;
		    g.Id_Nota,;
		    g.Ndp,;
		    g.TransactionDate,;
		    g.TransactionDescription,;
		    g.StandardAccountID,;
		    g.DebitCreditIndicator,;
		    g.Cont,;
		    g.ContCorespondent,;
		    g.CeCont,;
		    g.TaxDCA,; 
		    t.Denumire;
		From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii g;
		Inner Join (Select Cod_Fiscal, Denumire From Terti Group By Cod_Fiscal) t On g.Cod_Fiscal=t.Cod_Fiscal;
		Where;
			Iif(;
				m.NumeDB<>"ecuaqua",;
				InList(JournalId, 'Intrari', 'Iesiri','Import','Export'),;
				InList(JournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import','Export');
				);

					Atc( Lower('Valoare intrare'	), TransactionDescription)>0;

					Or;

					Or;
					Atc( Lower('Val. intr.'			),	TransactionDescription)>0;
				);
			And PadR(AllTrim(Cont)            ,20,'0') <> PadR(AllTrim( StrExtract( StrExtract( TransactionDescription, '{', '}'), ',')), 20, '0');
			And PadR(AllTrim(ContCorespondent),20,'0') <> PadR(AllTrim( StrExtract( StrExtract( TransactionDescription, '{', '}'), ',')), 20, '0');
		Group By TransactionDescription, Id_Nota;
		Into Cursor Verif_Expl_Cont

			Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);

				    "Corectam ?",4+64+256, "Info...")=6

				*
				Scatter MemVar
				lcSearchExpl =	','+AllTrim(m.Cont_Expl)+"}"

		From cGeneralLedgerEntries;
		Where;
			( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Categorie,'')))>0 And Nvl(IdCategorie,0)=0 );
			Or;
			( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Categorie,'')))=0 And Nvl(IdCategorie,0)>0 );

		    *
		    cTipEroare = ' #Completat partial IdCategorie/Categorie (realegeti categoria si resalvati operatiunea)'
	    	*
