		    Values ('#GeneralLedgerEntries:',;
		    	        					+ AllTrim(Erori_Categorie.JournalId)		+;
		    	        ', '				+ DToC(Erori_Categorie.TransactionDate)		+;
		    	        ', Doc: '			+ AllTrim(Erori_Categorie.Ndp)				+;
		    	        ', Cont: '			+ AllTrim(Erori_Categorie.ContD)			+;
		    	        ', ContCores.: '	+ AllTrim(Erori_Categorie.ContC)			+;
		    	        ', Suma: '			+ AllTrim(Str(Erori_Categorie.Suma,10,2))	+;
		    	        ', CodTva: '		+ AllTrim(Erori_Categorie.CodTva)			+;
		    	        ', Categorie: '		+ AllTrim(Nvl(Erori_Categorie.Categorie, ''))		+;
		    	        ', IdCategorie: '	+ AllTrim(Str(Nvl(Erori_Categorie.IdCategorie,0)))	+;
		    	        cTipEroare)
		EndScan
		*
		&& Nicc.26.09.2023
		*
		Select Erori_Categorie
		Locate
		lnMonth = Month(Data1)  &&& Erori_Categorie.Period
		lnYear  = Year(Data1)   &&& Erori_Categorie.PeriodYear
		Count To;
			lnCategorie_Nu_Trebuia;
		For;
				Nvl(Categorie, Space(1))<> Space(1);
			And	Not Inlist(Left(ContD, 1), '6', '7');
			And	Not Inlist(Left(ContC, 1), '6', '7');
			And	Year(TransactionDate)	= lnYear;
			And	Month(TransactionDate)	= lnMonth
			
		*If lnCategorie_Nu_Trebuia=0 or lnMonth=0 
		*	Set Step On
		*EndIf
		
		If lnCategorie_Nu_Trebuia>0;
				And MessageBox("Exista inregistrari pt. care s-a ales completat campul [Categorie] dar nu exista un cont din clasa 6 sau 7 pe debit/credit."+Chr(13)+;
				"Corectam ?",4+64+256,"Info...")=6
			*
			Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge			
				Update Registru
				Set
						Categorie	= ''
					,	IdCategorie	= Null
				Where
						Sters					= 0
					And IsNull(IdCategorie, 0)	= 0
					And IsNull(Categorie, '')	<> ''
					And Left(ContD, 1) Not In	('6', '7')
					And Left(ContC, 1) Not In	('6', '7')
					And Month(Data)	= ?lnMonth
					And Year(Data)	= ?lnYear
			EndText
			mySQLExec(lcSQL)
			*						
		EndIf	
		*
		&& Acelasi lucru pt Categorie completata fara IdCategorie si NC pe 6 sau 7 pe D sau C
		*
		Select Erori_Categorie
		Locate
		Count To lnCategorie_Fara_ID;
		For;
				Nvl(Categorie, Space(1))<> Space(1);	
			And NVL(IdCategorie, 0) = 0;
			And ;
			(;
				Inlist(Left(ContD, 1), '6', '7');
			Or;
				Inlist(Left(ContC, 1), '6', '7');
			);
			And Year(TransactionDate)	= lnYear;
			And Month(TransactionDate)	= lnMonth
		*
		If lnCategorie_Fara_ID>0;
				And MessageBox("Exista inregistrari cu campul [Categorie] completat dar campul [IdCategorie] este necompletat."+Chr(13)+;
				"Corectam ?",4+64+256,"Info...")=6
			*
			Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge			
				Update r
				Set
					IdCategorie = c.ID
				From Registru r
				Inner Join Categorii c On c.Denumire = r.Categorie
				Where
					IsNull(r.Sters, 0) = 0
					And IsNull(r.IdCategorie, 0) = 0
					And IsNull(r.Categorie, '')<> ''
					And 
					(
					Left(r.ContD, 1) In ('6', '7')
					Or 
					Left(r.ContC, 1) In ('6', '7')
					)
					And Month(Data)				= ?lnMonth
					And Year(Data)				= ?lnYear
			EndText
			mySQLExec(lcSQL)
			*						
		EndIf
		*
		&& Vicos.28.04.2025
		*
		Select Erori_Categorie
		Locate
		Count To lnCategorie_Fara_ID;
		For;
				Nvl(Categorie, Space(1))<> Space(1);	
			And NVL(IdCategorie, 0) = 0;
			And ;
			(;
				Inlist(Left(ContD, 1), '6', '7');
			Or;
				Inlist(Left(ContC, 1), '6', '7');
			);
			And Year(TransactionDate)	= lnYear;
			And Month(TransactionDate)	= lnMonth
		*
		If lnCategorie_Fara_ID>0;
				And MessageBox("Exista inregistrari cu campul [Categorie] completat dar campul [IdCategorie] este necompletat."+Chr(13)+;
				"Corectam ?",4+64+256,"Info...")=6
			*
			Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge			
				Update r
				Set
					Categorie = c.Denumire
				From Registru r
				Inner Join Categorii c On c.ID = r.IdCategorie
				Where
						IsNull(r.Sters, 0)			=	0
					And IsNull(r.IdCategorie, 0)	=	0
					And IsNull(r.Categorie, '')		<>	''
					And 
						(
						Left(r.ContD, 1) In ('6', '7')
						Or 
						Left(r.ContC, 1) In ('6', '7')
						)
					And Month(Data)					= ?lnMonth
					And Year(Data)					= ?lnYear
			EndText
			mySQLExec(lcSQL)
			*						
		EndIf
		*
		&& Vicos.28.04.2025
		*	
	EndIf 
	*
	*9. Lipsa cod fiscal in TERTI
	If tcParam1='TERTI_LIPSA_CIF' 
		Select TERTI_LIPSA_CIF
		Scan
	    cTipEroare = ' #CIF_Terti necompletat:'
	    	*
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#TERTI SAF-T Lipsa CIF:',;
		    	        'Cont: '		+ AllTrim(TERTI_LIPSA_CIF.Cont)+;
		    	        ', Denumire: '	+ AllTrim(TERTI_LIPSA_CIF.Denumire)+;
		    	        ', Tara: '		+ AllTrim(TERTI_LIPSA_CIF.Tara)+;
		    	        ', Tip_Tert: '	+ AllTrim(TERTI_LIPSA_CIF.Tip_Tert)+;
		    	        cTipEroare)
		EndScan
		*
	EndIf
	*
	*10. VIES esuat
	If tcParam1='Erori_la_VIES3' 
		Select Erori_la_VIES3
		Scan
	    *cTipEroare = ' #VIES_ERR (verificari VIES esuate) :'
	    cTipEroare = ''
	    	*
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('TERTI SAF-T #VIES_ERR:',;
		    	        'Cont: '		+ AllTrim(Erori_la_VIES3.Cont)+;
		    	        ', Denumire: '	+ AllTrim(Erori_la_VIES3.Denumire)+;
		    	        ', Tara: '		+ AllTrim(Erori_la_VIES3.Tara)+;
		    	        ', Tip_Tert: '	+ AllTrim(Erori_la_VIES3.Tip_Tert)+;
		    	        cTipEroare)
		EndScan
		*
	EndIf
	*
	*11. DubluriConturiDupaMapareSaft
	If tcParam1='DubluriConturiDupaMapareSaft' 
		Select AcelasiCont
		Scan
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Conturi dublate dupa mapare conform SAFT:',;
		    	        'Cont: '+ AllTrim(AcelasiCont.StandardAccountID)+;
		    	        '- '	+ AllTrim(AcelasiCont.AccountDescription);
		    	   )
		EndScan
		*
	EndIf
	*
	*12. Balanta_Versus_GLA
	If tcParam1='Balanta_Versus_GLA' 	
		Scan
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Diferente SI/SF balanta versus ICAS:',;
		    	        'Cont SAFT: ' + Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Diferente_Solduri.Cont_Saft,'')))=0, '...', AllTrim(Nvl(Diferente_Solduri.Cont_Saft,'')) )+;
		    	        ' Cont_ICAS: '+ Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Diferente_Solduri.Cont_icas,'')))=0, '...', AllTrim(Nvl(Diferente_Solduri.Cont_icas,'')) );
		    	   )
		EndScan
		*
	EndIf
	*
	*13. Mijloace fixe
	If tcParam1='MijloaceFixe' 	
		*
		Select m.NrInv, m.Denumire, IdMF ;
		From Mf_unice m;
		Inner Join ( Select NrInv, Count(NrInv) As Dubluri From Mf_Unice Having Dubluri>1 Group By NrInv ) u;
				   On u.NrInv = m.NrInv;
		Into Cursor Dubluri_MF 	   
		*
		Scan 
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Mijloace fixe - numar inventar dublate:',;
		    	     'nr.inv.: '+AllTrim(Str(Dubluri_MF.NrInv))+' - ' + 'IdMF: ' + Transform(Dubluri_MF.IdMF) + ' - ' + AllTrim(Dubluri_MF.Denumire);
		    	   )
		EndScan
		*		
		Select Mf_Unice
		*
		Scan For Len(AllTrim(Nvl(CodCls,'')))=0 
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Mijloace fixe - lipsa cod clasificare:',;
		    	     'nr.inv.: '+ AllTrim(Str(Mf_Unice.NrInv))+' - ' + 'IdMF: ' + Transform(Mf_Unice.IdMF) + ' - ' + AllTrim(Mf_Unice.Denumire);
		    	   )
		EndScan
		*
		lAutoCorrection = .F.
		Locate For ValActual = 0
		*
		If Found()
			lAutoCorrection = MessageBox('Incercam sa calculam valoarea actuala pentru imobilizarile la care aceasta este 0(zero)?', 4+256+32, 'Assets')=6
		EndIf
		*
		Scan For ValActual=0
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Mijloace fixe - valoare totala este zero:',;
		    	     'nr.inv.: '+ AllTrim(Str(Mf_Unice.NrInv))+' - ' + 'IdMF: ' + Transform(Mf_Unice.IdMF) + ' - ' + AllTrim(Mf_Unice.Denumire);
		    	   )
		    *
		    If lAutoCorrection
				mySQLExec( "Update MijloaceFixe Set ValActual= ValRam + AmortPl	Where ValActual=0 And Id  = ?Mf_Unice.IdMF" )
				mySQLExec( "Update Amortizari   Set ValActual= ValRam + AmortPl	Where ValActual=0 And IdMF= ?Mf_Unice.IdMF" )
				mySQLExec( "Update MijloaceFixe Set ValActual= Val Where ValRam + AmortPl = 0 And ValActual=0 And Id  = ?Mf_Unice.IdMF" )
				mySQLExec( "Update Amortizari   Set ValActual= Val Where ValRam + AmortPl = 0 And ValActual=0 And IdMF= ?Mf_Unice.IdMF" )
			EndIf
			*
		EndScan
		*
		Scan For Val<0 Or ValActual<0  Or ValRam<0 Or AmortPl<0
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Mijloace fixe - valori negative:',;
		    	     		    	     'nr.inv.: '+ AllTrim(Str(Mf_Unice.NrInv))+' - ' + 'IdMF: ' + Transform(Mf_Unice.IdMF) + ' - ' + AllTrim(Mf_Unice.Denumire)+' '+;
		    	       Iif( Val<0      , 'Valoare intrare: '+ AllTrim(Str(Val      ,15,2)), '')+;
		    	       Iif( ValActual<0, 'Valoare totala: ' + AllTrim(Str(ValActual,15,2)), '')+;
		    	       Iif( ValRam <0  , 'Valoare ramasa: ' + AllTrim(Str(ValRam   ,15,2)), '');
		    	   )
		EndScan
		*
		Sele AmortizariDetaliatAn
		Scan For Valoare=0
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Mijloace fixe - valoare zero:',;
		    	     'nr.inv.:'+ AllTrim(Str(AmortizariDetaliatAn.NrInv))+' - '  + 'IdMF: ' + Transform(AmortizariDetaliatAn.Id_MijlocFix) + AllTrim(AmortizariDetaliatAn.Den_Amortizari)+' '+ AllTrim(AmortizariDetaliatAn.Denumire)+'=0';
		    	   )
		EndScan
		*
	EndIf
*!*		*14. CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare
*!*		If tcParam1='CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare' 	
*!*			Select CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare
*!*			Scan 
*!*			    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
*!*			    Values ('#Plata/Incasare cote multiple tva incasare:',;
*!*			    	        'Data: ' + Transform(CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare.Data)+' suma:' + AllTrim(Str(CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare.suma,15,2))+;
*!*			    	        ' '+AllTrim(CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare.ContD)+' ' +AllTrim(CoteTvaMultiple_TvaIncasare.TransactionDescription); 
*!*			    	   )
*!*			EndScan
*!*			*
*!*		EndIf
	*		
	*15. Cont_CF_Eronat
	If tcParam1='Cont_CF_Eronat' 	
		Select Cont_CF_Eronat
		Scan 
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
		    Values ('#Cod fiscal eronat:',;
		    		'Cont: ' + AllTrim(Cont_CF_Eronat.Cont)+' ' + AllTrim(Cont_CF_Eronat.Denumire)+ ' CF: ' +AllT(Cont_CF_Eronat.Cod_Fiscal) ) 
		EndScan
		*
	EndIf
	*
	*16. Stocuri
	If tcParam1='Stocuri' 	
		If lcTipDeclaratie = 'C'
			***  MovementTypeTable
			If RecCount('MovementTypeTable')=0
			    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
			    Values ('#Stocuri:', 'Nu aveti date in MovementTypeTable.' ) 				
			Else			
				Select MovementTypeTable
				Scan For Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementType,'')))=0 Or Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Description,'')))=0
				    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
				    Values ( '#Stocuri:',;
				    		 "MovementTypeTable inregistrarea "+AllTrim(Str(RecNo('MovementTypeTable')))+ ": "+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementTypeTable.MovementType,'')))=0, +" lipsa tip operatiune.", "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementTypeTable.Description,'')))=0 , +" lipsa descriere"		 , "");
				    	   )
				EndScan
			EndIf
			*** Products
			If RecCount('crsProducts')=0
			    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
			    Values ('#Stocuri:', 'Nu aveti date in Products.' ) 				
			Else			
				Select crsProducts
				Scan For Len(AllTrim(Nvl(ProductCode,'')))=0 Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Description,'')))=0 Or;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(UomBase,'')))=0     Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(UomStandard,'')))=0 Or;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(UomToUomBaseConversionFactor,'')))=0 Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(ProductCommodityCode,'')))=0 
					*	 							    
				    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
				    Values ( '#Stocuri:',;
				    		 "Products inregistrarea "+AllTrim(Str(RecNo('MovementTypeTable')))+ ": "+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(crsProducts.ProductCode,'')))=0, +" lipsa cod produs.", "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(crsProducts.Description,'')))=0, +" lipsa descriere." , "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(crsProducts.UomBase,'')))=0	  , +" lipsa um." 		, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(crsProducts.UomStandard,'')))=0, +" lipsa um standard.","")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(crsProducts.UomToUomBaseConversionFactor,'')))=0, +" lipsa um secundar.", "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(crsProducts.ProductCommodityCode,'')))=0	  	   , +" lipsa clasificarea pentru import / export.", "");
				    	   )
				EndScan
			EndIf
			*** PhysicalStock
			If RecCount('PhysicalStock')=0
			    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
			    Values ('#Stocuri:', 'Nu aveti date in PhysicalStock.' ) 				
			Else			
				Select PhysicalStock
				Scan For Len(AllTrim(Nvl(WareHouseID,'')))=0 					Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(ProductCode,'')))=0 					Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(ProductType,'')))=0    				Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(OwnerID,'')))=0 						Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(UomPhysicalStock,'')))=0 				Or ;
						 Nvl(UomToUomBaseConversionFactor,'')=0 				Or ;
						 Nvl(UnitPrice,0)=0  									Or ;
						 OpeningStockQuantity=null  							Or ;
						 OpeningStockValue=null  								Or ;
						 ClosingStockQuantity=null  							Or ;
						 ClosingStockValue=null 
					*	 							    
				    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
				    Values ( '#Stocuri:',;
				    		 "PhysicalStock inregistrarea "+AllTrim(Str(RecNo('PhysicalStock')))+ ": "+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(PhysicalStock.WareHouseID,'')))=0, 				 +" lipsa gestiune."					, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(PhysicalStock.ProductCode,'')))=0, 				 +" lipsa cod produs." 					, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(PhysicalStock.ProductType,'')))=0, 				 +" lipsa tip produs."					, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(PhysicalStock.OwnerID,'')))=0, 					 +" lipsa proprietar."					, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(PhysicalStock.UomPhysicalStock,'')))=0,			 +" lipsa um."							, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Nvl(PhysicalStock.UomToUomBaseConversionFactor,'')=0,			 +" lipsa um secundar."					, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Nvl(PhysicalStock.UnitPrice,'')=0,								 +" lipsa pret."						, "")+;
				    		  Iif( PhysicalStock.OpeningStockQuantity=null,							 +" lipsa stoc inceput."				, "")+;
				    		  Iif( PhysicalStock.OpeningStockValue=null, 							 +" lipsa valoare stoc inceput."		, "")+;
				    		  Iif( PhysicalStock.ClosingStockQuantity=null, 						 +" lipsa stoc sfarsit."				, "")+;
				    		  Iif( PhysicalStock.ClosingStockValue=null, 							 +" lipsa valoare stoc sfarsit."		, "");
				    	   )
				EndScan
			EndIf
			***  MovementOfGoods
			If RecCount('MovementOfGoods')=0
			    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
			    Values ('#Stocuri:', 'Nu aveti date in MovementOfGoods.' ) 				
			Else			
				Select MovementOfGoods
				Scan For NumberOfMovementLines=null					Or ;
						 TotalQuantityReceived=null					Or ;
						 TotalQuantityIssued=null					Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementReference,'')))=0 	Or ;
						 Nvl(MovementDate,{//})={//}				Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementType,'')))=0 		Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(DocumentType,'')))=0    	Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(DocumentNumber,'')))=0 	Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(LineNumber,'')))=0 		Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(AccountID,'')))=0 			Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(CustomerID,'')))=0 		Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(SupplierID,'')))=0 		Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(ProductCode,'')))=0 		Or ;
						 Quantity=null  							Or ;
 						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(UnitOfMeasure,'')))=0 		Or ;
						 UomToUomPhysicalStockConversion=null 		Or ;
						 Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementSubType,'')))=0 	
					*	 							    
				    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
				    Values ( '#Stocuri:',;
				    		 "MovementOfGoods inregistrarea " +AllTrim(Str(RecNo('MovementOfGoods')))+ ": "+;
				    		  Iif( MovementOfGoods.NumberOfMovementLines=null, 				 	+" lipsa nr. operatiuni."			, "")+;
				    		  Iif( MovementOfGoods.TotalQuantityReceived=null	, 				+" lipsa cantitate intrata." 		, "")+;
				    		  Iif( MovementOfGoods.TotalQuantityIssued=null, 				 	+" lipsa cantitate iesita."			, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.MovementReference,'')))=0,  	+" lipsa identificator operatiune."	, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Nvl(MovementOfGoods.MovementDate,{//})={//},				 	+" lipsa data operatiune."			, "")+; 
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.MovementType,'')))=0, 		+" lipsa tip operatiune."			, "")+; 
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.DocumentType,'')))=0, 		+" lipsa nume operatiune."			, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.DocumentNumber,'')))=0 ,	 	+" lipsa numar document."			, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.LineNumber,'')))=0,			+" lipsa linie per document."		, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.AccountID,'')))=0,			+" lipsa cont produs."				, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.CustomerID,'')))=0, 			+" lipsa cod client."				, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.SupplierID,'')))=0 ,			+" lipsa cod furnizor."				, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.ProductCode,'')))=0 ,		+" lipsa cod produs."				, "")+;
				    		  Iif( MovementOfGoods.Quantity=null  ,		 						+" cantitate nedefinita."			, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.UnitOfMeasure,'')))=0  ,		+" lipsa clasificare um."			, "")+;
				    		  Iif( MovementOfGoods.UomToUomPhysicalStockConversion=null ,		+" lipsa um secundar."				, "")+;
				    		  Iif( Len(AllTrim(Nvl(MovementOfGoods.MovementSubType,'')))=0 ,	+" lipsa tip operatiune."			, "");
				    	   )
				EndScan
				*	
			EndIf
			*
			***  verific CustomersId/SupplierID (cod fiscal) din MovementOfGoods sa existe in GLA
			Select CustomerId As RegistrationNumber, Partener From MovementOfGoods Where Len(AllTrim(Nvl(CustomerID,''))) > 1 Group By 1;
			Union All;
			Select SupplierId As RegistrationNumber, Partener From MovementOfGoods Where Len(AllTrim(Nvl(SupplierID,''))) > 1 Group By 1;
			Into Cursor Tert_Stocuri
							 
			Select c.RegistrationNumber, c.Partener ;
				From Terti t;
				Right join ( Select RegistrationNumber, Partener From Tert_Stocuri Group By RegistrationNumber) c ;
					On c.RegistrationNumber = t.RegistrationNumber ;
				Into Cursor Lipsa_Tert_Stocuri
			*
			Scan 
				*	 							    
			    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User);
			    Values ( '#Stocuri:',;
			    		 "Customers/Supplier din MovementOfGoods nu exista in Customers/Supplier : "+;
			    		  AllTrim(Lipsa_Tert_Stocuri.RegistrationNumber) +" - "+ AllTrim(Lipsa_Tert_Stocuri.Partener) )
			EndScan
			*
		EndIf
		*
	EndIf
	*	
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________	
Function GetSAFTPaymentMechanismCode(tcCont)
	#if .f.
	Se vor utiliza codificarile din coloana ”Code for Payment Mechanism” 
	pentru completarea câmpului ”PaymentMechanism” din PaymentSettlement si ”PaymentMechanism” din InvoiceSettlement
	#endif
	Do Case
		Case InList(tcCont, ICAS.oConturi.Casa, ICAS.oConturi.CasaVal)
			Return "10"
		Case InList(tcCont, ICAS.oConturi.Banca, ICAS.oConturi.Banca2, ICAS.oConturi.Banca3, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal2, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal3)
			Return "48"
		Case InList(tcCont, ICAS.oConturi.c403, ICAS.oConturi.c403)
			Return "20"
    	Case InList(tcCont, "427", "4282")
        	Return "8"
		Case InList(tcCont, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi2, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi3, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal2, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal3)
			Return "97"
		OtherWise
			Return ""
	EndCase
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetSAFTPaymentCode(tcContD, tcContC)
	#if .f.
	Se vor utiliza codificarile (doar codul format din doua cifre) din coloana ”Code used for Payment Method” 
	pentru completarea câmpului ”PaymentMethod” din Payment
	#endif
	*
	Do Case
		*
		Case InList(tcContC, ICAS.oConturi.Casa, ICAS.oConturi.CasaVal) .Or. InList(tcContD, ICAS.oConturi.Casa, ICAS.oConturi.CasaVal)
			lcPaymentMethod = "01"
		*
		Case (InList(tcContC, ICAS.oConturi.Furnizori, ICAS.oConturi.FImob, ICAS.oConturi.FurCred) .Or. InList(tcContC, ICAS.oConturi.Clienti, ICAS.oConturi.CliDeb));
			.And. (InList(tcContD, ICAS.oConturi.Furnizori, ICAS.oConturi.FImob, ICAS.oConturi.FurCred) .Or. InList(tcContD, ICAS.oConturi.Clienti, ICAS.oConturi.CliDeb))
			lcPaymentMethod = "02"
		*
		Case InList(tcContC, ICAS.oConturi.Banca, ICAS.oConturi.Banca2, ICAS.oConturi.Banca3, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal2, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal3, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi2, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi3, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal2, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal3) .Or. InList(tcContD, ICAS.oConturi.Banca, ICAS.oConturi.Banca2, ICAS.oConturi.Banca3, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal2, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal3, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi2, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi3, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal2, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal3)
			lcPaymentMethod = "03"
		*
		Case InList(tcContC, '427', '4282') .AND. InList(tcContD, '421')
        	lcPaymentMethod = "03"
		*
		Case InList(tcContC, "427") .And. InList(tcContD, ICAS.oConturi.Banca, ICAS.oConturi.Banca2, ICAS.oConturi.Banca3, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal2, ICAS.oConturi.BancaVal3, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi2, ICAS.oConturi.Deconturi3, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal2, ICAS.oConturi.DeconturiVal3)
			lcPaymentMethod = "03"
		*
		OtherWise
			lcPaymentMethod = "99"
	EndCase
	*
	Return lcPaymentMethod
	*
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function SameDayPay(tnIdUnicFactura, tdDataDoc, tcJournalId)
	ldDataFactura			= Transform(DToS(tdDataDoc), '@R 9999-99-99')
*!*		lnCursFactura			= ICase(InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Iesiri'), 1, Nvl( SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=Curs From <<tcJournalId>> Where IdUnic=<<tnIdUnicFactura>>") ), 1))
	If InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Iesiri')
		lnCursFactura = 1
	Else
		Select ExchangeRate From crsSalesInvoices Where IdUnic=tnIdUnicFactura;
		Union All;
		Select ExchangeRate From crsSalesInvoices Where IdUnic=tnIdUnicFactura;
		Into Array aGetCurs
		lnCursFactura=Iif(_Tally>0, aGetCurs(1), 1)
	EndIf
	*									
*!*		Do Case
*!*			Case InList(tcJournalId, 'Iesiri', 'Export')
*!*				lnTotalInvoice		= ;
*!*					Nvl( SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=Total From Iesiri Where IdUnic=<<tnIdUnicFactura>>") ), 0)+;
*!*					Nvl( SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=Total From Export Where IdUnic=<<tnIdUnicFactura>>") ), 0)
*!*			Case  tcJournalId='Import'
*!*				lnTotalInvoice		= Nvl( SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=TotalLei From <<tcJournalId>> Where IdUnic=<<tnIdUnicFactura>>") ), 0)
*!*			OtherWise
*!*				lnTotalInvoice		= Nvl( SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=Total From <<tcJournalId>> Where IdUnic=<<tnIdUnicFactura>>") ), 0)
*!*		EndCase

	Do Case
		Case InList(tcJournalId, 'Iesiri', 'Export')
			Select Total From crsSalesInvoices Where IdUnic=tnIdUnicFactura Into Array aGetTotalIesiriExport
			*lnTotalInvoice		= aGetTotalIesiriExport(1)
			lnTotalInvoice		= Iif( VarType(aGetTotalIesiriExport)<>'U', aGetTotalIesiriExport(1), 0 )		&&Nic.26.09.2023
		*Case  tcJournalId='Import'
		*	Select Total From crsPurchaseInvoices Where IdUnic=tnIdUnicFactura Into Array aGetTotalIntrariImport
		*	lnTotalInvoice		= aGetTotalIntrariImport(1)
		OtherWise
			Select Total From crsPurchaseInvoices Where IdUnic=tnIdUnicFactura Into Array aGetTotalIntrariImport
			*lnTotalInvoice		= aGetTotalIntrariImport(1)
			lnTotalInvoice		= Iif( VarType(aGetTotalIntrariImport)<>'U', aGetTotalIntrariImport(1), 0 )		&&Nic.26.09.2023
	EndCase

*!*		lnTotalPaymentRon		= ;
*!*								Nvl( SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=Sum(Suma) From NoteFacturi Where IdFactura=<<tnIdUnicFactura>> And Data = '<<ldDataFactura>>'") ), 0)
*!*		lnTotalPaymentVal		= Nvl( SQLReturn( TextMerge("Select [SQLValue]=Sum(Suma) From NoteFacturiValuta Where IdFactura=<<tnIdUnicFactura>> And Data = '<<ldDataFactura>>'") ), 0) && * lnCursFactura 
*!*		lnTotalPaymentRonT		= lnTotalPaymentRon + lnTotalPaymentVal
	
	Select Sum(Suma) From cNF_ALL Where IdFactura=tnIdUnicFactura And Data = tdDataDoc Into Array aGetTotalPaymentRon
	lnTotalPaymentRonT		= IIf(_Tally>0, aGetTotalPaymentRon(1), 0)
	

*!*		If _Vfp.StartMode=0
*!*			Clear
*!*			Activate Screen
*!*			?'tnIdUnicFactura',						tnIdUnicFactura
*!*			?'tcJournalId',							tcJournalId
*!*			?'ldDataFactura',						ldDataFactura
*!*			?'lnCursFactura',						lnCursFactura
*!*			?'lnTotalInvoice',						lnTotalInvoice
*!*			?'lnTotalPaymentRon',					lnTotalPaymentRon
*!*			?'lnTotalPaymentVal',					lnTotalPaymentVal
*!*			?'lnTotalPaymentRonT',					lnTotalPaymentRonT
*!*			?'lnTotalPaymentRonT = lnTotalInvoice',	lnTotalPaymentRonT = lnTotalInvoice
*!*		EndIf

	Return lnTotalPaymentRonT = lnTotalInvoice
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetTaxCodeAchizitiiNeded(tcTipDed, tnProcTva, tlTVaIncasare, tlTaxareInversa, tcTipTert, tlServicii, tcGrupa, tlPartTva, tlAnterior, tlTvaZERO, tlNuTVA)
	*
	If Empty(tcGrupa)
		tcGrupa='A'
	EndIf
	*
	*lnPartTva = Iif(tcGrupa='RA', .F., .T.)
	lnPartTva = tlPartTva
	*
	Select TaxCode;
	From dbf\SaftTVA;
	Where;
			AllTrim(cTva)	== AllTrim(tcTipDed);
		And	pTva			= tnProcTva;
		And tIncasare		= tlTVaIncasare;
		And tInversa		= tlTaxareInversa;
		And AllTrim(Tie)	== AllTrim(tcTipTert);
		And Servicii		= tlServicii;
		And Grupa			= tcGrupa;
		And PartTva			= lnPartTva;
		And Anterior		= tlAnterior;
		And TvaZERO			= tlTvaZERO;
		And NuTVA			= tlNuTVA;
	Into Array aGetTaxCode_A_Ned
	ln_Tally = _Tally
	*
	If ln_Tally>1
		*Wait Window [tcTipDed ]+tcTipDed
		*
		Select *;
		From dbf\SaftTVA;
		Where;
				AllTrim(cTva)	== AllTrim(tcTipDed);
			And	pTva			= tnProcTva;
			And tIncasare		= tlTVaIncasare;
			And tInversa		= tlTaxareInversa;
			And AllTrim(Tie)	== AllTrim(tcTipTert);
			And Servicii		= tlServicii;
			And Grupa			= tcGrupa;
			And PartTva			= lnPartTva;
			And Anterior		= tlAnterior;
			And TvaZERO			= tlTvaZERO;
			And NuTVA			= tlNuTVA;
		Into Cursor MultipleInregistrari
		ShowHideScreen(.T.)		
		Browse Last Title Alias()
		ShowHideScreen(.F.)
	EndIf
	*
	*Return ICase(ln_Tally>1, 'Multiple inregistrari', ln_Tally=1, aGetTaxCode_A_Ned(1), '??????')
	Return IIf(ln_Tally=1, aGetTaxCode_A_Ned(1), '??????')
EndFunc

EndFunc

Function GetTaxCode_Test(tcJournalId, tlStatusTvaIncasare, tcCont, tcCodTva, tcTip_Tert, tnId_Nota, tlVies, tcCeCont, tdDataDoc, tdDataDocReala, tcTaxBaseDescription, tcTipFactura)
	* GetTaxCode_Test('Iesiri', .F., '', '15-16', '1', 0, .T., '701', {31.03.2023}, {31.03.2023}, '', '')
	*	GetTaxCodeAchizitiiNeded(tcTipDed, tnProcTva, tlTVaIncasare, tlTaxareInversa, tcTipTert, tlServicii, tcGrupa, tlPartTva, tlAnterior, tlTvaZERO, tlNuTVA)
	
	If IsNullOrEmpty(tdDataDocReala)
		tdDataDocReala=tdDataDoc
	EndIf	
	*
	Local lSir_Servicii_Intrari
	lcSirBunuri_Intrari = InList(tcCont, '3', '20', '21', '22', '23', '60', '4091', '4093')
	lSir_Servicii_Intrari;
						= InList( Left(AllTrim(Nvl(tcCeCont, Space(0))),2), '62','63','64','65','66','67','68','69', '46')
	lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri;
						= InList( Left(AllTrim(Nvl(tcCeCont, Space(0))), 3), '701', '702', '703', '707', '7583', '419')
	lcTaxCode			= '#TaxCode'

	llAnterior			= tdDataDocReala<tdDataDoc
	lcTipDed			= ICase;
						(;
							Atc( 'tva nedeductibil 50%' , Lower(Nvl(tcTaxBaseDescription,'')))>0,  'N050',;
							Atc( 'tva nedeductibil integral', Lower(Nvl(tcTaxBaseDescription,'')))>0, 'N100',;
							'';
						)
	lnProcTva			= Icase;
						(;
						tcCodTva='6-7',		19,;
						tcCodTva='8-9',		9,;
						tcCodTva='19-20',	5,;
						tcCodTva='10',		0,;
						tcCodTva='12',		0,;
						tcCodTva='14',		0,;
						tcCodTva='15',		0,;
						tcCodTva='16',		0,;
						tcCodTva='17',		0,;
						tcCodTva='18',		0,;
						tcCodTva='15-16',	0,;
						'Eroare Procent Tva';
						)
	llTVaIncasare		= tlStatusTvaIncasare = .T.	And Iif( tnId_Nota=0, .T., Not SameDayPay(tnId_Nota, tdDataDoc, tcJournalId) )
	llTaxareInversa		= ICase;
						(;
						tcCodTva='18',														.F.,;
						tcCodTva='17',														.F.,;
						tcCodTva='12' And tcTipFactura='E',									.F.,;
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='2' And tcTipFactura='T',				.F.,;
						InList(tcJournalId,'Iesiri','Export') And tcTip_Tert='1' And tlVies,.T.,;
						tcTipFactura='T',													.T.,;
						tcTip_Tert='2' And lcSirBunuri_Intrari And llAnterior,				.F.,;
						tcTip_Tert='2' And Empty(tcTipFactura) And  lcSirBunuri_Intrari,	.T.,;
																							.F.;
						)
	*Wait Window [llTaxareInversa ] + Transform(llTaxareInversa)
	*Wait Window [lSir_Servicii_Intrari ] + Transform(lSir_Servicii_Intrari)
	*Wait Window [lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri ] + Transform(lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri)
	
	llServicii			= ICase;
						(;
						tcTipFactura='Y' And tcTip_Tert='2',													.T.,;
						tcCodTva='16' And tcTip_Tert<>'1',														.F.,;
						tcCodTva='15' And Empty(tcTipFactura) And tcTip_Tert='2'  And lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,		.F.,;
						tcCodTva='15' And Empty(tcTipFactura) And tcTip_Tert='2'  And Not lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,	.T.,;
						tcCodTva='15' And tcTip_Tert='3' And lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,								.F.,;
						tcCodTva='15' And tcTip_Tert='3' And Not lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,							.T.,;
						tcCodTva='12' And tcTipFactura='E' And lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,								.F.,;
						tcCodTva='14', 																			.F.,;
						tcCodTva='15', 																			.F.,;
						tcCodTva='12' And tcTipFactura='E' And !lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,								.T.,;
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='1' And Empty(tcTipFactura),								.F.,;
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='2' And tcTipFactura='T',									.F.,;
						tcTip_Tert='2' And Not lcSirBunuri_Intrari,												.T.,;
						tcTip_Tert='2' And tcTipFactura='T' And lcSirBunuri_Intrari,							.F.,;
						tcTip_Tert#'1' And tcTipFactura='T' And lSir_Servicii_Intrari,							.T.,;
																												.F.;
						)
	lcTipTert			= ICase;				&& '', 'E', 'I', 'IE', 'L', 'T', 'TE', 'TEI'
						(;
						tcCodTva='18',																		'T',;
						tcTip_Tert='1' And tlStatusTvaIncasare, 											'T',;
						tcCodTva='16' And tcTip_Tert='3',													'E',;
						tcCodTva='16' And tcTip_Tert<>'1',													'T',;
						tcCodTva='15' And tcTip_Tert='3' And lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,							'IE',;
						tcCodTva='15' And tcTip_Tert='3' And Not lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri,						'E',;
						tcCodTva='12' And tcTipFactura<>'E',												'E',;
						tcCodTva='12' And tcTipFactura='E' And Not lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri And tcTip_Tert='2',	'I',;
						tcCodTva='12' And tcTipFactura='E' And Not lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri And tcTip_Tert='3',	'E',;
						tcCodTva='12' And tcTipFactura='E' And lSir_Bunuri_Iesiri And tcTip_Tert<>'1',		'IE',;
						InList(tcJournalId,'Iesiri','Export') And tcTip_Tert='1' And tlVies,				'T',;
						InList(tcJournalId, 'Iesiri', 'Export');
							And tcTip_Tert='1';
							And Empty(tcTipFactura) And Not llTVaIncasare,									'TEI',; 
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, '', 'n', 'U'),			'TE',;							
						tcCodTva='10' and tcTip_Tert='1' And Empty(tcTipFactura),							'TE',;
						tcTip_Tert='1',																		'T',;
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='2' And tcTipFactura='T',								'TE',;
						tcTip_Tert<>'1' And tcTipFactura='T' And Not llServicii,							'IE',;
						tcTip_Tert<>'1' And tcTipFactura='T' And llServicii,								'I',;
						InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import');
							And tcTip_Tert='2';
							And tcTipFactura='T';
							And lcSirBunuri_Intrari;
							,																				'I',;	
						tcTip_Tert='2',																		'I',;
																											'EROARE tcTip_Tert';
						)
	*Wait Window;
			[llServicii ] + Transform(llServicii) + Chr(13) +;
			[tcTip_Tert ] + Transform(tcTip_Tert) + Chr(13) +;
			[lcSirBunuri_Intrari ] + Transform(lcSirBunuri_Intrari)
	lcGrupa				= ICase;
						(;
						InList(tcJournalId, 'Iesiri', 'Export') And llAnterior, 'RV',;
						InList(tcJournalId, 'Iesiri', 'Export') And Not llAnterior, 'V',;
						InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import') And llAnterior, 'RA',;
						InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import') And Not llAnterior, 'A';
						)
	llPartTva			= ICase;
						(;
						llAnterior,																				.F.,;
						tcCodTva='18',																			.F.,;
						tcCodTva='17',																			.F.,;
						tcCodTva='16' And tcTip_Tert<>'1',														.F.,;
						tcCodTva='15' And Empty(tcTipFactura) And tcTip_Tert='2',								.T.,;
						tcCodTva='15' And tcTip_Tert='3',														.T.,;	
						tcCodTva='14' And Empty(tcTipFactura) And tcTip_Tert='2' And tlVies=.T., 				.T.,;
						InList(tcJournalId,'Iesiri','Export') And tcTip_Tert#'1' And tcTipFactura='E',			.T.,;
						InList(tcJournalId, 'Iesiri', 'Export') And tcTip_Tert='1' And tlVies,					.T.,;
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='2' And tcTipFactura='T',									.F.,;
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='1' And InList(tcTipFactura, '', 'n', 'U'),				.F.,;
						tcCodTva='10' And tcTip_Tert='1' And Empty(tcTipFactura),								.F.,;
						tcTip_Tert='2' And tcTipFactura='T' And lcSirBunuri_Intrari,							.T.,;
						tcTip_Tert='1' And tlStatusTvaIncasare And InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import'),	.T.,; 
						tcTip_Tert='1' And !tlStatusTvaIncasare And InList(tcJournalId, 'Intrari', 'Import'),	.T.,; 
						tcTip_Tert='1' And Empty(tcTipFactura), 												.F.,;
						tcTip_Tert='2' And tcTipFactura='Y',													.F.,;
						tcTip_Tert#'1' And tcTipFactura='T',													.T.,;
						lFirmaTVAI,																				.F.,;
																												.T.;
						)
	*Wait Window [llPartTva ]+Transform(llPartTva)	
	*llAnterior			= tdDataDocReala<tdDataDoc
	llTvaZERO			= ICase;
						(;
						tcCodTva='16' And tcTip_Tert='1',												.F.,;
						tcCodTva='15-16' And tcTip_Tert='1',											.F.,;
						InList(tcCodTva, '10', '12', '15', '16', '17'),									.T.,;
																										.F.;
						)
						
	llNuTVA			= ICase;
						(;
						tcCodTva='18',	.T.,;
										.F.;
						)						
Clear	
Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
GetTaxCodeAchizitiiNeded('<<lcTipDed>>', <<lnProcTva>>, <<llTVaIncasare>>, <<llTaxareInversa>>, '<<lcTipTert>>', <<llServicii>>, '<<lcGrupa>>', <<llPartTva>>, <<llAnterior>>, <<llTvaZERO>>, <<llNuTVA>>)
EndText
	? lcSQL
	Return GetTaxCodeAchizitiiNeded(lcTipDed, lnProcTva, llTVaIncasare, llTaxareInversa, lcTipTert, llServicii, lcGrupa, llPartTva, llAnterior, llTvaZERO, llNuTVA)
EndFunc	
*__________________________________________________________________________________
Procedure Verific_Balanta_Icas_Cu_GLA
	*** verific balanta centralizata din icas cu GLA din saft  (SI db/cr si SF db/cr)
	*
	Select *, 0 As cuAnalitic From Con_Temp Into Cursor Bal_ICAS ReadWrite
	Select Bal_ICAS
	Scan For Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Cont,'')))=3
		Scatter Memvar
		Select;
			 Cont;
		From Bal_ICAS;
		Where;
			Len(AllTrim(Nvl(Cont,''))) > 3 And ;
		    Left(AllTrim(Nvl(Cont,'')), 3) == m.Cont;
		Into Cursor aSintetic_Cu_Analitice
		lnTally = _Tally
		Replace cuAnalitic With Iif( lnTally > 0, 1, 0) In Bal_ICAS
	EndScan
	*
	** ce am aici scot din verificare sa nu mai apara mereu sinteticele de 3 care au analitice
	*Browse Last for cuAnalitic=1
	
	*** am la 401 furnizori platit in plus Si cr = -0.10 mc service 01.2023, am rezolvat doar cr negativ, pt db negativ vom vedea
*!*		REPLACE OpeningDebitBalance  WITH  Abs(OpeningCreditBalance),;
*!*			    OpeningCreditBalance WITH  0.00,;
*!*			    ClosingDebitBalance  WITH  Abs(ClosingCreditBalance),;
*!*			    ClosingCreditBalance WITH  0.00 ;
*!*			for (OpeningDebitBalance=0 AND OpeningCreditBalance<0) OR (ClosingDebitBalance=0 AND ClosingCreditBalance<0);
*!*			in crsGeneralLedgerAccounts	
	*
	Select;
		StandardAccountID			As Cont_saft,;
	    Sum(OpeningDebitBalance)	As SI_DB_saft,;
	    Sum(OpeningCreditBalance)	As SI_CR_saft,;
	    Sum(ClosingDebitBalance)	As SF_DB_saft,;
	    Sum(ClosingCreditBalance)	As SF_CR_saft ;
	From crsGeneralLedgerAccounts;
	Group By StandardAccountID ;
	Order By StandardAccountID ;
	Into CURSOR GLA_grupat ReadWrite
	*      
	Select;
		a.Cont_Saft,;
		a.Si_Db_Saft,;
		a.Si_Cr_Saft,;
		a.Sf_Db_Saft,;
		a.Sf_Cr_Saft,;
	    b.Cont		As Cont_Icas ,;
	    b.Sold_In_D	As Si_Db_Icas,;
	    b.Sold_In_C	As Si_Cr_Icas,;
	    b.Fin_D		As Sf_Db_Icas,;
	    b.Fin_C		As Sf_Cr_Icas,;
	    b.CuAnalitic,;
		Nvl(a.Si_Db_Saft,0) - Nvl(b.Sold_In_D,0)	As Dif_Si_Db,;
		Nvl(a.Si_Cr_Saft,0) - Nvl(b.Sold_In_C,0)	As Dif_Si_Cr,;
		Nvl(a.Sf_Db_Saft,0) - Nvl(b.Fin_d,0)		As Dif_Sf_Db,;
	    Nvl(a.Sf_Cr_Saft,0) - Nvl(b.Fin_c,0)		As Dif_Sf_Cr ;
	From GLA_Grupat a;
	Full Outer Join Bal_ICAS b On a.Cont_Saft = b.Cont;
	Where;
		b.CuAnalitic=0;
	Order By a.Cont_Saft;		
	Into Cursor Verific_Gla		
	*	
	Erase lcDirectorSAFT+'diferente_si_sf_intre_GLA_si_bal_centralizata.xls'

	Select * From Verific_Gla Where Dif_si_db<>0 or Dif_si_cr<>0 or Dif_sf_db<>0 or Dif_sf_cr <>0 Into cursor Diferente_Solduri
	If RecCount('Diferente_Solduri')>0
		cFisierXls = lcDirectorSAFT + 'diferente_si_sf_intre_GLA_si_bal_centralizata'
		Copy To cFisierXls type xls
		__Consola_Timing( "Saft.prg : diferente icas-saft la rulaj luna...  pot fi diferente, sintetice cu analitice care in balanta exista dar Saft-ul nu le solicita..." )
	EndIf		
	*
EndProc
*__________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetRegimFiscal(tdData1)	HelpString 'NOMENCLATOR REGIMURI FISCALE'
	#If .F.
		Codificarile de mai jos vor fi utilizate pentru completarea câmpului TaxType din TaxIDStructure.
		Nomenclator pentru definirea regimurilor fiscale din punct de vedere Tva.
	Cod TaxType utilizat în raportarea SAF-T	Descriere cod (RO)	Code description (EN)
	100010	Persoana impozabila înregistrata în scopuri de TVA
	100020	Persoana impozabila ce aplica regimul special de scutire pentru întreprinderile mici
	100030	Persoana impozabila ce are codul de TVA anulat ex-oficio, în baza art. 316 alin. (11) din Legea 227/2015 privind Codul Fiscal
	100040	Persoana impozabila înregistrat? în scopuri de TVA, ce aplic? sistemul de TVA la încasare
	100050	Persoana neimpozabila
		
	#EndIf
	*
	lcTaxType = ''
	*
	If IsNullOrEmpty(Header_Structure.TaxType)
		*
		lnTipTvaCurent = GetModPlataTva(tdData1)
		*
		Do Case
				*
			Case lnTipTvaCurent=3								&& Persoana neimpozabila
				*lcTaxType = '100050'
				lcTaxType = '100020'
				*
			Case IsInVATCollectionSystem(tdData1)				&& Persoana impozabila înregistrata în scopuri de TVA, ce aplica sistemul de TVA la încasare
				lcTaxType = '100040'
				*
			Case lnTipTvaCurent=1 .Or. lnTipTvaCurent=2			&& Persoana impozabila înregistrata în scopuri de TVA
				lcTaxType = '100010'
		EndCase
		*
	EndIf
	*
	Return lcTaxType
EndFunc
*__________________________________________________________________________________
Function Neded_Reg HelpString 'Micsoreaza tva-ul integral pt cele care au tva nedeductibil in GLE_Linii'
	Select;
		Id_Nota,;
		CeCont,;
		ProcTva,;
		IdCategorie,;
		Iif(Occurs('tva nedeductibil 50%', Lower(TransactionDescription))>0, 'N050', 'N100') As Tip_Ded,;
		Sum(TaxAmount);
	From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii;
	Where;
		Occurs('tva nedeductibil', Lower(TransactionDescription))>0;
	Group By 1, 2, 3, 4, 5;
	Into Cursor Neded_Reg
	*
	If _Tally>0
		*
		*			,	Amount		= GetBazaTva(n.Sum_TaxAmount, n.ProcTva);			
		*
		Update g;
		Set;
				TaxAmount	= n.Sum_TaxAmount;
			,	Tip_Ded		= n.Tip_Ded;
		From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii g;
		Inner Join Neded_Reg n On;
					n.Id_Nota 	= g.Id_Nota;
			And		n.CeCont	= g.CeCont;
			And 	n.ProcTva	= g.ProcTva;
			And		n.IdCategorie	= g.IdCategorie;
		Where;
			Occurs('tva nedeductibil', Lower(g.TransactionDescription))=0;
			And g.AccountID = n.CeCont
		*	
	EndIf
	*
EndFunc

				Return '310307'		&& Prestari de servicii intracomunitare care nu beneficiaza de scutire in statul membru in care taxa este datorata (servicii cf reg B2B)
			EndIf

									*--	R03

									*--	R14
									*TODO R14.1 si R14.2
		Case tcCodTva='17'			&& Livrari scutite fara drept de deducere
			Return '310326'			&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii scutite fara drept de deducere
									*--	R15

		Case tcCodTva='21-22'		&& Bunuri/servicii taxabile cu TVA la încasare 19%
			Return '310335'			&& Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 19%
									*--	
			*sau 310309				Daca este incasat	*--	R09	
		Case tcCodTva='23-24'		&& Bunuri/servicii taxabile cu TVA la încasare 9%
			Return '310336'			&& Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 9%
									*--
			*sau 310310				Daca este incasat	*--	R10
		Case tcCodTva='25-26'		&& Bunuri/servicii taxabile cu TVA la încasare 5%
			Return '310337'			&& Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 5%
									*--
			*sau 310311				Daca este incasat	*--	R11
			
		*Case tcCodTva='31-32'		&& Bunuri/servicii taxabile 5% cu TVA la încasare neîncasate de 90 zile
		*	Return '310337'			&& Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 5%
									*--	R09	
		Case tcCodTva='6-7'			&& 19% Bunuri/servicii taxabile
			Return '310309'			&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii taxabile cu cota 19%
									*--	R09
		Case tcCodTva='27-28'		&& Servicii intracomunitare cf. art.278 alin.(1) cu locul prestarii în România
			Return '310320'			&& Vânzari intracomunitare de bunuri la distanta si prestari de servicii de telecomunicatii, de radiodifuziune si televiziune, precum si servicii furnizate pe cale electronica catre persoane neimpozabile dintr-un alt stat membru,  
									*--	R17
		OtherWise
			Return '000000'
	EndCase
	*
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetTaxCode_Achizitii(tcCodTva, nProcTva, cIsPlatitorTVa)
	**********************************************************************************
	***		DACA SE MODIFICA D300 TREBUIE MODIFICAT SI AICI 					   ***
	*** am preluat    100% deductibile											   ***
	***				   50% deductibile											   ***
	***					   nedeductibile                                           ***
	*** NU am preluat: 50% nedeductibile( poate fi inlocuita cu 50% deductibile)   ***
	***				   50% baserate												   ***
	***				   50% not_known											   ***
	***					   baserate												   ***
	***					   not_known											   ***
	**********************************************************************************

	**********************************************************************************************
	*** pe produs in intrari/import **************************************************************
	**********************************************************************************************
	*!*	codtva	proctva				denumire
	*!*	8-9	9	9% Achizitii de bunuri/servicii din tara si importuri de bunuri taxabile
	*!*	10		0	Achizitii bunuri din tara si din import scutite, alte achizitii neimpozabile
	*!*	11-12	0	AIC taxabile
	*!*	13		0	AIC bunuri si servicii scutite
	*!*	14		0	AIC neimpozabile
	*!*	15-16	0	Cumparari pt.care cumparatorul este obligat la plata TVA art. 307
	*!*	19-20	5	5% Achizitii de bunuri din tara taxabile
	*!*	27-28	9	Cumparari pt.care cumparatorul este obligat la plata TVA art. 331 cu cota 9%
	*!*	29-30	5	Cumparari pt.care cumparatorul este obligat la plata TVA art. 331 cu cota 5%
	*!*	31-32	8	8% REGIMUL SPECIAL TVA PENTRU AGRICULTORI
	*!*	6-7		19	19%  Achizitii de bunuri/servicii din tara si importuri de bunuri taxabile
	*!*	6-7		19	Achizitii de bunuri/servicii din tara cu TVA la plata 19%
	*!*	6-7		20	Achizitii de bunuri/servicii din tara cu TVA la plata 20%
	*!*	6-7		24	Achizitii de bunuri/servicii din tara cu TVA la plata 24%

	**********************************************************************************************
	*** D300  achizitii				**************************************************************
	**********************************************************************************************
	*!*	codTva		   	   rand	denumire
	*!*	11-12				5	Achiziþii aic de bunuri pentru care cumparatorul este obligat la plata TVA (taxare inversã) din care:

* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Procedure GetSegments
	Local lnCntTotal, lnCntRegTotal, lnCntJc, lnCntJv, lnCntPayments, lnCntSuppliers, lnCntCustomers, lnCntConturi,;
		lnCntProducts, lnCntPhysicalStock, lnCntStockMovements, lnSegments
	Store 0 To lnCntTotal, lnCntRegTotal, lnCntJc, lnCntJv, lnCntPayments, lnCntSuppliers, lnCntCustomers, lnCntConturi,;
		lnCntProducts, lnCntPhysicalStock, lnCntStockMovements
	Store 1 To lnSegments
	Do Case
		Case InList(lcTipDeclaratie, 'L', 'T')
			Select Count(*) From GLE_GeneralLedgerEntries Into Array aGetCntRegTotal
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aGetCntRegTotal(1))
				lnCntRegTotal = aGetCntRegTotal(1)
			EndIf
			Select Count(*) From SourceDocuments_Payments Into Array aCntPayments
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aCntPayments(1))
				lnCntPayments = aCntPayments(1)
			EndIf
			Select Count(*) From SourceDocuments_PurchaseInvoices Into Array aCntJC
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aCntJC(1))
				lnCntJc = aCntJC(1)
			EndIf
			Select Count(*) From SourceDocuments_SalesInvoices Into Array aCntJV
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aCntJV(1))
				lnCntJv = aCntJV(1)
			EndIf
			Select Count(*) From MasterFiles_Customers Into Array aCntCustomers
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aCntCustomers(1))
				lnCntCustomers = aCntCustomers(1)
			EndIf
			Select Count(*) From MasterFiles_Suppliers Into Array aCntSuppliers
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aCntSuppliers(1))
				lnCntSuppliers = aCntSuppliers(1)
			EndIf
			Select Count(*) From MasterFiles_GeneralLedgerAccounts Into Array aCntConturi
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aCntConturi(1))
				lnCntConturi = aCntConturi(1)
			EndIf
			Select Count(*) From MasterFiles_Products Into Array aCntProducts
			If _Tally>0 .And.  .Not. IsNull(aCntProducts(1))
				lnCntProducts = aCntProducts(1)
			EndIf
			lnCntTotal = lnCntRegTotal+lnCntPayments+lnCntJc+lnCntJv+lnCntCustomers+lnCntSuppliers+lnCntConturi+lnCntProducts
			Do Case
				Case lnCntTotal>500000
					lnSegments = 4
				Case lnCntTotal>300000
					lnSegments = 3
				Case lnCntTotal>200000
					lnSegments = 2
				OtherWise
					lnSegments = 1
			EndCase
	EndCase
	Return lnSegments
EndProc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function TaxCode_GLE_vs_Facturi()
	*
	Select ;
		JournalId,;
		Id_Nota,;
		TransactionDate,;
		TransactionDescription,;
		Ndp,;
		Cod_Fiscal,;
		CodTva,;
		Sum(Iif( Amount>0 And TaxAmount<0, -Amount, Amount )) As Amount,;
		Sum(TaxAmount) As TaxAmount,;
		TaxCode,;
		IsTvaIncasare,;
		Cont,;
		Tip_Tert,;
		Vies,;
		CeCont,;
		TaxPercentage As ProcTva,;
		TipFactura,;
		DataDocReala;
		, Description;
	From cGeneralLedgerEntries_Linii L;
	Where;
		InList(JournalId, 'Intrari','Import','Iesiri','Export');
		And AllTrim(TaxCode)		<> '000000';
		And ( AllTrim(AccountID)	== AllTrim(CeCont);
		And !IsNullOrEmpty(CodTva) );
		And Not InList(TipFactura, 'A', 'B', 'E', 'n');		&&	Nic.26.03.2025 - bon fiscal fara cif, etc  And Occurs('TVA nedeductibil', TransactionDescription)=0;	--Vicos.29.11.2023 CridaPharm
	Group by Id_Nota, TaxCode;		
	Order By TransactionDate, Ndp, TaxCode;
	Into Cursor TaxCode_GLE ReadWrite

*!*		&& Nic.24042025
*!*		Replace All;
*!*			Amount 		With -Amount,;
*!*			TaxAmount 	With -TaxAmount;
*!*		For InList(Cont, '609', '709' ) &&& pt. astea la fel ???  '667', '767' 
*!*		&& Nic.24042025
	

#if .f.	
		And Not InList(TipFactura, 'A', 'B', 'C', 'E', 'n');		&&	Nic.03.10.2023 - bon fiscal fara cif, etc  And Occurs('TVA nedeductibil', TransactionDescription)=0;	--Vicos.29.11.2023 CridaPharm

	Replace TaxAmount With Amount;
	For;
		Occurs('TVA nedeductibil', TransactionDescription)>0;
		And TaxAmount=0;
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(TaxCode)		
	
	#if .f.
	*&& Nic.11.09.2023

					 '')
	*&& Nic.11.09.2023					 
	#endif

	Select ;
		GetWordNum(TipDocument, 2, '.') As TipDocument,;
		IdUnic,;
		InvoiceDate,;
		InvoiceNo,;
		Cod_Fiscal,;
		CodTva,;
		Sum(ICase(Tip_Ded='50', Round(TaxAmount/2, 2), Tip_Ded='100', TaxAmount, TaxAmount)) As Amount,;
		Sum(ICase(Tip_Ded='50', Round(TaxAmount/2, 2), Tip_Ded='100', TaxAmount, TaxAmount )) As TaxAmount,;
		GetTaxCode_Neded(Tip_Ded, ProcTva, Tip_Tert) As TaxCode,;
		IsTvaIncasare,;
		LineAccountId,;
		Tip_Tert,;
		Vies,;
		LineAccountId As CeCont,;

		TipFactura,;

	From crsPurchaseInvoices;
	Where;
		Not IsNullOrEmpty(Tip_Ded);
	Group By IdUnic, TaxCode;
	Union All;
	Select ;
		GetWordNum(TipDocument, 2, '.') As TipDocument,;
		IdUnic,;
		InvoiceDate,;
		InvoiceNo,;
		Cod_Fiscal,;
		CodTva,;
		Sum(Amount) As Amount,;
		Sum(TaxAmount) As TaxAmount,;
		TaxCode,;
		IsTvaIncasare,;
		LineAccountId,;
		Tip_Tert,;
		Vies,;
		LineAccountId As CeCont,;
		ProcTva,;
		TipFactura,;
		DataDocReala;
		, Description;
	From crsPurchaseInvoices;
	Group By IdUnic, TaxCode;	
	Where;
		(Left(SupplierId,2)<>'03' Or Left(SupplierId, 2)='03' And InList(TipImpozit, ' ', '0', '2', '3'));
		And Not InList(TipFactura, 'A', 'B', 'E', 'n');		&& Nic.26.03.2025
	Union All;
	Select ;
		GetWordNum(TipDocument, 2, '.') As TipDocument,;
		IdUnic,;
		InvoiceDate,;
		InvoiceNo,;
		Cod_Fiscal,;
		CodTva,;
		Sum(Amount) As Amount,;
		Sum(TaxAmount) As TaxAmount,;

		IsTvaIncasare,;

	From crsSalesInvoices ;
	Where;
		Iif( m.NumeDB = "ecuaqua", TaxCode<>'310326' And Nvl(TipFactura,'%')<>'n' , 0=0 );		&& Nic.24.11.2023	sa nu verifice penalizarea
	Group By IdUnic, TaxCode;
	Into Cursor TaxCode_Purchase_Sales_TMP ReadWrite

	*		And Not InList(TipFactura, 'A', 'B', 'C', 'E', 'n');		&& Nic.03.10.2023
	*
	** nic 02.04.2024
	* doar la intrari sterg pt. ca nu face contare ( la iesiri face )
***	DELETE FOR Amount=0 AND TaxAmount=0 And TipDocument='Intrari' &&& 2 pozitii factura care se anuleaza reciproc + cu -
**  asta de sus rezolva + -  (fara contare)
**  daca o comentez     + -  (cu contare da eroare)
	Text To lcSQL NoShow TextMerge
		Select
			IdUnic, DataDoc 
		From Intrari
		Where
			IdUnic Not In 
				(
				Select
					Id_Nota 
				From Registru 
				Where
					Fel_D='Intrari'
					And Sters=0
					And Data>=?Data1 And Data<=?Data2 
				)
			And Sters=0				
			And DataDoc>=?Data1 And DataDoc<=?Data2
	EndText
	mySQLExec(lcSQL, [Intrari_Fara_NC])
	*
*	Select TaxCode_Purchase_Sales 
*	Delete For IdUnic In (Select IdUnic From Intrari_fara_NC) 
	
	Select * From TaxCode_Purchase_Sales_TMP Where IdUnic Not In (Select IdUnic From Intrari_fara_NC) Into Cursor TaxCode_Purchase_Sales 
	*
	&&Nic.27.09.2023
	cGroupBy = Iif( m.NumeDB <> "ecuaqua",;
					"g.Id_Nota, g.TaxCode",;
					"p.IdUnic, p.TaxCode";
				  )
	&&Nic.27.09.2023
	*
	*Sum(Iif(InList(p.LineAccountId, '709'), -P.Amount, P.Amount)) As Amount_P,			&& schimbat 22.08.2024
	
	Select ;
		g.JournalId 				As JournalId_g,;
		p.TipDocument 				As TipDocument_p,;
		g.Id_Nota					As Id_Nota_g,;
		p.IdUnic 					As IdUnic_p,;
		g.TransactionDate			As TransactionDate_g,;
		p.InvoiceDate 				As InvoiceDate_p,;
		p.InvoiceNo					As InvoiceNo_p,;
		g.Ndp 						As Ndp_g,;
		g.TransactionDescription	As TransactionDescription_g,;
		g.Cod_Fiscal 				As Cod_Fiscal_g,;
		p.Cod_Fiscal				As Cod_Fiscal_p,;
		g.CodTva 					As CodTva_g,;
		p.CodTva					As CodTva_p,;
		Sum(g.Amount) 				As Amount_g,;
		Sum(P.Amount)				As Amount_P,;
		Sum(g.TaxAmount) 			As TaxAmount_g,;
		Sum(P.TaxAmount)			As TaxAmount_P,;
		g.TaxCode 					As TaxCode_g,;
		P.TaxCode					As TaxCode_p,;
		g.IsTvaIncasare 			As IsTvaIncasare_g,;
		p.IsTvaIncasare				As IsTvaIncasare_p,;
		g.CeCont					As StandardAccountId_g,;
		p.LineAccountId				As StandardAccountId_p,;
		g.Tip_Tert 					As Tip_Tert_g,;
		p.Tip_Tert					As Tip_Tert_p,;
		g.Vies 						As Vies_g,;
		p.Vies						As Vies_p,;
		g.CeCont			 		As CeCont_g,;
		p.LineAccountId				As CeCont_p,;
		g.ProcTva 					As ProcTva_g,;
		p.ProcTva					As ProcTva_p,;
		g.TipFactura 				As TipFactura_g,;
		p.TipFactura				As TipFactura_p,;
		g.DataDocReala 				As DataDocReala_g,;
		p.DataDocReala				As DataDocReala_p,;
		p.Description				As Description_p,;
		g.Description				As Description_g;
	From TaxCode_GLE g;
		Full Outer Join TaxCode_Purchase_Sales P On g.Id_nota=P.IdUnic And g.TaxCode=p.TaxCode;
	Group By &cGroupBy;
	Where;
		p.TipFactura Not In('A');
	Order By g.TransactionDate, g.Ndp, g.TaxCode;
	Into Cursor Verif_TaxCode_GLE_cu_Purchase_Sales_TMP
	*	
	*
	Select *;
	From Verif_TaxCode_GLE_cu_Purchase_Sales_TMP;
	Where;
			Nvl(Amount_g,0)		<> Nvl(Amount_p,0) And Abs(Nvl(Amount_g,0)-Nvl(Amount_p,0))>0.03;
		Or	Nvl(TaxAmount_g,0)	<> Nvl(TaxAmount_p,0);
		Or	Nvl(TaxCode_g,'@@@')<> Nvl(TaxCode_p,'@@@');
	Into Cursor Verif_TaxCode_GLE_cu_Purchase_Sales
	*
	If RecCount('Verif_TaxCode_GLE_cu_Purchase_Sales')>0
*!*			ShowHideScreen(.T.)
*!*			Browse Title '#TaxCode GLE versus Purchase/Sales'
*!*			ShowHideScreen(.T.)
		lcSectiune ='#TaxCode GLE versus Purchase/Sales:'	&& +Chr(13)
		cTipEroare = ''
		Scan
		    cTipEroare = ;
				Iif( Not IsNullOrEmpty(JournalId_g), AllTrim(JournalId_g), AllTrim(TipDocument_p) )+;	    	    	
					' '+'Doc: ' +Iif( Not IsNullOrEmpty(Ndp_g), AllTrim(Ndp_g), AllTrim(InvoiceNo_p) )+;	    
				' / ' + Iif( Not IsNullOrEmpty(TransactionDate_g)	, DToC(TransactionDate_g), DToC(InvoiceDate_p) )+","+;
				Iif(Nvl(TaxCode_g,'^^^')<>Nvl(TaxCode_p,'^^^'),;
					' TaxCode G/F: '+ Iif( IsNullOrEmpty(TaxCode_g), "''", "'"+AllTrim(Nvl(TaxCode_g,'')) +"'")+ ' <> '+;
									  Iif( IsNullOrEmpty(TaxCode_p), "''", "'"+AllTrim(Nvl(TaxCode_p,'')) +"'")+",",;
					'')+;				
				Iif(Nvl(Amount_g,0)<>Nvl(Amount_p,0),;
					' Amount G/F: '+ AllTrim(Str(Nvl(Amount_g,0),10,2)) + ' <> '+AllTrim(Str(Nvl(Amount_p,0),10,2))+",",;
					'')+;
				Iif(Nvl(TaxAmount_g,0)<>Nvl(TaxAmount_p,0),;
					' TaxAmount G/F: '+ AllTrim(Str(Nvl(TaxAmount_g,0),10,2)) + ' <> '+AllTrim(Str(Nvl(TaxAmount_p,0),10,2))+",",;
					'')+;
				Iif( Not IsNullOrEmpty(TransactionDescription_g) And Atc( 'neded', TransactionDescription_g)>0, ' expl: '+AllTrim(TransactionDescription_g),;
					 '')
			*
		    Insert Into Erori_Saft (Sectiune, Erori_User) Values (lcSectiune, cTipEroare)
		EndScan
	EndIf
	*
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetTaxCode_Neded(tcTip_Ded, tnProcTva, tcTip_Tert)
	lcTaxCode='#TaxCo'
	Do Case
		Case tcTip_Ded='50' And tnProcTva=19
			If tcTip_Tert='2'
				lcTaxCode='390301'
			Else
				lcTaxCode='391101'
			EndIf	
		
		Case tcTip_Ded='50' And tnProcTva=9
			If tcTip_Tert='2'
				lcTaxCode='390302'
			Else
				lcTaxCode='391102'
			EndIf	

		Case tcTip_Ded='50' And tnProcTva=5
			If tcTip_Tert='2'
				lcTaxCode='390303'
			Else
				lcTaxCode='391103'
			EndIf	

		
		Case tcTip_Ded='100' And tnProcTva=19
			If tcTip_Tert='2'
				lcTaxCode='350101'
			Else
				lcTaxCode='351101'
			EndIf	
		Case tcTip_Ded='100' And tnProcTva=9
			If tcTip_Tert='2'
				lcTaxCode='350102'
			Else
				lcTaxCode='351102'
			EndIf
		Case tcTip_Ded='100' And tnProcTva=5
			If tcTip_Tert='2'
				lcTaxCode='350103'
			Else
				lcTaxCode='351103'
			EndIf
	EndCase
	Return lcTaxCode
EndFunc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Function GetTaxCode_TVAI_OLD(tnProcTva, tcCont)
	Local Tva_Art As Number
	Tva_Art = tnProcTva
	lcTip_Ded           = ICase( Atc( 'tva nedeductibil 50%', Lower(Nvl(TaxBaseDescription,'')))>0, 'N050',;
								 Atc( 'tva nedeductibil integral', Lower(Nvl(TaxBaseDescription,'')))>0, 'N100',;
								 '';
							   )					&& de ce nu o folosesc ????
	If tcCont = '4426'
		*
		Do Case
			Case InList(Tva_Art, 19)
				&& D300. NU EXISTA
				&& Achizitii de la furnizori
				&& care aplica TVA la incasare 19%
				lcTaxCode = '301301'
				Do Case
					Case lcTip_Ded='N050'
						lcTaxCode = '391301'
					Case lcTip_Ded='N100'
						lcTaxCode = '351301'
				EndCase
			Case InList(Tva_Art, 9)
				&& D300.NU EXISTA
				&& Achizitii de la furnizori
				&& care aplica TVA la incasare 9%
				lcTaxCode = '301302'
				Do Case
					Case lcTip_Ded='N050'
						lcTaxCode = '000000'
					Case lcTip_Ded='N100'
						lcTaxCode = '351302'
				EndCase
			Case InList(Tva_Art, 5)
				&& D300.NU EXISTA
				&& Achizitii de la furnizori
				&& care aplica TVA la incasare 5%
				lcTaxCode = '301303'
				Do Case
					Case lcTip_Ded='N050'
						lcTaxCode = '000000'
					Case lcTip_Ded='N100'
						lcTaxCode = '351303'
				EndCase
		EndCase
	*	
	Else
		*
		Store .F. To llSameDayPay
		*
		Do Case
			*
			Case InList(Tva_Art, 19)
				If llSameDayPay							&& ??? Vicos.11.09.2023 Nu cred ca este cazul la LIVRARI
					&& d300.R09
					&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii taxabile cu cota 19%
					lcTaxCode='310309'
				Else
					&& D300.NU EXISTA
					** Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 19%
					lcTaxCode='310335'
				EndIf	
				
			*
			Case InList(Tva_Art, 9)
				If llSameDayPay
					&& D300.R10
					&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii taxabile cu cota 9%
					lcTaxCode='310310'
				Else
					&& D300.NU EXISTA
					&& Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 9%
					lcTaxCode='310336'
				EndIf	
			*
			Case InList(Tva_Art, 5)
				If llSameDayPay
					&& D300.R11
					&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii taxabile cu cota 5%
					lcTaxCode='310311'
				Else
					&& D300.NU EXISTA
					** Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 5%
					lcTaxCode = '310337'
				EndIf			
		EndCase
		*
	EndIf
	
	Return lcTaxCode
EndFunc

Function GetTaxCode_TVAI(tnProcTva, tcTabela, tcTip_Ded)
*** Tip_Ded="
*	,Deductibil,
*	100,Nedeductibil integral,
*	50,Nedeductibil 50%"
	*		   )
	If InList(tcTabela,  'Intrari', 'Import')
		*
		Do Case
			Case InList(tnProcTva, 19)
				&& D300. NU EXISTA
				&& Achizitii de la furnizori
				&& care aplica TVA la incasare 19%
				lcTaxCode = '301301'
				Do Case
					Case tcTip_Ded='N050'
						lcTaxCode = '391301'
					Case tcTip_Ded='N100'
						lcTaxCode = '351301'
				EndCase
			Case InList(tnProcTva, 9)
				&& D300.NU EXISTA
				&& Achizitii de la furnizori
				&& care aplica TVA la incasare 9%
				lcTaxCode = '301302'
				Do Case
					Case tcTip_Ded='N050'
						lcTaxCode = '000000'
					Case tcTip_Ded='N100'
						lcTaxCode = '351302'
				EndCase
			Case InList(tnProcTva, 5)
				&& D300.NU EXISTA
				&& Achizitii de la furnizori
				&& care aplica TVA la incasare 5%
				lcTaxCode = '301303'
				Do Case
					Case tcTip_Ded='N050'
						lcTaxCode = '000000'
					Case tcTip_Ded='N100'
						lcTaxCode = '351303'
				EndCase
		EndCase
	*	
	Else
		*
		Store .F. To llSameDayPay
		*
		Do Case
			*
			Case InList(tnProcTva, 19)
				If llSameDayPay							&& ??? Vicos.11.09.2023 Nu cred ca este cazul la LIVRARI
					&& d300.R09
					&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii taxabile cu cota 19%
					lcTaxCode='310309'
				Else
					&& D300.NU EXISTA
					** Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 19%
					lcTaxCode='310335'
				EndIf	
				
			*
			Case InList(tnProcTva, 9)
				If llSameDayPay
					&& D300.R10
					&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii taxabile cu cota 9%
					lcTaxCode='310310'
				Else
					&& D300.NU EXISTA
					&& Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 9%
					lcTaxCode='310336'
				EndIf	
			*
			Case InList(tnProcTva, 5)
				If llSameDayPay
					&& D300.R11
					&& Livrari de bunuri si prestari de servicii taxabile cu cota 5%
					lcTaxCode='310311'
				Else
					&& D300.NU EXISTA
					** Livrari/ prestari de catre furnizorii care aplica TVA la încasare 5%
					lcTaxCode = '310337'
				EndIf			
		EndCase
		*
	EndIf
	
	Return lcTaxCode
EndFunc

Procedure Teste_Consistenta_ANAF()
	*
	*GLA, Customers, Suppliers, TaxTable, GLE, Payments  nu e cazul de Order
	Select Teste_Consistenta_anaf
	Zap
	*
	Select * From SourceDocuments_Payments Into Cursor Payments_Ordonat ReadWrite
	*
	Select * From SourceDocuments_PurchaseInvoices Order By NrCrt  Into Cursor PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat ReadWrite 	
	Update PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat;
		Set PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat.NrCrt = PurchaseInvoices.NrCrt;
	From PurchaseInvoices;
	Where;
			PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat.InvoiceNo	= PurchaseInvoices.InvoiceNo		;
		And PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat.InvoiceDate= CToD(PurchaseInvoices.InvoiceDate);
		And PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat.AccountID  = PurchaseInvoices.AccountID
	*
	Select * From SourceDocuments_SalesInvoices	Order By NrCrt 	Into Cursor SalesInvoices_Ordonat ReadWrite
	Update SalesInvoices_Ordonat;
		Set SalesInvoices_Ordonat.NrCrt = SalesInvoices.NrCrt;
	From SalesInvoices;
	Where;
			SalesInvoices_Ordonat.InvoiceNo	 = SalesInvoices.InvoiceNo		  ;
		And SalesInvoices_Ordonat.InvoiceDate= CToD(SalesInvoices.InvoiceDate);
		And SalesInvoices_Ordonat.AccountID  = SalesInvoices.AccountID
	*
	Select * From GLE_GeneralLedgerEntries Order By Id_Nota	Into Cursor GLE_Ordonat ReadWrite
	*
	nRotunjireMin = -1.00
	nRotunjireMax =  1.00
	*
	lcSerie_Test	= 'I'
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
    *** 1. Header: City, Country *****
    && ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		1. În sectiunea Header, subsectiunea Header Structure - Company -
		Company Header Structure - Adress - verificarea completarii câmpurilor
		City si Country		
	EndText
    *
    lcSection = '1.Header'
    Select Header_Structure

	If DEBUG_SAFT
		*Wait Window 'trece 1...'
		Replace City With ''
	EndIf 
    *
	If IsNullOrEmpty(City)
	    Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(lcSerie_Test, '1', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '5.1 Address Structure', 'City', 'City', 0)
	EndIf
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT
		Replace Country With ''
	EndIf 
	If IsNullOrEmpty(Country)

	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 12. GLA: OpeningDebitBalance =  OpeningCreditBalance
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		12. În sec.iunea Master File verificarea egalita.ii între totalul soldurilor
		ini.iale ale conturilor debitoare (Opening Debit Balance) .i totalul soldurilor
		ini.iale creditoare (Opening Credit Balance) (mai pu.in conturile din clasele 8 si 9)
	EndText
	*
	Select MasterFiles_GeneralLedgerAccounts
	*
	Sum;
		OpeningDebitBalance, OpeningCreditBalance;
	To ;
	    GLA_SI_Debit, GLA_SI_Credit;
	For;
		Not InList( Left(AllTrim(AccountID),1), '8', '9');
		And Len(AllTrim(AccountID)) = 3							&& 01.10.2024
	*	
    If GLA_SI_Debit <> GLA_SI_Credit
    	*
		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(lcSerie_Test, '12', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '2.1 GeneralLedgerAccounts', '',;

	    * 	   
	EndIf

	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 13. GLA: ClosingDebitBalance =  ClosingCreditBalance

	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge

	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 14. GLA: ClosingDebitBalance,ClosingCreditBalance (luna anterioara) ==  OpeningDebitBalance,OpeningCreditBalance (luna curenta)

	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge

		Balance si Closing Credit Balance) ale lunii sa coincida cu soldurile ini.iale

	*
	cPrimaZiLunaAnterioara  = GoMonth( Data1, -1)
	cUltimaZiLunaAnterioara = Data1 - 1	
	lcFisierSAFTAnterior    = ExpDir;
								+ [Declaratii\D406\];
								+ AllTrim(myMonth(myFirstDay(Month(cPrimaZiLunaAnterioara), Year(cPrimaZiLunaAnterioara))));
								+ AllTrim(Str( Year(cPrimaZiLunaAnterioara  )))+"\";
								+ GetNumeDir();
								+ "\"+;
								'D406_' + ICAS.oSoc.CodFiscal + '_' + StrTran(DToC(cUltimaZiLunaAnterioara), ".", "-") + '_Inf.xml'
	*lcDirectorSAFTAnterior ='D:\ICAS\Declaratii\D406\Mai2024\AVICOLA_DRAGOS_VODA_SA\D406_1921798_31-05-2024_Inf.xml'
	*Wait Window 'lcFisierSAFTAnterior ='+lcFisierSAFTAnterior 
	*
	If File(lcFisierSAFTAnterior)
		*
		Select AccountId, ClosingDebitBalance, ClosingCreditBalance From MasterFiles_GeneralLedgerAccounts Where 0=9 Into Cursor GLA_anterior ReadWrite 
		*
		*** nic 28.05.2025  ecoaqua are fisierul xml mare (217 mb) si uneori spune ca nu are memorie suficienta plus ca nu am nevoie de string la ei, iau din dbf din folderul anterior
		***cStringXML = StrExtract( FileToStr(lcFisierSAFTAnterior), '<GeneralLedgerAccounts>', '</GeneralLedgerAccounts>')
		If m.NumeDB="ecuaqua"  or 	m.NumeDB="SCECOSALSERVICIIOLTENITASRL"

		For contor = 1 To nNrConturi
*!*				<Account>

			 cSectiuneCont 	= StrExtract( cStringXML , '<Account>', '</Account>', contor )

			 cGLA_Debit	    = Val( Nvl( StrExtract( cSectiuneCont, '<ClosingDebitBalance>' , '</ClosingDebitBalance>' ), '0') )

			 Insert Into GLA_anterior( AccountId, ClosingDebitBalance, ClosingCreditBalance );
			 				  Values ( cGLA_AccountID,  cGLA_Debit, cGLA_Credit)
		EndFor
		
		Select;
				a.ClosingDebitBalance			;
			,	a.ClosingCreditBalance			;
			,	c.AccountID						;
			,	c.StandardAccountID As Cont		;
			,	c.AccountDescription			;
			,	c.OpeningDebitBalance			;
			,	c.OpeningCreditBalance			;
			,	Iif( a.ClosingDebitBalance<>c.OpeningDebitBalance, a.ClosingDebitBalance-c.OpeningDebitBalance, a.ClosingCreditBalance-c.OpeningCreditBalance) As diferente;
			,	Cast( '' As L(1)) As IsSintetic	;
		From MasterFiles_GeneralLedgerAccounts c;
		Left Outer Join GLA_anterior a On a.AccountID = c.AccountID;
		Where;
				a.ClosingDebitBalance <> c.OpeningDebitBalance;
			Or  a.ClosingCreditBalance<> c.OpeningCreditBalance;
		Into Cursor dif_GLA_Anterior_curent ReadWrite

										   		 "SF Db anterior (" +AllTrim(Str(m.ClosingDebitBalance,15,2))+") <> SI Db curent (" +AllTrim(Str(m.OpeningDebitBalance,15,2))+;
										   		 ") diferenta: "+AllTrim(Str(m.Diferente,15,2)),;
										   		 "SF Cr anterior (" +AllTrim(Str(m.ClosingCreditBalance,15,2))+") <> SI Cr curent (" +AllTrim(Str(m.OpeningCreditBalance,15,2))+;

	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 15. GLA: ClosingDebitBalance, ClosingCreditBalance in functie de tip=A,P,B
	&& ========================================================================&&
	*	  Activ: 		OpeningDebitBalance-OpeningCreditBalance(GLA) + Debit(GLE)  - Credit(GLE) = ClosingDebitBalance-ClosingCreditBalance (GLE)
	*	  Pasiv: 		OpeningDebitBalance-OpeningCreditBalance(GLA) + Credit(GLE) - Debit(GLE)  = ClosingDebitBalance-ClosingCreditBalance (GLE)
	* 	  Bifunctional: in functie de sold initial (GLA) daca am sold initial debit(Activ) credit(Pasiv)
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		15. În sec.iunea Master File se verifica soldurile finale ale conturilor
		lunii (Closing Debit Balance si Closing Credit Balance) .inând cont de	urmatoarele formule :
		- Activ : Sold ini.ial (Opening Balance din sec.iunea  Master File) + Rulaj debitor (Debit Amount - General Ledger Entries - Entries) -
		          Rulaj creditor (Credit Amount - General Ledger Entries - Entries) = Sold final	(Closing Balance din sec.iunea Master File)
		- Pasiv : Sold ini.ial (Opening Balance din sec.iunea  Master File) + Rulaj creditor (Credit Amount - General Ledger Entries - Entries)-
			      Rulaj debitor (Debit Amount - General Ledger Entries - Entries) = Sold final	(Closing Balance din sec.iunea Master File)
		- Bifunc.ionale se vor urma acelea.i reguli de mai sus, în	func.ie de soldul ini.ial al contului		
	EndText
	*
	Select;
			a.AccountID						;
		,	a.StandardAccountID				;
		,	a.StandardAccountID As Cont		;
		,	a.AccountDescription			;
		,	Cast( .F. As L) As IsSintetic	;
		,	a.AccountType					;
		,	a.OpeningDebitBalance  As SiD	;
		,	a.OpeningCreditBalance As SiC	;				     
		,	Nvl(e.RulajD,0) 	   As RulajD;
		,	Nvl(e.RulajC,0) 	   As RulajC;     
		,	a.ClosingDebitBalance  As SfD	;
		,	a.ClosingCreditBalance As SfC	;
		,	CAST( 0 As N(15,2)) As diferente;     				     	
		From MasterFiles_GeneralLedgerAccounts a;
		Left Join ( Select;
							AccountID;
						,	Sum(Iif( DebitCreditIndicator='D', Amount, 0.00)) As RulajD;
						,   Sum(Iif( DebitCreditIndicator='C', Amount, 0.00)) As RulajC;
					From GLE_Ordonat;
					Group By AccountID;
				  ) e On a.AccountID = e.AccountID;
		Into Cursor dif_GLA_cu_GLE ReadWrite 
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT
		Replace SiD with 100 for InList(cont, '624','627','635','401410','401549','205','208','706','766')
	EndIf
	**
	REPLACE  Diferente WITH 	Icase(  AccountType='Activ',    SiD-SiC + RulajD-RulajC -SfD-SfC,;
										AccountType='Pasiv',    SiC-SiD + RulajC-RulajD -SfC-SfD,;
										Abs(SiD-SiC + RulajD-RulajC) -Abs(SfD-SfC);
								     ) 								     
	*BROWSE FOR diferente<>0 and IsSintetic=.F.
	*Update dif_GLA_cu_GLE Set IsSintetic = AreAnalitic( Cont, .F., 'dif_GLA_cu_GLE')
	Update Dif_GLA_cu_GLE;
	Set;
		IsSintetic = AreAnalitic( Cont, .F., 'dif_GLA_cu_GLE') ;
	Where;
		Not InList( Left(AccountID, 4), '4111', '4118') &&& 05.11.2024 pt. ecoaqua ~29.000 inregistrari statea 30 minute	
	*
	Scan For Diferente<>0 and IsSintetic=.F.
	    *
	    cSi_plus_Rulaj = Icase( AccountType='Activ',  AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiC + dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajC, 15,2)),;
							    AccountType='Pasiv',  AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiC-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiD + dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajC-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajD, 15,2)),;
							    Iif( dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiC>0,;
							 	     AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiC + dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajC, 15,2)),;
							 	     AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiC-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SiD + dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajC-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.RulajD, 15,2));
							 	   );  
						      )
	    cSf 		   = Icase( AccountType='Activ', AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfC, 15, 2 )),;
								AccountType='Pasiv', AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfC-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfD, 15, 2 )),;
								Iif( dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfC>0,;
									   AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfD-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfC, 15,2)),;
								 	   AllTrim(Str(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfC-dif_GLA_cu_GLE.SfD, 15,2));
								   );	   
						 	  )
	   	*
		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(lcSerie_Test, '15', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '2.1 GeneralLedgerAccounts', '',;
							    		'Cont: '+AllTrim(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.AccountID)+' ' + AllTrim(dif_GLA_cu_GLE.AccountDescription)+ ;
										' sold initial+rulaj (' + AllTrim(cSi_Plus_Rulaj)+ ') <>'+;
										' sold final( '         + AllTrim(cSf)+;
										') dif: '+ AllTrim(Str( dif_GLA_cu_GLE.diferente, 15, 2)),;
										dif_GLA_cu_GLE.diferente; 
									  ) 		
	EndScan
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 16. GLE: RulajDB = RulajCR
	&& ========================================================================&&	
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		16. În sec.iunea General Ledger Entries subsec.iunea Entries se verifica
		daca totalul rulajelor debitoare (Debit Amount - General Ledger Entries - Entries) este egal cu 
		totalul rulajelor creditoare     (Credit Amount- General Ledger Entries - Entries)
	EndText
	*
	lcSection = '3. GeneralLedgerEntries'
	Select;
			AccountID;
		,	Sum(Iif( DebitCreditIndicator='D', Amount, 0.00)) As RulajD;
		,	Sum(Iif( DebitCreditIndicator='C', Amount, 0.00)) As RulajC;
	From GLE_Ordonat;
	Group By AccountID;
	Into Cursor GLE_Db_Cr
	*						
	Sum RulajD, RulajC To GLE_Db, GLE_Cr &&& For Not InList( Left(AllTrim(AccountID),1), '8', '9')
	If DEBUG_SAFT
		GLE_Db = GLE_Db -120

	*
    If GLE_Db <> GLE_Cr

			    						"Total rulaj credit ("+ AllTrim(Str(Nvl(GLE_Cr,0), 15, 2))+")" +;
						    			"   Diferenta: "+AllTrim(Str(GLE_Db - GLE_Cr,15,2)),;
						    			GLE_Db - GLE_Cr; 
									  ) 		
	EndIf

	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 17. GLE, Sales, Purchase, Payments  CurrencyAmount = Amount si CurrencyAmount<>'RON' And and  Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode)
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		17. În toate structurile care se referã la Amount/Amount Structure, se	verificã ipoteza ca
		Amount sã fie egal cu Currency Amount doar în situa.ia în care Currency code este RON.
	EndText
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT	
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat 			 SET Amount=100, CurrencyAmount =100, CurrencyCode='EUR', ExchangeRate=1  Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE SalesInvoices_Ordonat 	 SET CurrencyAmount =Amount, CurrencyCode='EUR', ExchangeRate=1  Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat SET CurrencyAmount =Amount, CurrencyCode='EUR', ExchangeRate=1  Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE Payments_Ordonat 		 SET CurrencyAmount =Amount, CurrencyCode='EUR', ExchangeRate=1  Where RECNO()<4
	EndIf
	*
	Select;
			Cast('GLE-'+Type As c(20)) As Tip	;
		,	TransactionDate						;
		,	TransactionDescription				;
		,	StandardAccountID					;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	ExchangeRate						;
		,	Ndp									;
		,  	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As pozitia;
	From GLE_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)=Amount 	;
		And CurrencyCode<>'RON'				;
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode)	;
	Union All;
	Select;
			'Iesiri'							;
		, 	InvoiceDate							;
		,	Description							;
		,	StandardAccountID					;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	ExchangeRate						;
		,	InvoiceNo							;
		, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(NrCrt,10,0)) As Pozitia;
	From SalesInvoices_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)=Amount	;
		And CurrencyCode<>'RON'				;
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode)	;
	Union All;
	Select;
			'Intrari'							;
		, 	InvoiceDate							;
		,	Description							;
		,	StandardAccountID					;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	ExchangeRate						;
		,	InvoiceNo							;	
		, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(NrCrt,10,0)) As Pozitia;
	From PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)=Amount	;
		And CurrencyCode<>'RON'				;
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode)	;
	Union All;
	Select;
			'Plati'								;
		, 	TransactionDate						;
		,	Description							;
		,	AccountID							;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	ExchangeRate						;
		,	Document							;
		,  	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As pozitia;						   
	From Payments_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)=Amount	;
		And CurrencyCode<>'RON' 			;
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode) ;
	Into Cursor dif_Currency_Amount_17

		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(lcSerie_Test, '17', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, lcSubSection, '',;
										AllTrim(m.pozitia)+', ' + DTOC(m.TransactionDate)+ ', doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+;
							    		', cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+', '+;
							    		"Amount (" + AllTrim(STR(m.Amount,15,2)) + ') = CurrencyAmount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.CurrencyAmount,15,2))+;
							    		")  dar CurrencyCode<>'RON' ( " + AllTrim(CurrencyCode)+ ')',;

									  )  	   														
	EndScan
