	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 18. GLE, Sales, Purchase, Payments  CurrencyAmount <> Amount si CurrencyAmount='RON'
	&& ========================================================================&&	
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		18. În toate structurile care se referã la Amount/Amount Structure se verificã ipoteza ca 
	    Amount sã fie diferit de Currency Amount în situa.ia în care Currency code este diferit de RON.
	EndText
	*	
	If DEBUG_SAFT	
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat 			 SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='RON' Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE SalesInvoices_Ordonat 	 SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='RON' Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='RON' Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE Payments_Ordonat 		 SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='RON' Where RECNO()<4
	EndIf
	****
	Select;
			Cast('GLE-'+Type As C(20)) as Tip	;
		,	TransactionDate						;
		,	TransactionDescription				;
		,	StandardAccountID					;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	Ndp									;
		,  	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As pozitia;						   		
	From GLE_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount)	;
		And Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)<>Amount		;
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')='RON'		;	
	Union All;
	Select;
			'Iesiri'							;
		,	InvoiceDate							;
		,	Description							;
		,	StandardAccountID					;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	InvoiceNo							;
		, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(NrCrt,10,0)) As Pozitia;
	From SalesInvoices_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount)	;
		And Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)<>Amount 		;
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')='RON'		;	
	Union All;
	Select;
			'Intrari'							;
		,	InvoiceDate							;
		,	Description							;
		,	StandardAccountID					;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	InvoiceNo							;	
		, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(NrCrt,10,0)) As Pozitia;
	From PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount)	;
		And Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)<>Amount 		;
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')='RON'		;	
	Union All;
	Select;
			'Plati'								;
		,	TransactionDate						;
		,	Description							;
		,	AccountID							;
		,	Amount								;
		,	CurrencyCode						;
		,	CurrencyAmount						;
		,	Document							;
		,  	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As pozitia;						   								   
	From Payments_Ordonat;
	Where;
			Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount)	;
		And Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0)<>Amount 		;
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')='RON'		;		
	Into Cursor dif_Currency_Amount_18
	*
    Scan 
	    Scatter Memv
		*GLE, Sales, Purchase, Payments
	   	lcSection = Iif( m.tip = 'GLE', '3. GeneralLedgerEntries', '4. SourceDocuments')
	   	*
	   	lcSubSection  = ICase(  m.tip = 'GLE'		, ''					,;
		   						m.tip = 'Iesiri'	, '4.1 SalesInvoices'	,;
		   						m.tip = 'Intrari'	, '4.2 PurchaseInvoices',;
		   						m.tip = 'Plati'		, '4.3 Payments'		 ;
		   					 )	
	   	*				 				
	   	Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(lcSerie_Test, '18', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, lcSubSection, '',;
										AllTrim(m.pozitia)+', ' +DTOC(m.TransactionDate)+ ', doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+;
										', cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;
								    	", Amount (" + AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + ') <> CurrencyAmount (' + AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.CurrencyAmount,0),15,2))+")  dar CurrencyCode='RON'",;
								    	0;
									  )  	   														
	EndScan
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	*19. GLE, Sales, Purchase, Payments  Amount = CurrencyAmount * ExchangeRate  si CurrencyAmount<>'RON'
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		19. În toate structurile care se referã la Amount/Amount Structure,
		tranzac.iile în monedã strãinã trebuie raportate corespunzãtor prin indicarea
		Currency Code relevant, astfel încât la Amount sã se completeze valoarea
		denominate în RON a tranzac.iei, iar la CurrencyAmount sã se completeze
		valoarea în moneda strãinã a tranzac.iei.
	EndText
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT	
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat 		SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='EUR' Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE SalesInvoices_Ordonat 	SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='EUR' Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='EUR' Where RECNO()<4
		UPDATE Payments_Ordonat 		SET CurrencyAmount =Amount-100, CurrencyCode='EUR' Where RECNO()<4
	EndIf
	****
	Select;
			Cast('GLE-'+Type As C(20)) as Tip;
		,	TransactionDate					;
		,	TransactionDescription			;
		,	StandardAccountID				;
		,	Amount							;
		,	CurrencyCode					;
		,	CurrencyAmount					;
		,	Ndp								;
		,	ExchangeRate					;
		,   Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) As calc;
		,   Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) - Amount As Dif;
		,  	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As pozitia;						   								   		
	From GLE_Ordonat;
	Where;
			IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount);
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')<>'RON';
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode);
	Union All;
	Select;
			Cast( 'Iesiri' As C(20)) As Tip ;
		,	InvoiceDate						;
		,	Description						;
		,	StandardAccountID				;
		,	Amount							;
		,	CurrencyCode					;
		,	CurrencyAmount					;
		,	InvoiceNo						;
		,	ExchangeRate					;
		,	Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) As calc;
		,	Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) - Amount As Dif;
		, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(NrCrt,10,0)) As Pozitia;
	From SalesInvoices_Ordonat;
	Where;
			IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount);
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')<>'RON';	
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode);
	Union All;
	Select;
			Cast( 'Intrari' As C(20)) As Tip;
		,	InvoiceDate						;
		,	Description						;
		,	StandardAccountID				;
		,	Amount							;
		,	CurrencyCode					;
		,	CurrencyAmount					;
		,	InvoiceNo						;
		,	ExchangeRate					;
		,	Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) As calc;
		,	Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) - Amount As Dif;
		, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(NrCrt,10,0)) As Pozitia;
	From PurchaseInvoices_Ordonat;
	Where;
			IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount);
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')<>'RON';	
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode);
	Union All;
	Select;
			Cast('Plati' As C(20)) As Tip 	;
		,	TransactionDate					;
		,	Description						;
		,	AccountID						;
		,	Amount							;
		,	CurrencyCode					;
		,	CurrencyAmount					;
		,	Document						;
		,	ExchangeRate					;
		,	Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) As calc;
		,	Round(Nvl(CurrencyAmount,0) * Nvl(ExchangeRate,0),2) - Amount As Dif;
		,  	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As pozitia;						   								   						
	From Payments_Ordonat;
	Where;
			IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyAmount);
		And Nvl(CurrencyCode,'RON')<>'RON';	
		And Not IsNullOrEmpty(CurrencyCode);
	Into Cursor dif_Currency_Amount_19
	*
    Scan 
	    Scatter Memv
		*GLE, Sales, Purchase, Payments
	   	lcSection = Iif( m.tip = 'GLE', '3. GeneralLedgerEntries', '4. SourceDocuments')
	   	*
	   	lcSubSection  = ICase(  m.tip = 'GLE'		, ''					,;
		   						m.tip = 'Iesiri'	, '4.1 SalesInvoices'	,;
		   						m.tip = 'Intrari'	, '4.2 PurchaseInvoices',;
		   						m.tip = 'Plati'		, '4.3 Payments'		 ;
		   					 )	
	   	*				 				
	   	Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(lcSerie_Test, '19', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, lcSubSection, '',;
										AllTrim(m.pozitia)+', '+AllTrim(m.tip)+', '+ + DTOC(m.TransactionDate)+ ', doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+;
								   		', cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;
								   		', CurrencyCode="'+ Nvl(CurrencyCode,'')+'"'+;
								   		', CurrencyAmount='+ AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.CurrencyAmount,0),15,2)),;
								   		0;
									  )  	   																									
	EndScan
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 20. GLE: Amount * TaxPercentage/100 				= TaxAmount And Not isnullorempty(TaxCode) ( pt. InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export')
	***	asta a cazut cind am introdus coloane noi ( 9 si 5)	= Amount * TaxPercentage/(100+TaxPercentage) = TaxAmount And Not isnullorempty(TaxCode) ( pt. restul)
	***  		 TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100 + TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100 +TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100 - TaxAmount-Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)-Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0) ( pt. restul)
	&& ========================================================================&&	
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		20. În sec.iunea GeneralLedgerEntries, Tax percentage aferent Tax code
		de TVA aplicat asupra bazei (Debit Amount/Credit Amount) da ca rezultat
		valoarea TVA înscrisa la nivelul DebitAmount/Credit Amount în structura
		TaxInformation la nivelul liniei respective din tranzac.ie.
	EndText
	*	
   	lcSection = '3. GeneralLedgerEntries'
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat SET TaxAmount = 100 Where RECNO()<4
	EndIf	
	*
*!*		Select;
*!*				Type As Tip				;					
*!*			,	TransactionDate			;
*!*			,	TransactionDescription	;
*!*			,	StandardAccountID		;
*!*			,	Amount					;
*!*			,	Ndp						;
*!*			,	ExchangeRate			;
*!*			,	TaxPercentage			;
*!*			,	TaxAmount +	Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0) + Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0) As TaxAmount;
*!*			,	TaxBase					;
*!*			,	TaxCode					;
*!*			,   Cast( ICase( InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export'), '(fara tva)', '(cu tva)') As C(10)) As BazaCalcul;
*!*			,	Iif( InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export') Or (JournalID='Casa' And Atc('Monetar(', TransactionDescription)>0),;
*!*					 Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2),;
*!*					 Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +  Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
*!*					 										Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 );
*!*				   ) As Tva_Calc;
*!*			,	Iif( InList(JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export') Or (JournalID='Casa' And Atc('Monetar(', TransactionDescription)>0),;
*!*					 Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2) -TaxAmount,;
*!*					   Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +;
*!*					   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
*!*					   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 ) ;
*!*					   - TaxAmount-Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)-Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0); 
*!*				    ) As Dif;
*!*			,	Id_Nota;
*!*			, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As Pozitia;
*!*		From GLE_Ordonat ;
*!*		Where;
*!*				Nvl(TaxCode,'000000')<>'000000';
*!*			And (;
*!*					Nvl(TaxPercentage,0)<>0		;
*!*				 Or Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0	;	
*!*				 Or Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0	;
*!*				);
*!*			And (;
*!*					Nvl(TaxAmount,0)<>0			;
*!*				 Or Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)<>0		;
*!*				 Or Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0)<>0		;
*!*			    );
*!*			And Not Between( Iif( 	InList(JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export') Or (JournalID='Casa' And Atc('Monetar(', TransactionDescription)>0),;
*!*									Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2) -TaxAmount,;
*!*									   Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +;
*!*									   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
*!*									   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 ) ;
*!*									   - TaxAmount-Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)-Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0); 
*!*								), nRotunjireMin, nRotunjireMax);							 
*!*		Into Cursor Dif_GLE_TaxAmount_Err ReadWrite
	*
	Select;
			Type As Tip				;					
		,	TransactionDate			;
		,	TransactionDescription	;
		,	StandardAccountID		;
		,	Amount					;
		,	Ndp						;
		,	ExchangeRate			;
		,	TaxPercentage			;
		,	TaxAmount +	Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0) + Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0) As TaxAmount;
		,	TaxBase					;
		,	TaxCode					;
		,   Cast( ICase( InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'), '(fara tva)', '(cu tva)') As C(10)) As BazaCalcul;
		,	Iif( InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Casa', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'),;
				 Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2),;
				 Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +  Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
				 										Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 );
			   ) As Tva_Calc;
		,	Iif( InList(JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Casa', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'),;
				 Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2) -TaxAmount,;
				   Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +;
				   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
				   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 ) ;
				   - TaxAmount-Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)-Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0); 
			    ) As Dif;
		,	Id_Nota;
		, 	'Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As Pozitia;
	From GLE_Ordonat ;
	Where;
			Nvl(TaxCode,'000000')<>'000000';
		And (;
				Nvl(TaxPercentage,0)<>0		;
			 Or Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0	;	
			 Or Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0	;
			);
		And (;
				Nvl(TaxAmount,0)<>0			;
			 Or Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)<>0		;
			 Or Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0)<>0		;
		    );
		And Not Between( Iif( 	InList(JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Casa', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'),;
								Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2) -TaxAmount,;
								   Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +;
								   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
								   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 ) ;
								   - TaxAmount-Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)-Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0); 
							), nRotunjireMin, nRotunjireMax);							 
	Into Cursor Dif_GLE_TaxAmount_Err ReadWrite
	*	
	Delete From;
		Dif_GLE_TaxAmount_Err;
	Where;
		Between( Abs(TaxAmount)-Abs(Tva_Calc), nRotunjireMin, nRotunjireMax);
		And Atc( 'discount', TransactionDescription)>0
	*
    Scan 
	    Scatter Memv
		*
   		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(lcSerie_Test, '20', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '','',;
								    	AllTrim(m.pozitia)+', ' +AllTrim(m.Tip)+', '++DToC(m.TransactionDate)+ ', '+ 'doc: '+AllTrim(m.Ndp)+', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+;
							    		', cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;
							    		', Amount '+AllTrim(m.BazaCalcul) +'('+ AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + ') raportat la TaxPercentage (' + AllTrim(Str(TaxPercentage,15,0))+;
							    		')=('+AllTrim(Str(m.tva_calc,15,2))+ ')'+ ' <> TaxAmount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.TaxAmount,15,2))+ '), diferenta='+ AllTrim(Str(Nvl(Dif,0),15,2)),;
							    		Nvl(Dif,0);
									  )  	   																																
	EndScan
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 21. SalesInvoices: Amount * TaxPercentage = TaxAmount
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		21. În sec.iunea Source Documents Tax percentage aferenta Tax code
		de TVA aplicata asupra bazei (Amount din sec.iunea Source Documents
		subsec.iunea Sales Invoices - Invoice - Invoice Line - Invoice Line Amount) da

			InvoiceDate		;

		,	StandardAccountID;

		,	InvoiceNo		;
		,	TaxPercentage	;
		,	TaxAmount		;

		,	Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2)- TaxAmount As Dif;

			Not Between( Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2)- TaxAmount, nRotunjireMin, nRotunjireMax);

							    		', Amount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + ') * TaxPercentage ('  +AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.TaxPercentage ,0), 15,2))+ ')=('+AllTrim(Str(m.tva_calc,15,2))+')'+;
							    		' <> TaxAmount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.TaxAmount,15,2))+ '), diferenta='+ AllTrim(Str(Nvl(Dif,0),15,2)),;
							    		Nvl(Dif,0);
									  )  	   																																	   
	EndScan
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	*** 22. PurchaseInvoices: Amount * TaxPercentage = TaxAmount

							    		', Amount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + ') * TaxPercentage ('  +AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.TaxPercentage ,0), 15,2))+ + ')=('+AllTrim(Str(m.tva_calc,15,2))+')'+;

							    		Nvl(Dif,0);

	lcSerie_Test= 'II'

	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		Testul 1 : In sectiunea General Ledger Entries se verifica daca la nivel de Transaction
		Line (GL.22), in subsectiunea Tax Information (GL.33), structura Tax Information
		Structure exista Tax Type (S.TI.1) completat cu 300 si Tax Amount Structure/ Amount
		(S.TI.6/ S.AM.1) <0> pentru care a fost completat Tax Code (S.TI.2) = 000000

	Into Cursor dif_GLE_T1

		*
		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(  lcSerie_Test, 'T1', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '','',;
										AllTrim(m.pozitia)+ ', '+ AllTrim(m.tip) + ', '+DToC(m.TransactionDate)+', doc: '+ALLTRIM(m.ndp)+ ', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+;
							    		', cont: '+ ALLTRIM(m.StandardAccountID)+;
							    		', Amount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + '), TaxPercentage ('  +AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.TaxPercentage ,0), 15,2))+;
							    		') TaxAmount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.TaxAmount,15,2))+ ") dar TaxCode='000000'",;
							    		0;
									  )
	EndScan
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	**** T2. GLE:  Amount * TaxPercentage = TaxAmount  pentru TaxCode <>'000000'
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		Testul 2 : In sectiunea General Ledger Entries se verifica daca la nivel de Transaction
		Line (GL.22), pentru Tax Information Structure/ Tax Amount Structure/ Amount
		(S.TI.6/ S.AM.1) <0>, procentul specific codului de taxa completat pentru campul Tax
		Code (S.TI.2) din structura Tax Information Structure, aplicat la Debit Amount/Credit
		Amount (GL.31/GL.32) da ca rezultat suma inscrisa la Tax Information Structure/ Tax
		Amount Structure/ Amount (S.TI.6/ S.AM.1)
	EndText
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat SET TaxAmount = 100, TaxCode='1111111' Where RECNO()<4
	EndIf		
	****
	Select;
			Type As Tip		;
		,	TransactionDate	;
		,	TransactionDescription	;
		,	StandardAccountID		;
		,	Amount					;
		,	Ndp						;
		,	ExchangeRate			;		
		,	TaxPercentage			;
		,	TaxAmount +	Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0) + Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0) As TaxAmount;
		,	TaxType					;
		,	TaxCode					;
		,	Cast( ICase( InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'), '(fara tva)', '(cu tva)') As C(10)) As BazaCalcul;
		,	Iif( InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Casa', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'),;
				 Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2),;
				 Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +  Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
				 										Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 );
			   ) As tva_calc;
		,	ICase( InList( JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Casa', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'),;
				   Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2) -TaxAmount,;
				     Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +;
				     Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
				     Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 ) ;
				     - TaxAmount-Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)-Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0); 
			    ) As Dif;
		,	Id_Nota;			    
		,   Cast('Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As C(20)) As Pozitia;		 
	From GLE_Ordonat g;
	Where;
			Nvl(TaxCode,'000000')<>'000000'	;
		And (;
				Nvl(TaxPercentage,0)<>0		;
			 Or Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0	;	
			 Or Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0	;
			);
		And (;
				Nvl(TaxAmount,0)<>0			;
			 Or Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)<>0		;
			 Or Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0)<>0		;
		    );
		And Not Between( Iif( 	InList(JournalID, 'Intrari', 'Import', 'Iesiri', 'Export', 'Casa', 'Deconturi', 'Note contabile'),;
								Round(Amount * TaxPercentage/100,2) -TaxAmount,;
								   Round(TaxBase*TaxPercentage/100, 2) +;
								   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_9,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_9,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_9* TaxPercentage_9/100, 2), 0.00 ) +;
								   Iif( Nvl(TaxBase_5,0)<>0 And Nvl(TaxPercentage_5,0)<>0, Round(TaxBase_5* TaxPercentage_5/100, 2), 0.00 ) ;
								   - TaxAmount-Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0)-Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0); 
							), nRotunjireMin, nRotunjireMax);					    
	Into Cursor dif_GLE_T2 ReadWrite
	*
	Delete From dif_GLE_T2;
	Where;
		Between( Abs(TaxAmount)-Abs(Tva_calc), nRotunjireMin, nRotunjireMax);
		And Atc( 'discount', TransactionDescription)>0
	*	*
    Scan 
	    Scatter Memv
		*
		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(  lcSerie_Test, 'T2', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '','',;
										AllTrim(m.pozitia)+ ', ' +AllTrim(m.tip) + ', '+DToC(m.TransactionDate)+ + ', doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+', '+;
							    		'cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;
							    		', TaxCode ('+AllTrim(m.TaxCode)+')'+;
							    		', Amount ' +AllTrim(BazaCalcul)+' ('+  AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + ') raportat la TaxPercentage ('  +AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.TaxPercentage ,0), 15,2))+;
							    		') = (' +AllTrim(Str(m.tva_calc,15,2))+ ') <> TaxAmount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.TaxAmount,15,2))+ ') dif='+ AllTrim(Str(m.dif,15,2)),;
							    		m.dif;
									  )
	EndScan 
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	**** T3. GLE:  TaxType='000' and TaxAmount<>0  pentru TaxCode='000000'
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		Testul 3 : In sectiunea General Ledger Entries se verifica daca la nivel de Transaction
		Line (GL.22), in subsectiunea Tax Information (GL.33), structura Tax Information
		Structure exista Tax Type (S.TI.1) completat cu 000 si Tax Amount Structure/ Amount
		(S.TI.6/ S.AM.1) <0> pentru care a fost completat Tax Code (S.TI.2) = 000000
	ENDTEXT
	*

	EndIf

			Type As Tip				;
		,	TransactionDate			;
		,	TransactionDescription	;
		,	StandardAccountID		;
		,	Amount					;
		,	Ndp						;
		,	TaxPercentage			;
		,	TaxAmount				;
		,	TaxType					;
		,	TaxCode					;
		,  Cast('Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As C(20)) As Pozitia;		 		
	From GLE_Ordonat;
	Where;
			TaxType='000'	;
		And TaxAmount<>0	;
		And TaxCode='000000';
	Into Cursor dif_GLE_T3

							    		ALLTRIM(m.Pozitia)+', '+AllTrim(m.tip) + ', '+DToC(m.TransactionDate)+ ', doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+', '+;
							    		'cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;

	****		And D300( rd. 1, 2, 3, 3.1, 4, 13, 14 , 15 )

	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		Testul 8 : In sectiunea General Ledger Entries se verifica daca la nivel de Transaction
		Line (GL.22), in subsectiunea Tax Information (GL.33), structura Tax Information
		Structure, exista Tax Code (S.TI.2) specific livrarilor/prestarilor scutite de
		TVA/neimpozabile/operatiuni supuse masurilor de simplificare declarate in decontul de
		TVA la randurile : 1, 2, 3, 3.1, 4, 13, 14 , 15 pentru care Tax Amount Structure/ Amount
		(S.TI.6/ S.AM.1) <0>
	ENDTEXT
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat SET TaxCode='310301', TaxAmount=11.11 Where BETWEEN(RECNO(), 30, 32)
	EndIf	
	****
	Select;
			g.*		;
		,	s.TTva	;
	From ( 	Select;
					Type As Tip				;
				,	TransactionDate			;
				,	TransactionDescription	;
				,	StandardAccountID		;
				,	Amount					;
				,	Ndp						;
				,	TaxPercentage			;
				,	TaxAmount + Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0) + Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0) As TaxAmount;
				,	TaxType					;
				,	TaxCode					;
				,   Cast('Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As C(20)) As Pozitia;		 						
			From GLE_Ordonat 	;
		) g;
	Inner Join (Select;
						TaxCode;
					,	TTva;
				From SaftTVA ;
 				Where;
 						AllTrim(Ttva)=='01';
 					Or	AllTrim(Ttva)=='02';
 					Or 	AllTrim(Ttva)=='03';
 					Or  AllTrim(Ttva)=='03.1';
 					Or  AllTrim(Ttva)=='04';
 					Or  AllTrim(Ttva)=='13';
 					Or  AllTrim(Ttva)=='14';
 					Or  AllTrim(Ttva)=='15';
 				) s On s.TaxCode=g.TaxCode;
	Where;
			g.TaxAmount<>0;
	Into Cursor dif_GLE_T8	
	*
    Scan 
	    Scatter Memv														
		*
		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(  lcSerie_Test, 'T8', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '','',;
							    		allt(m.Pozitia)++', '+AllTrim(m.tip) + ', '+DTOC(m.TransactionDate)+  ', doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+ ', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+', '+;
							    		'cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;
							    		', Amount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + '), TaxPercentage ('  +AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.TaxPercentage ,0), 15,2))+;

							    		'), TaxCode="' +AllTrim(m.TaxCode)+'" (rd. '+AllTrim(m.tTva)+' in D300)' +' dar TaxType="' + AllTrim(m.TaxType) +'"',;

	&& ========================================================================&&
	**** T10. GLE:	TaxAmount<>0
	****			And TaxCode ='achizitiilor scutite/neimpozabile declarate in decontul de TVA la randurile : 30, 30.1
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		Testul 10 : In sectiunea General Ledger Entries se verifica daca la nivel de Transaction
		Line (GL.22), in subsectiunea Tax Information (GL.33), structura Tax Information
		Structure, exista Tax Code (S.TI.2) specific achizitiilor scutite/neimpozabile declarate
		in decontul de TVA la randurile : 30, 30.1 pentru care Tax Amount Structure/ Amount
		(S.TI.6/ S.AM.1) <0>
	ENDTEXT
	*
	If DEBUG_SAFT
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat SET TaxCode='348303', TaxAmount=12.12 Where BETWEEN(RECNO(), 50, 53)
	EndIf
	****
	Select;
			g.*;
		,	s.TTva;
	From ( 	Select;
					Type As Tip				;
				,	AccountID				;
				,	StandardAccountID		;	
				,	TransactionDate			;
				,	TransactionDescription	;
				,	Amount					;
				,	Ndp						;
				,	TaxPercentage			;
				,	TaxAmount + Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0) + Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0) As TaxAmount;
				,	TaxType					;
				,	TaxCode					;
				,	Cast('Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As C(20)) As Pozitia;		 						
			From GLE_Ordonat ;
		) g;
	Inner Join ( Select;
						TaxCode;
					,	TTva;
				 From SaftTVA;
				 Where;
				 		AllTrim(Ttva)=='30'	 ;
				 	 Or	AllTrim(Ttva)=='30.1';
			   ) s On g.TaxCode=s.TaxCode;
	Where;
			g.TaxAmount<>0 ;
	Into Cursor dif_GLE_T10
	*
    Scan 
	    Scatter Memv
		*
		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(  lcSerie_Test, 'T10', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '','',;
							    		allt(m.Pozitia)+', '+AllTrim(m.tip) + ', '+DTOC(m.TransactionDate)+ ', cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;
							    		', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+', '+;
							    		+ 'doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+;
							    		', Amount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + '), TaxPercentage ('  +AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.TaxPercentage ,0), 15,2))+;
							    		') TaxAmount <>0 (' + AllTrim(Str(m.TaxAmount,15,2))+ ') dar TaxCode="' +AllTrim(m.TaxCode)+'" (rd. '+AllTrim(m.tTva)+' in D300)',;
							    		0;
									  )		    	   														
	EndScan 
	*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	**** T11. GLE:		TaxAmount<>0
	****				And TaxCode =380001/380002/380003/380004/380005
	****				And not AccountId <> 4426*,4427*,4428*
	&& ========================================================================&&
	Text To lcDescriere_Test NoShow TextMerge
		Testul 11 : In sectiunea General Ledger Entries 
		se verifica daca la nivel de Transaction Line (GL.22), 
		in subsectiunea Tax Information (GL.33), 
		structura Tax Information Structure, 
		pentru notele contabile de autocoloectare exista Tax Code (S.TI.2) 380001/380002/380003/380004/380005 
		pentru care Tax Amount Structure/ Amount (S.TI.6/ S.AM.1) <0> 
		(Se exclud din evaluare liniile de tranzactie pentru care Account_ID
		= 4426*/4427*/4428*, care sunt evaluate la testele 4, 5 si 6)
	ENDTEXT
	*	
	If DEBUG_SAFT		
		UPDATE GLE_Ordonat SET TaxCode='380001', TaxAmount=12.12 Where BETWEEN(RECNO(), 60, 63)
	EndIf	
	****
	Select;
			Type As Tip				;
		,	TransactionDate			;
		,	TransactionDescription	;
		,	StandardAccountID		;
		,	Amount					;
		,	Ndp						;
		,	TaxPercentage			;
		,	TaxAmount + Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0) + Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0) As TaxAmount;
		,	TaxType					;
		,	TaxCode					;
		,	Cast('Pozitia: '+AllTrim(Str(RecNo())) As C(20)) As Pozitia;
	From GLE_Ordonat;
	Where;
			TaxAmount + Nvl(TaxAmount_9,0) + Nvl(TaxAmount_5,0)<>0;
		And		Not Empty(TaxCode);	
		And 	InList(	AllTrim(TaxCode),	'380001','380002','380003','380004','380005');
		And Not	InList(	Left(AccountId, 4),	'4426', '4427', '4428');	
	Into Cursor dif_GLE_T11
	*
    Scan 
	    Scatter  Memv
	   	*
		Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(  lcSerie_Test, 'T11', lcDescriere_Test, lcSection, '','',;
							    		allt(m.Pozitia)+', '+AllTrim(m.tip) + ', '+DTOC(m.TransactionDate)+ ', doc: '+AllTrim(m.ndp)+', '+AllTrim(m.TransactionDescription)+', '+;
							    		'cont: '+ AllTrim(m.StandardAccountID)+;
							    		', Amount (' + AllTrim(Str(m.Amount,15,2)) + '), TaxPercentage ('  +AllTrim(Str(Nvl(m.TaxPercentage ,0), 15,2))+;
							    		') TaxAmount <>0 (' + AllTrim(Str(m.TaxAmount,15,2))+ ') dar  TaxCode="' +AllTrim(m.TaxCode)+'" - autocolectare',;
							    		0;
									  )	
		*
	EndScan
	*
EndProc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
Procedure Insert_Teste_Consistenta_ANAF(tcSerie_Test, tcDenumire_Test, tcDescriere_Test, tcSection, tcSub_Section, tcElement, tcInconsistenta, tnDiferenta)
	Insert Into Teste_Consistenta_ANAF;
		(	Serie_Test,		Denumire_Test,		Descriere_Test,		Sectiune,	Sub_Sectiune,	Element,  	Inconsistenta	, Diferenta );
	Values;
		(	tcSerie_Test,	tcDenumire_Test,	tcDescriere_Test,	tcSection,	tcSub_Section,	tcElement,  tcInconsistenta	,tnDiferenta)
EndProc
* __________________________________________________________________________________________________________
